The results show that (a) soft-prompt tuning saturates format-following within 80 steps (~640 samples) making it highly efficient, (b) soft-prompt tuning significantly outperforms zero- and few-shot prompting, surfacing base model knowledge that standard prompting misses, that (c) even post-trained models can benefit from soft-prompts to maximize format compliance, and that (d) soft-prompted base model performance predicts post-trained model rankings more reliably than zero- and few-shot baselines, offering a low-cost proxy for downstream model quality. Our contributions include (1) metrics which disentangle format-following and knowledge accuracy, (2) a fairer benchmarking protocol of LLM knowledge, and (3) a cost- and memory-effective recipe to identify optimal pre-training strategies early in LLM development.

</details>


### [73] [A Controlled Study of Decoding-Time Truthfulness Methods on Instruction-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12160)
*Ao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CHAIR 是一种通过分析 LLM 内部各层 Token Logit 特征来实时检测幻觉的框架，该方法有效提升了幻觉识别的准确性，并为优化生成式模型的解码策略提供了新的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）常产生幻觉，且现有的检测方法在有效性、鲁棒性和泛化能力上仍面临挑战；作者试图通过挖掘模型内部表征来解决这一问题。

Method: 构建了一个监督学习框架，通过提取 LLM 每层每个 token 的内部 Logit 特征（包括最大值、最小值、均值、标准差和斜率），训练分类器来识别幻觉。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA 和 MMLU 数据集上的实验表明，CHAIR 在零样本（zero-shot）场景下显著提高了幻觉检测的准确性，表现出良好的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: CHAIR 提出了一种利用 LLM 内部层级 Logit 特征检测幻觉的方法，证明了内部表征在提升模型事实准确性和指导自适应解码策略方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CHAIR (Classifier of Hallucination As ImproveR), a supervised framework for detecting hallucinations by analyzing internal logits from each layer of every token. Our method extracts a compact set of features such as maximum, minimum, mean, standard deviation, and slope-from the token logits across all layers, enabling effective hallucination detection without overfitting. Experiments on TruthfulQA and MMLU datasets demonstrate that CHAIR significantly improves detection accuracy, particularly in zero-shot scenarios, showcasing its robustness and generalizability. Beyond hallucination detection, CHAIR highlights the potential of using internal representations for designing advanced decoding strategies. By leveraging patterns in logits, we suggest that more sophisticated models and adaptive decoding methods could further reduce hallucinations and enhance text completion quality. CHAIR not only offers a practical solution for detecting hallucinations but also lays the groundwork for exploring richer representations in LLMs to improve their factuality and coherence.

</details>


### [74] [A Resource for Enthymeme Detection in Controversial Political Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12186)
*Martial Pastor,Nelleke Oostdijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对隐含前提（Enthymemes）标注的主观性难题，提出一套基于Walton论证方案的结构化标注方法，并证明了保留并利用标注分歧数据能显著提升NLP模型处理复杂人类推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐含前提（Enthymemes）标注往往被视为带有主观性而被“去噪”处理或消除，导致忽视了分歧背后的信息及其对模型学习的潜在价值。需要一种更科学的方法来处理这种主观性并提升模型性能。

Method: 基于Walton论证方案（Argumentation Schemes）构建标注指南；收集1482条政治争议推文；进行标注任务复杂度分析，并对比使用“标注分歧”数据与“多数投票硬标签”数据的模型性能。

Result: 实验表明，在包含标注分歧的数据集上训练的模型，优于仅基于多数投票（硬标签）训练的模型；研究识别出了任务中导致高认知负载和不一致标注的关键因素。

Conclusion: 隐含前提（Enthymemes）的标注具有主观性，通过保留并利用标注分歧而非消除分歧，可以提升NLP模型在处理人类推理任务时的表现。结构化的标注指导方针有助于平衡任务的约束性与解释空间。

Abstract: Enthymemes, arguments with unstated premises or conclusions, are pervasive in persuasive discourse, yet their annotation remains notoriously subjective. We present a resource of 1,482 tweets from politically controversial discourse, annotated by five annotators for the presence of enthymemes and their argument structure, designed to study label variation. We first revisit the definition of enthymemes and propose annotation guidelines anchored in Walton's argumentation schemes, offering a structured and constrained approach that nonetheless preserves room for the interpretive nature of the task. This contrasts with past resources, which tend to eliminate disagreement, obscuring its sources and preventing investigation of its potential benefits for model performance. We further propose a complexity analysis of the task, identifying where annotation imposes high cognitive load and may give rise to inconsistent annotation. Our preliminary experiments show that models trained on annotator disagreement outperform models trained on hard majority-vote labels. We close by reflecting on how structural openness in enthymeme definitions and guidelines enables the study of variation in subjective inferential processes for future resources and downstream NLP applications concerned with human inference.

</details>


### [75] [Agentic Environment Engineering for Large Language Models: A Survey of Environment Modeling, Synthesis, Evaluation, and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12191)
*Jiachun Li,Zhuoran Jin,Tianyi Men,Yupu Hao,Kejian Zhu,Lingshuai Wang,Dongqi Huang,Longxiang Wang,Shengjia Hua,Lu Wang,Jinshan Gao,Hongbang Yuan,Ruilin Xu,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对LLM智能体环境进行了系统综述，提出了覆盖建模、合成、评估及应用的生命周期框架，并阐述了智能体-环境的共演化机制，旨在为构建更强健的智能体提供方法论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对大模型（LLM）智能体生存与交互环境的系统性分类与深度分析。随着环境在智能体能力提升中扮演的角色日益重要，亟需建立一套完整的环境工程理论框架以指导未来研究。

Method: 1. 结构化分析：以环境工程生命周期（建模、合成、评估、应用）为逻辑主线；2. 多维分类：基于八大属性与八大领域对现有环境进行调研；3. 范式归纳：引入符号/神经环境合成范式，以及四种智能体演化路径（记忆、编排、轨迹、探索）和三种环境演化范式（神经驱动、难度驱动、规模驱动）。

Result: 提出了完整的环境工程生命周期建模体系；明确了智能体在动态环境中的四条关键演化路径；总结了当前环境合成与评估的技术范式，并提出了“环境即服务”（EaaS）、多智能体环境及神经符号环境作为未来关键突破点。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地梳理了智能体环境的研究现状，确立了环境工程生命周期框架，并定义了智能体-环境共演化的机制。研究指出，环境的构建、评估与演化是提升LLM智能体能力的核心催化剂，并为未来多智能体及神经符号环境的研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Environments serve as interactive systems for large language model (LLM) based agents across diverse scenarios and play a crucial role in driving the continual evolution of model capabilities. Despite this importance, existing work lacks a systematic categorization and deep analysis. This paper systematically studies current researches on agentic environments from the perspective of the environment engineering lifecycle, covering their modeling, synthesis, evaluation and application. Specifically, the paper first introduces representative environments from the perspectives of eight attributes and eight domains, providing detailed analyses of their development paths and highlighting their core capabilities. Second, for automated environment synthesis, two paradigms are introduced, such as symbolic synthesis and neural synthesis. This paper also shows different environment evaluation methods in each paradigm. Thirdly, the corresponding environment applications from the perspective of agent-environment co-evolution are discussed. In specific, the paper characterizes the primary pathways for agent evolution in dynamic environments from four complementary perspectives: memory-centric experience evolution, orchestration-centric workflow evolution, trajectory-centric offline evolution, and exploration-centric online evolution. And three paradigms of environment evolution are identified, namely neural-driven, difficulty-driven, and scaling-driven approaches. At last, several promising future directions are discussed, including Environment-as-a-Service, Multi-agent Environments, and Neural-Symbolic Environments.

</details>


### [76] [Adaptive Multi-Resolution Procedural Knowledge Compression for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12203)
*Changyue Wang,Weihang Su,Qingyao Ai,Yichen Tang,Runzhong Qiao,Xuancheng Li,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SKIM 是一种针对 LLM 过程性技能的自适应多分辨率软标记压缩框架，通过根据任务复杂度动态压缩技能文本，在大幅降低推理开销的同时，有效保留了工具协议和逻辑依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）中复用自然语言技能时，将完整文本放入上下文会导致高额的预填充成本和延迟。现有的文档压缩方法侧重于事实性知识，难以处理过程性知识的逻辑依赖和复杂性，因此需要一种专门针对程序化技能的压缩方法。

Method: 提出 SKIM (SKIll coMpression) 框架，采用自适应多分辨率软标记（soft token）压缩方案。根据技能的复杂程度，动态分配软标记数量，旨在保留过程流中的逻辑依赖和工具协议。该方法支持轻量级离线压缩，适用于频繁更新的场景。

Result: 实验表明，SKIM 可将技能压缩至原 token 长度的 30% 到 60%，在降低推理成本的同时，任务性能表现优于现有的文本压缩方法。

Conclusion: SKIM 是一个针对过程性技能的有效压缩框架，能够在显著减少提示词长度（30%-60%）、降低推理性成本的同时，维持甚至超越现有压缩方法在任务执行中的表现，证明了多分辨率软标记在处理过程性知识时的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used to tackle complex tasks with autonomous workflows. Recently, reusable natural language skills have emerged as a popular paradigm to inject procedural knowledge into LLM applications. Since popular skills are often invoked repeatedly, placing their full text in every context significantly increases prefill cost and latency. While text compression techniques have the potential to solve this problem, most existing methods are designed to compress factual knowledge in documents instead of procedural knowledge, making them insufficient for skill compression. In this paper, we argue that an effective skill compression method should: 1) preserve logical dependencies among workflows and tool protocols, 2) enable lightweight, offline compression for frequently updated community skills, and 3) be adaptable to varying complexities across skills. To address this, we present SKIM (SKIll coMpression), an adaptive multi-resolution soft token compression framework for procedural skills. Depending on the complexity of each skill, SKIM creates different numbers of soft tokens that not only improve the efficiency of LLM inference, but also preserve the effectiveness of skill usage. Experiments indicate that SKIM compresses skills to 30 to 60 percent of their original token length while preserving task performance better than existing compression methods.We have released our code at https://github.com/bebr2/SKIM .

</details>


### [77] [Can News Predict the Market? Limits of Zero-Shot Financial NLP and the Role of Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12210)
*Ali M Karaoglu,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，零样本大语言模型在预测短期股价变动方面效果不佳，但通过引入多层次可解释性框架，可以识别预测的可信度，为金融决策提供辅助支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型技术取得了进展，但“金融新闻能否可靠预测短期股价变动”的问题仍未解决，且现有模型缺乏对高风险金融决策所需的透明度和可解释性。

Method: 提出了一种结构化的处理流程：结合零样本自然语言推理（Zero-shot NLI）与时间聚合模型（考虑新闻的近因效应及事件影响周期）；并构建了一个多层次的可解释性框架（关联Token级、文章级和聚合证据，生成自然语言解释）。

Result: 零样本方法在预测短期股价变动上始终无法超越简单的基准模型，特别是在预测股价下跌（负面运动）时表现较差；但可解释性信号能够有效区分预测结果的可信度，具备实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 零样本学习（Zero-shot）在金融新闻预测短期股价变动方面存在结构性局限，难以通过简单的文本情感映射实现盈利。研究强调，未来的金融NLP应用应从单纯追求预测准确率转向以透明度和不确定性感知为核心的辅助决策系统。

Abstract: Can financial news reliably predict short-term stock movements? Despite advances in large language models, this question remains unresolved. We revisit this problem using a zero-shot natural language processing framework, investigating whether models can extract actionable signals from financial news without domain-specific training. We design a structured pipeline that combines zero-shot natural language inference with temporal aggregation, explicitly modelling recency and event-dependent impact horizons when integrating information across articles. To address the need for transparency in high-stakes settings, we introduce a multi-layered explainability framework that links predictions to token-level, article-level, and aggregate evidence, and produces grounded natural language rationales. Across multiple models and prediction horizons, we find that zero-shot approaches consistently fail to outperform simple baselines, with particularly weak performance on negative movements, suggesting deeper structural limitations in mapping news sentiment to short-term price dynamics. However, explainability signals reliably distinguish between trustworthy and unreliable predictions, offering practical value even when accuracy is limited. These findings highlight the limits of zero-shot financial NLP and motivate a shift toward decision-support systems that prioritise transparency and uncertainty awareness. Code: https://github.com/alimert05/zero-shot-stock-xai

</details>


### [78] [On The Effectiveness-Fluency Trade-Off In LLM Conditioning: A Systematic Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12234)
*Iuri Macocco,Pau Rodríguez,Arno Blaas,Luca Zappella,Marco Baroni,Xavier Suau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大语言模型的控制方法，揭示了控制有效性与模型流畅性及训练范式之间的冲突，并提出低成本的评估指标以取代昂贵的测评方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的可控性对于可靠部署至关重要，但现有研究在控制策略（注入/移除）与生成质量（流畅性）之间的权衡缺乏深入理解。

Method: 对多种控制方法（激活引导、提示工程、监督微调）在概念注入和移除场景下进行系统性评估，并将文本指标与LLM-as-judge评分进行相关性分析。

Result: 高效的引导方法往往会牺牲生成内容的流畅性；激活引导方法在指令微调模型上的有效性远低于基座模型；发现了廉价文本指标与昂贵的LLM-as-judge评分之间存在高度相关性。

Conclusion: 控制大语言模型输出的权衡机制复杂；激活引导（Activation Steering）在指令微调模型上效能显著下降；简单提示词与监督微调适用于概念注入，但在概念移除上表现欠佳；廉价文本指标可有效替代昂贵的LLM-as-judge评估。

Abstract: Controlling the output of Large Language Models (LLMs) is a central challenge for their reliable deployment, yet a clear understanding of the involved trade-offs remains elusive. Current approaches to conditioning are often evaluated with a narrow focus on their effectiveness at injecting or removing a target concept, neglecting generation quality. We systematically investigate a range of conditioning methods in both injection and removal scenarios. We find that efficient steering methods frequently achieve conditioning at a steep cost to fluency. Furthermore, we identify a critical yet previously overlooked interaction with the training paradigm: activation steering methods are far less effective on instruction-tuned models than on their base counterparts. Simple prompting and full-fledged supervised fine-tuning, on the other hand, are viable options for concept injection, but are not as good at concept removal. Finally, cheaply computed textual metrics highly correlate to costly LLM-as-judge scores, and provide insights on the behavior of conditioning methods.

</details>


### [79] [VIA-SD: Verification via Intra-Model Routing for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12243)
*Yuchen Xian,Yang He,Yunqiu Xu,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VIA-SD 提出了一种多层级推测解码框架，通过引入由大模型派生出的“轻量化子模型”作为中间验证层，减少了完整模型验证的负担，显著提升了 LLM 推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码（SD）仅采用二元判定（直接接受或完全重计算），导致大量被草稿模拒绝的 token 在大模型中产生极高的验证开销。作者通过研究发现，许多被拒绝的 token 其实可以通过比完整模型更轻量的验证器来准确处理。

Method: 提出了一种名为 VIA-SD 的多层级推测解码框架。其核心是“模型内路由”（Intra-Model Routing）技术，将草稿验证分为三个层级：1. 高置信度直接接受；2. 中置信度由派生出的“轻量化子模型”（Slim-verifier）重生成；3. 低置信度回退至完整大模型验证。该方法无需修改模型原始训练过程。

Result: 在四项代表性任务及多个模型系列上，VIA-SD 将重拒绝率降低了 0.10-0.22，相比现有顶尖 SD 基线提升了 10%-20% 的速度，对比非草稿推理则实现了 2.5-3 倍的加速。

Conclusion: 多层级推测解码（Multi-tier SD）被证实是一种通用的、可扩展的 LLM 推理加速范式。VIA-SD 通过引入“分层验证”机制，有效降低了传统 SD 中昂贵的大模型验证频率，并提升了整体推理吞吐量。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) addresses the high inference costs of LLMs by having lightweight drafters generate candidates for large verifiers to validate in parallel. Existing draft-verify methods use binary decisions: accept or fully recompute. Yet we find that many rejected tokens can be verified correctly by a slim submodel derived from the full verifier via intra-model routing, instead of the full verifier. This motivates our slim-verifier to handle tokens requiring moderate verification resources, reducing expensive large-model calls. We propose Verification via Intra-Model Routing for Speculative Decoding (VIA-SD), a multi-tier framework using a routed slim-verifier. Draft tokens are processed hierarchically: direct acceptance for high-confidence cases, slim-verifier regeneration for medium-confidence cases, and full-model verification for uncertain cases. Across four representative tasks and multiple model families, VIA-SD reduces rejection rates by 0.10-0.22 and delivers 10-20% speedups over strong SD baselines, while achieving 2.5-3x acceleration over non-drafting decoding. Moreover, VIA-SD is compatible with existing SD frameworks without modifying their training procedures. Our results suggest multi-tier SD as a general paradigm for scalable and efficient LLM inference. Project page: https://zju-xyc.github.io/VIA-SD-Project-Page/

</details>


### [80] [Reassessing High-Performing LLMs on Polish Medical Exams: True Competence or Bias-Driven Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12250)
*Antoni Lasik,Jakub Pokrywka,Łukasz Grzybowski,Jeremi Ignacy Kaczmarek,Gabriela Korzańska,Janusz Świeczkowski-Feiz,Oskar Pastuszek,Paulina Hoffman,Jakub Tomasz Dąbrowski,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究指出当前医学数据集测评LLM存在易猜测的缺陷，通过构建更难的评估基准验证发现，模型在严苛环境下的实际医学表现远低于传统MCQA测试分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM医学评估主要依赖MCQA（多项选择题），存在易被猜测和答案偏见误导的问题，难以真实反映临床能力。因此，需要开发一种更具挑战性、更严谨的基准来准确评估医学大模型的推理水平。

Method: 在该研究中，研究者扩展了基于波兰医学考试的基准，通过增加1.5万道题目、拓展两个新领域，并引入四种结构性修饰以减少MCQA特有的偏见和猜测策略，以此构建了一个更具挑战性的评估框架。研究对21个LLM进行了对比测试。

Result: 在更严格的评估设置下，即使是表现最好的模型（Qwen3.5-122B）在英语和波兰语考试中的得分也分别下降了28.4和31个百分点。实验表明，标准MCQA评分无法可靠地反映真实医学能力，且模型性能受评估设计的强烈影响。

Conclusion: 现有的医学MCQA评估基准可能高估了LLM的临床实际能力。评估设计（如题型结构、难度）对模型表现有显著影响，开发更具挑战性的评估方式对于衡量真实医学推理能力至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) in medicine are mainly evaluated using multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), which can overestimate real clinical ability due to guessing strategies and answer biases. To address these limitations, we introduce an expanded and more challenging benchmark based on Polish medical exams, adding over 15,000 questions, two new domains, and four structural modifications that reduce MCQA-specific artifacts and better test reasoning. We evaluate 21 LLMs and show that evaluation design strongly affects results. Under our harder setup, the best model (Qwen3.5-122B) drops by 28.4 and 31 pp on English and Polish exams, respectively. Despite low evidence of data contamination, standard MCQA scores do not reliably reflect true medical competence. To facilitate further research, we make our benchmark publicly available.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Fully Random Masking: Attention-Guided Denoising and Optimization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12273)
*Jia Deng,Junyi Li,Wayne Xin Zhao,Jinpeng Wang,Hongyu Lu,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AGDO 是一种通过注意力机制引导扩散大模型（dLLM）去噪顺序与优化策略的新兴框架，有效解决了传统随机掩码方法忽略 token 依赖的问题，并在推理与编程任务中取得了 SOTA 效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散大模型（dLLM）训练方法多依赖于随机掩码策略，忽视了 token 之间内在的依赖关系，导致推理能力受限。

Method: 1. 经验分析：分析了注意力机制在 dLLM 中的作用，发现对未掩码上下文有强注意力权重的 token 对生成稳定性和推理至关重要。2. AGDO 框架：基于注意力结构决定去噪顺序；在监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）阶段强化对注意力关键 token 的学习。

Result: 在数学和编程任务基准测试中，AGDO 框架表现优于当前最先进（SOTA）的 dLLM 后训练方法，显著提升了模型的推理性能。

Conclusion: AGDO框架通过利用注意力机制来指导扩散大模型的去噪顺序与训练权重，证明了在扩散模型中整合 token 依赖关系能显著提升模型的推理与代码生成能力，优于现有的随机掩码方法。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer an efficient alternative to autoregressive models through parallel decoding, yet existing post-training methods largely rely on random masking strategies that overlook intrinsic token dependencies. In this work, we present an empirical analysis of attention in dLLMs and show that tokens attending more strongly to unmasked context exhibit greater generation stability and play a critical role in reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we propose AGDO, an attention-guided denoising and optimization framework that aligns both training and optimization with attention-derived dependencies. AGDO determines the denoising order based on attention structure and emphasizes attention-critical tokens during supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical and coding benchmarks demonstrate that AGDO consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming state-of-the-art post-training methods for dLLMs.

</details>


### [82] [Measuring Epistemic Resilience of LLMs Under Misleading Medical Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12291)
*Hongjian Zhou,Xinyu Zou,Jinge Wu,Sean Wu,Junchi Yu,Bradley Max Segal,Tobias Erich Niebuhr,Sara Amro,Michael Petrus,Sheikh Momin,Alexandra M. Cardoso Pinto,Rachel Niesen,Laura Sophie Wegner,Dhruv Darji,Jung Moses Koo,Joshua Fieggen,Kapil Narain,Mingde Zeng,Lei Clifton,Linda Shapiro,Fenglin Liu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现LLM在医学领域缺乏“认识论韧性”，容易被误导性上下文带偏。通过构建MedMisBench，证实模型在干扰下医疗判断准确率大幅下滑，且部分错误决策具有严重的患者医疗风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在医学执照考试中表现优异，但研究者怀疑高分并不等同于安全的医疗判断。LLM在面对误导性上下文时表现出的脆弱性亟待量化评估，以防止其在医疗建议场景中造成危害。

Method: 构建了MedMisBench数据集，包含10,932道医学问题及48,889对误导性上下文与选项组合；通过在问题中注入误导性信息测试11种LLM配置，并邀请14位国际临床专家对模型受误导后的输出进行医疗危害评估。

Result: 平均准确率从原始的71.1%显著下降至受干扰后的38.0%（攻击成功率51.5%）；伪造的“权威框架”和“例外毒化”声明最具误导性，分别达到69.5%和64.1%的攻击成功率；专家评审指出38.2%的错误案例具有严重危害性。

Conclusion: 现有的医学领域LLM基准测试主要评估知识储备，忽略了模型在面对误导性信息时的判断稳定性。该研究引入的MedMisBench揭示了LLM在医学判断上的“认识论韧性（epistemic resilience）”不足，医疗环境中盲目信任LLM存在严重的安全风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now reach expert-level scores on medical licensing exams, encouraging the assumption that high scores imply safe medical judgment while patients increasingly use them for health advice. We show this assumption is fragile: when misleading context is injected into questions that LLMs originally answer correctly, they abandon the correct answer. We call the ability to maintain correct judgment under adversarial context epistemic resilience, and introduce MedMisBench to measure it. MedMisBench contains 10,932 medical question items and 48,889 misleading context-option pairs spanning medical reasoning, agentic capability, and patient-journey evaluation. Across 11 model configurations, mean accuracy falls from 71.1% on original questions to 38.0% under focused misleading context, with 51.5% attack success. The most damaging injections are formal, rule-like fabrications: authority-framed falsehoods reach 69.5% attack success and exception-poisoning claims reach 64.1%. A 14-member clinical panel from 7 countries identified serious potential harm in 38.2% of reviewed cases. MedMisBench exposes a structural blind spot in LLM evaluation in medical settings: existing benchmarks measure what models know, but not whether they preserve correct medical judgment under misleading context.

</details>


### [83] [Measuring Semantic Progress in Multi-turn Dialogue via Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12332)
*Paul He,Shiva Kasiviswanathan,Dominik Janzing*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于信息论的指标，无需依赖大模型推理，仅通过轻量级嵌入模型即可高效且准确地量化多轮对话中的“语义进展”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多轮对话评估方法难以衡量“语义进展”（即对话过程中新信息和非冗余信息的积累），且依赖LLM作为裁判的方法存在计算成本高、不可复现性等问题，因此需要一种高效、可解释且数学上严谨的评估指标。

Method: 研究者将语义进展形式化为“条件于问题的（question-conditioned）不确定性约减”，利用嵌入空间中的高斯建模及对数行列式结构（log-determinant structure）构建了一个无需自回归推理的闭式评估指标，通过计算嵌入表示中的信息增益来量化对话的进展。

Result: 在MT-Bench、Chatbot Arena和UltraFeedback等数据集上的实验表明，该指标与人类判断高度一致，在MT-Bench和UltraFeedback上的表现甚至超过了一些现有的LLM-as-a-judge方法，且在轻量级嵌入模型和CPU环境下依然有效。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于信息论的语义进展度量指标（Semantic Progress）能够以低计算成本高效评估多轮对话质量，其在捕捉信息增量方面的表现优于部分LLM评估器，且具备理论上的严谨性和实验上的稳健性。

Abstract: Evaluating multi-turn dialogue is challenging because quality emerges across turns rather than within individual responses. We focus on a key dimension of information-seeking dialogue: semantic progress, defined as the accumulation of new, question-relevant, and non-redundant information over the course of a conversation. We formalize semantic progress as question-conditioned uncertainty reduction and introduce an information-theoretic metric that approximates it in embedding space. Our main estimator uses a tractable Gaussian formulation with closed-form updates, while a complementary maximum-entropy argument shows why log-determinant structure arises more broadly when only second-order embedding information is retained. This formulation yields desirable theoretical properties, including monotonicity, additive decomposition of total information gain across turns, and diminishing returns for redundant evidence. Unlike LLM-as-a-judge approaches, our metric requires no autoregressive inference at evaluation time and is fully reproducible for a fixed embedding model. Experiments on MT-Bench, Chatbot Arena, and UltraFeedback show that the proposed metric achieves competitive agreement with human judgments despite targeting only semantic progress, with improved alignment on MT-Bench and UltraFeedback compared to several LLM-based judges. Notably, the method remains effective with lightweight embedding models under CPU-only execution, indicating that semantic progress can be captured without reliance on large model capacity.

</details>


### [84] [ALIGNBEAM : Inference-Time Alignment Transfer via Cross-Vocabulary Logit Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12342)
*Chirag Chawla,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对微调导致 LLM 安全性下降的问题，本文提出 ALIGNBEAM 方法，通过跨模型词表映射与 LLM 判断器，在推理阶段实现无需重训练的跨模型安全注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域微调会导致大型语言模型（LLM）的安全性下降，而现有的基于 Logit 混合的防御方法要求模型架构或词表必须一致，限制了其在跨家族模型间的应用。

Method: 提出 ALIGNBEAM 方法：在解码过程的每一步，将锚点模型（Anchor Model）的 Logits 转换为目标模型的词表空间，并利用小型 LLM 判断器（Judge）从 K 个候选词中选择最安全的输出。

Result: 在跨词表和同词表评估场景下，ALIGNBEAM 显著提高了模型对对抗性提示词的拒绝率，同时将任务准确率和推理延迟控制在合理范围内。

Conclusion: 通过 ALIGNBEAM，可以在推理阶段实现跨模型家族的安全对齐，无需修改模型权重，在保持任务性能的同时有效提升了模型的防御能力。

Abstract: Domain fine-tuning degrades the safety of large language models: fine-tuned specialists readily comply with harmful prompts framed in domain language. Existing inference-time defenses that mix logits from a safe anchor model require both models to share a vocabulary, which rules them out for the cross-family specialists where safety is most degraded. We present ALIGNBEAM, a training-free method that lifts this restriction by translating anchor logits into the target model's vocabulary token-by-token at each decoding step; a small LLM judge then selects the safest among K candidate continuations. No weights are changed, and the safety-utility trade-off can be tuned at deployment without retraining. Across both cross-vocabulary and same-vocabulary evaluation pairs, ALIGNBEAM substantially raises refusal on adversarial benchmarks while keeping task accuracy and inference overhead within practical bounds. The results show that safety alignment can be transferred between model families at inference time, without touching either model's weights.

</details>


### [85] [Verifiable Environments Are LEGO Bricks: Recursive Composition for Reasoning Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12373)
*Hao Xiang,Qiaoyu Tang,Le Yu,Yaojie Lu,Xianpei Han,Ben He,Le Sun,Bowen Yu,Peng Wang,Hongyu Lin,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RACES 是一种通过递归组合小规模基础环境来自动化构建大规模、多样化验证环境的框架，解决了大模型强化学习训练中环境扩展困难的问题，显著提升了模型的推理泛化能力与数据使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型通过强化学习（RL）进行推理能力增强时，严重依赖大规模手动构建的验证环境，这种方式存在可扩展性瓶颈，难以实现推理能力的泛化。

Method: 提出了 RACES 递归自动化合成框架，将可验证环境视为可组合模块，定义了 SEQUENTIAL, PARALLEL, SORT, SELECT 四种组合算子，实现了从基础环境规模化生成复杂多样的任务环境。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B 和 Qwen3-14B 模型上实验表明，RACES 可带来平均 3.1 和 2.3 点的性能提升，且在仅使用 1/6 基础环境的情况下，即可达到与全量环境训练相当的效果。

Conclusion: RACES 通过递归组合基础环境，有效缓解了推理环境构建的线性扩张限制，证明了合成环境在提升大模型逻辑推理泛化能力方面的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) with verifiable environments has emerged as a powerful approach for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). While prior research demonstrates that scaling environment quantity improves RL performance, existing manual or individual construction methods suffer from linear scaling limits, thereby hindering scalable reasoning generalization. This paper introduces RACES (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{A}utomated \textbf{C}omposition for \textbf{E}nvironment \textbf{S}caling), a framework that conceptualizes verifiable environments as composable building blocks that can be recursively assembled. The key insight is that when the codomain (output type) of one environment matches the domain (input type) of another, they can be automatically fused into a new verifiable environment, enabling recursive composition. RACES is implemented with 300 individual environments and defines a set of composition operators (\textsc{SEQUENTIAL}, \textsc{PARALLEL}, \textsc{SORT}, and \textsc{SELECT}) that induce diverse reasoning patterns. Extensive experiments show that RL training on these composite environments consistently enhances reasoning generalization. Specifically, RACES improves DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B by an average of 3.1 points (from 48.2 to 51.3) and boosts Qwen3-14B performance from 58.8 to 61.1 on six benchmarks, which are unseen during the construction of training environments. Moreover, RACES achieves performance comparable to training on 300 individual environments using only 50 base environments, demonstrating significant efficiency in environment utilization.

</details>


### [86] [Which Models Are Our Models Built On? Auditing Invisible Dependencies in Modern LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12385)
*Sanjay Adhikesaven,Haoxiang Sun,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大模型训练流水线中难以追踪的递归依赖问题，作者开发了 ModSleuth 代理系统，通过自动化重构依赖图提升了模型开发的可解释性与供应链透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 训练流水线高度依赖其他模型生成和过滤数据，依赖关系呈递归且碎片化分布，已超出人类的可追踪能力，导致供应链透明度严重缺失。

Method: 提出 ModSleuth 代理系统，通过形式化定义依赖类型（直接与间接）、基于操作的关系建模以及跨仓库/版本的人工制品身份解析，递归地从公共资料中重构 LLM 依赖图。

Result: 在四个具有丰富公共资料的 LLM 项目中成功恢复了 1,060 个经源头验证的依赖关系，并构建了大模型开发依赖图，揭示了以往难以发现的多跳许可义务问题及训练-评估过程中的不一致性。

Conclusion: ModSleuth 能够有效应对现代 LLM 开发中日益复杂的递归依赖问题，揭示了包含许可义务、训练与评估耦合以及文档不一致等关键风险，对于提升 AI 供应链的透明度具有重要意义。

Abstract: Modern LLM training pipelines increasingly rely on other models to generate data, filter corpora, judge outputs, and guide development decisions. These dependencies are recursive: a model may depend on an upstream artifact whose own dependencies are documented only in separate releases and artifacts. As a result, the full dependency structure is fragmented across heterogeneous public artifacts, with complexity and recursive depth far outpacing humans' ability to trace. We introduce ModSleuth, an agentic system that recursively reconstructs LLM dependency graphs from public artifacts with source-grounded evidence. We find that the primary challenge is no longer information extraction, but defining what constitutes a dependency and reconciling artifact references across inconsistent documentation. We address these challenges through a formalization that distinguishes direct and indirect dependencies, represents heterogeneous pipeline roles through operation-centered relationships, and resolves artifact identities across names, versions, and repositories. Applying ModSleuth to four public-artifact-rich LLM releases, we recover 1,060 source-verified dependencies and construct large-scale dependency graphs of modern LLM development. These graphs reveal multi-hop license obligations, train-evaluation coupling, discrepancies between released and training-time artifacts, and documentation inconsistencies that would otherwise be difficult to uncover. We release ModSleuth and the resulting dependency graphs to support transparent analysis of the increasingly complex ecosystems underlying modern LLMs.

</details>


### [87] [System Report for CCL25-Eval Task 5: New Dataset and LoRA-Fine-Tuned Qwen2.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12392)
*Haotao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对古典诗歌处理中通用方法局限及数据集匮乏的问题，本文构建了高质量数据集CCPoetry-49K，并通过微调Qwen2.5-14B提出了PoetryQwen模型，显著提升了古典诗歌的翻译与情感理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在古典诗歌处理上多采用通用领域方法，缺乏对诗歌艺术特征的处理，且高质量、领域特定的训练数据集极其匮乏，限制了模型在精准翻译和情感语义理解上的深度。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：清洗并对齐公开数据集，形成包含49,404条指令微调对的CCPoetry-49K数据集。2. 任务解构：将诗歌赏析拆解为词汇解释、语义解析和情感推理三个子任务。3. 模型微调：基于Qwen2.5-14B模型，通过LoRA（低秩自适应）技术进行领域专项微调，得到PoetryQwen模型。

Result: 在CCL25-Eval Task 5基准测试中，PoetryQwen模型得分为0.757，相较于基准模型Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct的0.690，性能提升了9.7%。

Conclusion: 通过构建高质量领域数据集（CCPoetry-49K）并应用针对性的微调策略（LoRA），能够显著提升大规模语言模型在古典诗歌翻译和情感语义解析方面的表现，为特定领域的大模型优化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Recently, large language models (LLMs) have achieved promising progress in the fields of classical Chinese translation and the generation of classical poetry. However, domain-specific research on precise translation and affective-semantic understanding of classical poetry remains limited. The main challenge is that most studies treat the poetic appreciation task as a general-domain problem, neglecting the distinctive features of poetic appreciation, while high-quality and domain-specific datasets are extremely limited. To address this limitation, we decompose the task into three subtasks: term interpretation, semantic interpretation, and emotional inference. Based on multiple open-source datasets, we perform data cleansing and alignment to construct the Classical Chinese Poetry Instruction Pair Dataset (CCPoetry-49K), which comprises 49,404 high-quality instruction-response pairs explicitly optimized for this domain. We then propose a domain-specialized LLM, called PoetryQwen, by applying Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the Qwen2.5-14B model. Experimental results on the CCL25-Eval Task 5 benchmark demonstrate that PoetryQwen achieves a score of 0.757, representing a 9.7% improvement over the Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct baseline (0.690). These findings clearly indicate that PoetryQwen significantly enhances performance in precise translation and emotional understanding of classical poetry. We present new dataset and methodological considerations intended to support the domain-specific optimization of LLMs.

</details>


### [88] [Context-Driven Incremental Compression for Multi-Turn Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12411)
*Yeongseo Jung,Jaehyeok Kim,Eunseo Jung,Jiachuan Wang,Yongqi Zhang,Ka Chun Cheung,Simon See,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 C-DIC 架构，通过引入可修订的对话线程记忆和高效的更新机制，显著提升了现代对话 AI 在超长对话中的效率、上下文保真度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话代理处理长对话时，随着长度增加，注意力机制和编码成本显著提升；直接截断或总结导致保真度下降，而现有上下文压缩方法缺乏跨轮信息共享与修订机制，易导致信息丢失及错误累积。

Method: 提出 Context-Driven Incremental Compression (C-DIC) 框架，将对话处理为交互式上下文线程（interleaved contextual threads）；引入轻量级的“检索-修订-回写”（retrieve, revise, and write-back）循环进行动态内存更新；并在多轮对话中适配了截断反向传播（TBPTT），以学习跨轮依赖而不依赖全历史回传。

Result: 在长对话基准测试中表现出卓越的性能与效率；在数百轮对话中，C-DIC 成功保持了推理延迟的稳定和困惑度的平稳，解决了传统方法在长序列生成中的性能衰减问题。

Conclusion: C-DIC 提出了一种通过对话线程化管理和可修订内存机制实现长对话建模的方法。实验证明该方法在处理长对话时能够保持推理延迟和困惑度（Perplexity）的稳定，性能优于传统压缩方法，为高品质、可扩展的对话建模提供了新路径。

Abstract: Modern conversational agents condition on an ever-growing dialogue history at each turn, incurring redundant attention and encoding costs that grow with conversation length. Naive truncation or summarization degrades fidelity, while existing context compressors lack cross-turn memory sharing or revision, causing information loss and compounding errors in long dialogues. We revisit the context compression under conversational dynamics and empirically present its fragility. To improve both efficiency and robustness, we introduce Context-Driven Incremental Compression (C-DIC), which treats a conversation as interleaved contextual threads and stores revisable per-thread compression states in a single, compact dialogue memory. At each turn, a lightweight retrieve, revise, and write-back loop shares information across turns and updates stale memories, stabilizing long-horizon behavior. In addition, we adapt truncated backpropagation-through-time (TBPTT) to our multi-turn setting, learning cross-turn dependencies without full-history backpropagation. Extensive experiments on long-form dialogue benchmarks demonstrate superior performance and efficiency of C-DIC; notably, C-DIC shows stable inference latency and perplexity over hundreds of dialogue turns, supporting a scalable path to high-quality dialogue modeling.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [MASK: Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling for Risk-Sensitive 6G Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11249)
*Ahmet Gunhan Aydin,Elif Tugce Ceran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对6G机器人协同控制中的频谱受限问题，提出MASK框架，通过语义重要性评估仅调度Top-K智能体，有效解决了通信资源冲突并降低了传输压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G连接机器人需要高性能协同控制，但受到物理无线信道严格的频谱资源限制，无法支持所有智能体同时传输，导致资源分配冲突。

Method: 提出了一种名为“多智能体语义K-调度（MASK）”的控制架构，核心组件是“仲裁辅助语义信息门控（A-SIG）”，基于局部计算的语义重要性评分仅调度Top-K智能体，并通过自监督全局编码器整合为紧凑的潜在状态以辅助决策。

Result: 实验表明，即使在通道访问受限的情况下，MASK的表现仍能媲美无通信限制的基准模型，并在应对数据稀疏性和抗丢包能力方面表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义调度（Semantic Scheduling）是实现资源受限的6G连接机器人系统的关键技术，MASK框架在保持高性能协调的同时克服了物理频谱限制。

Abstract: Realizing the vision of 6G connected robotics requires reconciling high-performance collaborative control with the rigid spectral limitations of physical wireless channels. In realistic collaborative sensing scenarios, spectral resources are quantized into finite physical resource blocks or orthogonal subcarriers, rendering simultaneous transmission by all agents infeasible. To address this, we propose Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling (MASK), a control architecture designed to sustain robust, risk-aware coordination under strict instantaneous bandwidth caps. We introduce Arbiter-Assisted Semantic Information Gating (A-SIG), a lightweight coordination mechanism that enforces hard access constraints by scheduling only the top-K agents based on locally computed semantic importance scores. By aggregating these prioritized observations into a compact latent state, a self-supervised global encoder enables a distributional policy to mitigate tail risks despite data sparsity. We evaluate MASK across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating that it matches the performance of communication-unconstrained baselines even when channel access is restricted to a small fraction of the swarm size. Furthermore, the framework exhibits inherent resilience to packet erasures, validating semantic scheduling as a critical enabler for resource-constrained 6G systems.

</details>


### [90] [Model-based Optimization of Anguilliform Swimming Gaits for Soft Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11278)
*Brian Van Stratum,James Gallentine,Caleb Rucker,Eric Barth,Jonathan E. Clark,Kourosh Shoele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型软体类七鳃鳗机器人SLIDER，通过建立高效的流固耦合模型与遗传算法协同优化，实现了跨水体环境的高效运动控制与设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决软体机器人（如模仿七鳃鳗设计的SLIDER）在复杂多环境（如水下和爬行）中设计与高效运动控制的挑战，通过建立物理耦合模型实现性能的最优化。

Method: 1. 基于Lighthill的大振幅细长体理论描述流体动力学环境；2. 开发并利用二阶Box方法求解流固耦合方程；3. 建立几何与材料非线性模型用于结构优化；4. 采用气动驱动系统在水槽中进行实验验证；5. 利用遗传算法对游泳控制模式与尾鳍设计进行协同优化。

Result: 1. 成功实现了SLIDER机器人，其在受限游泳测试中的速度达到21.7 ± 0.4 cm/s (0.59 Bl/s)；2. 揭示了低频运动主要受阻力环境影响，高频运动主要受惯性流体效应影响的物理本质；3. 验证了计算模型与实验结果的一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过计算模型与遗传算法协同优化，可以为兼具游泳和爬行能力的柔性多模态机器人设计出有效的控制模式和结构参数。研究揭示了流体环境力（惯性力、涡流力及粘性耗散）在不同频率下对柔性体推进的影响机制。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Soft Lamprey-Inspired Dual Environment Robot (SLIDER) and a proper modeling and optimization procedure employed to design the robot. We represent the primary fluid environment actions - inertial effects, vortex forces, and viscous dissipation - using Lighthill's theory for large-amplitude elongated bodies. For structural design parameters such as internal pressure, tail size, and body stiffness, a fast, geometrically and materially nonlinear model is developed and validated. The fluid-structure interaction equations are solved implicitly with an efficient second-order box method. A pneumatic manifold robotic system is employed to actuate SLIDER in a quiescent water tank environment, allowing cross-comparison of computational and experimental results. We find that low-frequency swimming is dominated by resistant environmental forces, whereas higher-frequency swimming is primarily affected by inertial fluid forces. Using our efficient model alongside a genetic algorithm, we co-optimize a swimming control pattern and caudal fin design (subject to SLIDER's climbing morphology) to achieve a tethered swimming speed of 21.7 +/- 0.4 cm/s (0.59 Bl/s). Furthermore, we investigate the optimization procedure for a multimodal robot performing both swimming and climbing tasks.

</details>


### [91] [Embodied-R1.5: Evolving Physical Intelligence via Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11324)
*Yifu Yuan,Yaoting Huang,Xianze Yao,Yutong Li,Shuoheng Zhang,Linqi Han,Pengyi Li,Jiangeng Sun,Wenting Jia,Zhao Zhang,Yuhao Liu,Ruihao Liao,Yucheng Hu,Qiyu Wu,Yuxiao Li,Zibin Dong,Fei Ni,Yan Zheng,Shuyang Gu,Yi Ma,Hongyao Tang,Han Hu,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Embodied-R1.5 是一个统一的具身基础模型，通过大规模数据构造、多任务强化学习及PGC闭环框架，以8B参数实现了SOTA的具身推理与操控能力，并支持高效的VLA下游任务微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有具身模型在统一架构、任务纠错及长程规划能力上的不足，旨在通过构建大规模数据体系和高效训练架构，提升通用物理智能水平。

Method: 1. 构建了超15B token的自动化数据系统；2. 设计多任务平衡强化学习（RL）配方；3. 提出Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC) 闭环框架，支持长程任务自主执行与纠错。

Result: 在8B参数规模下，在16/24项具身VLM基准测试中达到SOTA；在VLA微调后超越π₀.₅，并在真实机器人实验中验证了优异的长程任务与复杂操控能力。

Conclusion: Embodied-R1.5 通过统一架构和闭环控制实现了强大的通用物理智能，证明了小参数规模模型在具备高质量数据和合理训练策略下可超越大模型，并为具身智能研究提供了开源基准和范式。

Abstract: We introduce Embodied-R1.5, a unified Embodied Foundation Model (EFM) that integrates comprehensive embodied reasoning capabilities, spanning embodied cognition, task planning, correction, and pointing, within a single architecture toward general physical intelligence. Leveraging three automated data construction pipelines to significantly expand the data coverage of critical capabilities, we build a large-scale data system of over 15B tokens, and design a multi-task balanced RL recipe to alleviate heterogeneous task conflicts. We further introduce a Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC) closed-loop framework that enables a single model to autonomously execute and self-correct over long-horizon tasks. With only 8B parameters, Embodied-R1.5 achieves SOTA on 16 out of 24 embodied VLM benchmarks, surpassing leading models like Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5 and GPT-5.4. Benefiting from the internalized embodied capabilities, Embodied-R1.5 can be fine-tuned into a VLA with only a small amount of data, outperforming leading VLA models like $π_{0.5}$ across 4 popular manipulation benchmark suites. We further conduct extensive zero-shot real-robot experiments, validating performance in instruction following, affordance grounding, articulated object manipulation, and long-horizon complex tasks, demonstrating strong generalization to the physical world. We open-source model weights, datasets, training code, and EmbodiedEvalKit, an evaluation framework tailored for embodied tasks, to facilitate future research in EFMs.

</details>


### [92] [HiPi: Reproducible High-Fidelity Piezoresistive Sensors for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11372)
*Changyi Lin,Raihan Haque,Hui-Ping Wang,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiPi是一种新型高保真压阻式触觉传感系统，通过标准工业化设计改善了传感器的装配难度、成本与精度，在保持高可重现性的同时显著提升了接触感知的空间分辨率与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉传感器领域存在“可重现性”与“读出高保真度”之间的权衡：现有的简单设计虽易于复制但性能较差，而高性能系统则制作与部署难度巨大。HiPi旨在解决这一矛盾，提供一种既易于制造又具备高精度的触觉传感系统。

Method: 提出了一种全新的压阻式触觉传感系统设计，包括：1. 采用低串扰读出架构；2. 与商用PCB制造兼容的紧凑型读出电路板（无需手动焊接）；3. 基于STM32的低成本微控制器模块；4. 优化的通信链路（双手机器人设置下实现220Hz刷新率）；5. 基于FPCB（柔性电路板）的导电层以简化传感器堆叠。

Result: 在接触几何形态重构测试中，HiPi表现显著优于基准方案：平均交并比（IoU）从0.428提升至0.797，平均Dice系数从0.539提升至0.886。该系统支持大规模多传感器阵列（总计2048个触点），满足 bimanual (双手) 操作的实时性要求。

Conclusion: HiPi成功弥合了“可重现性”与“高保真度”之间的鸿沟，为双手机器人操作和多指传感系统提供了一种高度实用、且易于部署的密集触觉感知解决方案。

Abstract: Piezoresistive tactile sensors are attractive for robotic manipulation because they are thin, lightweight, low-cost, and scalable to dense large-area sensing. However, existing systems still face a practical trade-off: recent reproducible designs emphasize accessibility and ease of reproduction, whereas high-fidelity readout architectures remain more difficult to fabricate, assemble, and deploy. We present HiPi, a reproducible high-fidelity piezoresistive sensing system for robotic manipulation. Building on a low-crosstalk readout principle, HiPi redesigns the complete hardware stack around reproducibility, deployability, and multi-sensor scalability. The system includes a compact readout PCB compatible with commercial PCB fabrication and assembly services, eliminating manual soldering; a smaller and lower-cost STM32-based MCU module; an optimized communication pipeline that achieves 220 Hz readout in a bimanual setup with four dense tactile arrays (2048 taxels in total); and FPCB-based conductive layers that simplify sensor fabrication and stacking. Experiments with structured 3D-printed contact patterns show that HiPi preserves contact geometry substantially better than a reproducible baseline, improving the average IoU from 0.428 to 0.797 and the average Dice score from 0.539 to 0.886. These results suggest that HiPi bridges an important gap between reproducible fabrication and high-fidelity readout, making dense piezoresistive tactile sensing more practical for bimanual manipulation and multi-fingered robotic systems.

</details>


### [93] [PLUME: Probabilistic Latent Unified World Modeling and Parameter Estimation for Multi-Finger Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11396)
*Abhinav Kumar,Soshi Iba,Rana Soltani Zarrin,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对灵巧操作中物理参数不确定性导致的控制失效问题，PLUME 通过联合学习世界模型与在线参数推断，实现了自适应的策略规划，无需重训练即可实现仿真到实体的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的域随机化（domain randomization）策略在处理诸如螺丝刀拧转等高精度灵巧操纵任务时往往不足，因为操纵策略需根据特定的物理参数（形状、姿态、摩擦力等）进行调整，而无法预知这些参数会导致系统动力学的不确定性。

Method: 提出了一种名为“概率潜空间统一世界模型与参数估计”（PLUME）的框架。该方法通过联合学习来动态更新对物理参数的信念（belief），并构建条件化的系统动力学模型，利用在线参数推断（online inference）而非重新训练或微调来对齐世界模型。

Result: 在螺丝刀拧转、阀门旋转、水桶提起和圆盘弹射等仿真任务，以及真实的螺丝刀拧转硬件任务中，PLUME 实现了成功的零样本迁移，性能超过了现有的离线强化学习及增强型行为克隆方法。

Conclusion: PLUME 通过联合学习物理参数估计与依赖于参数的系统动力学，在处理复杂灵巧手操作任务中表现优异，实现了从仿真到真实环境的高效零样本迁移，优于现有的离线强化学习和行为克隆基线。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-finger hands can be sensitive to physical parameters such as object shape, pose, and friction coefficients. While simulation enables large-scale data collection with known parameter values, simulation-trained policies must still handle uncertainty at deployment, where the true parameters and therefore the true dynamics are unknown. Standard domain randomization strategies may be insufficient for precise tasks like screwdriver turning, as manipulation strategies may need to change depending on specific parameter values. To address this, we propose Probabilistic Latent Unified world Modeling and parameter Estimation (PLUME), a world model that jointly learns to evolve a belief over parameter values as well as the system dynamics conditioned on those parameters. We learn a latent space to jointly represent multiple qualitatively different physical parameters along with rewards, themselves functions of partially-observable variables, to inform planning. Our novel learning framework leads to efficient alignment of the world model to true dynamics through online parameter inference as opposed to re-training or fine-tuning. We evaluate our method on simulated screwdriver turning, valve turning, bucket lifting, and disk flicking tasks, as well as a hardware screwdriver turning task, where we achieve successful zero-shot transfer of our simulation-trained policy and outperform state-of-the-art offline reinforcement learning and world-model-augmented behavior cloning baselines. Please see our website at https://plume-world-model.github.io for videos.

</details>


### [94] [Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction for Chunk-based Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11408)
*Yuchi Zhao,Miroslav Bogdanovic,Arjun Sohal,Liyu Tao,Kourosh Darvish,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Florian Shkurti,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人动作分块策略中执行范围固定导致的反应能力不足问题，DEHP 方法通过轻量化动态预测分支实现执行范围的按需分配，在保持原有策略不变的同时显著提升了任务执行效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人策略中广泛使用的“动作分块（Action Chunking）”通常依赖固定的执行范围，这导致在需要频繁重规划的精细操作任务中反应迟钝，且执行范围的选择高度依赖经验调整。

Method: 提出动态执行范围预测（DEHP）方法，通过在线强化学习训练一个轻量化分支来预测执行范围，同时保持预训练的动作分块策略（Black-box policy）不变。

Result: 在多项高精度及长跨度操作任务中，DEHP 显著提升了任务成功率，并能根据任务阶段自动调整：在精细操作阶段预测较短的执行范围以提高反馈频率，在自由空间移动阶段预测较长的执行范围以提高效率。

Conclusion: DEHP 通过动态预测动作执行范围，成功平衡了开环动作分块（Action Chunking）的效率与闭环控制的反应能力，为高精度和长跨度操作任务提供了更鲁棒的控制方案。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a standard design in modern robot policies, from diffusion/flow policies to vision-language-action models, where the policy predicts a sequence of actions and executes a fixed number of them instead of acting one step at a time. However, this paradigm relies on a key assumption: a fixed execution horizon. During chunk execution, the policy operates open-loop, which is particularly problematic for fine-grained manipulation tasks that require frequent replanning. In practice, the execution horizon is typically chosen through empirical tuning and is highly task-dependent. To this end, we propose Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction (DEHP), an effective method that trains a lightweight execution-horizon prediction branch using online reinforcement learning while keeping the pretrained chunk policy completely frozen. This makes the method compatible with black-box chunk policies and isolates the effect of adapting the execution horizon from changes to the underlying action generator. Across our evaluations, DEHP improves the success rate of different high-precision and long-horizon manipulation tasks by a large margin. Our qualitative analysis further shows that DEHP predicts shorter execution horizons during fine-grained stages of the task and longer horizons during free-space motion. In this way, DEHP balances the efficiency of open-loop chunk execution with the reactivity of closed-loop single-step control. Project page: https://dehp-chunking.github.io/

</details>


### [95] [A Modular Dual-Camera Pipeline for Micro-Inspection Using Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11419)
*S. H. Mirtajadini,N. Rublein,R. M. Ramakrishnan,G. ter Maat,M. Aldibaja,A. Y. Mersha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对传统无人机近距离巡检困难及对先验知识依赖高等问题，本文推出了一套基于双相机视觉反馈的通用微观巡检流水线（aerial_micro_inspection），实现了在复杂环境扰动下对非结构化目标的安全、精细化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机巡检系统存在三大瓶颈：(1) 对目标精细捕捉时要求飞行距离过近，存在碰撞风险；(2) 飞行扰动及定位漂移易导致在窄视角下丢失目标；(3) 轨迹规划通常过度依赖目标的先验几何信息，不适用于不规则形状的非结构化目标。

Method: 提出了一种名为 aerial_micro_inspection 的流水线。硬件配置包含一套变焦云台相机（用于精细捕捉）及一套广角立体导航相机（用于环境感知与目标分区）。核心算法引入了视觉反馈闭环机制用于补偿无人机飞行扰动，并采用基于目标分区与视觉引导的机制完成精细化巡检。

Result: 在模拟实验中显著提升了极端扰动下的巡检覆盖鲁棒性；在实际场景（橡树毛虫检测与温室粘虫板检查）中，成功实现了非结构化目标的高细节成像与精准检测。

Conclusion: aerial_micro_inspection 是一套通用、鲁棒的无人机微观检测方案，通过双目视觉配置和主动补偿机制，降低了对飞行控制精度和目标先验知识的依赖，在多场景下验证了其实用性。

Abstract: Most existing drone-based inspection systems require the drone to fly dangerously close to the target or follow complex flight paths to capture small details. In addition, drone flight is affected by disturbances and localization inaccuracies, which can cause the drone to lose sight of its supposed target when it has a narrow view. Furthermore, trajectory planning often requires prior information about the target's geometry, position, and orientation, which is not always available for non-structural targets such as trees, vehicles, or people. To address these challenges, this paper presents aerial_micro_inspection, a generic pipeline for aerial micro-inspection across different use cases. The pipeline assumes a PX4-powered drone equipped with two cameras: (i) a zoomed, gimbal-mounted inspection camera that captures fine details without requiring the drone to fly very close to the target, and (ii) a wide-field-of-view stereo navigation camera that acquires the target surface on site, estimates its range, and partitions it into smaller inspection regions. In addition, a vision-based feedback loop compensates for drone motion while the inspection camera visits small partitions of a larger surface. We evaluate the pipeline in simulation and real-world experiments, mainly in two use-case scenarios: tree inspection for detecting oak processionary caterpillars and their eggs, and greenhouse inspection of sticky traps for detecting whiteflies. The results show improved coverage robustness under drone disturbances in simulation, as well as effective detection of caterpillars and eggs and high-detail imaging of insects in real-world experiments. The pipeline is open-source, developed in ROS 2, and can be adapted to new applications by replacing the surface-segmentation and micro-target detection checkpoints. The code is available at: https://github.com/SaxionMechatronics/aerial_micro_inspection

</details>


### [96] [Bridging the sim2real gap in the table tennis robot with a transformer-based ball states predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11464)
*Yin Bi,Christian Conti,Bilan Yang,Alexander Sigrist,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的乒乓球轨迹预测模型，通过大规模真实数据训练，并结合SPAD（部署时预测器交换）策略，有效解决了机器人乒乓球任务中物理模型依赖性强与sim-to-real迁移难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人乒乓球预测中，基于物理的方法高度依赖模型参数和初始状态，而现有的学习方法难以捕捉长时序依赖且受限于训练数据规模和仿真偏差，导致sim-to-real迁移困难。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于Transformer的架构，直接从历史观测中建模长距离时序相关性，无需显式物理模型； 2. 构建大规模真实世界数据集（涵盖不同技能水平和发球机配置）； 3. 引入SPAD策略：在部署阶段用训练好的Transformer预测器替换仿真器中的物理模型，实现即插即用的sim-to-real转移。

Result: 该方法实现了高精度的长时程球状态预测，并通过SPAD策略在不进行策略重训的前提下，显著提升了机器人在真实环境中的控制效果。

Conclusion: 通过提出的基于Transformer的预测器和SPAD（部署时预测器交换）策略，可以有效缩小仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的鸿沟，在无需重训的情况下提升机器人乒乓球策略的迁移能力。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis is a representative benchmark for high-speed, closed-loop robotic control in dynamic environments, where accurate and fast prediction of ball states is critical for reliable planning and control. Physics-based approaches rely heavily on accurate parameter identification and precise initial state, while learning-based methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies and are typically trained on limited or simulated data. We propose a transformer-based framework for table tennis ball state prediction that leverages attention mechanisms to model long-range temporal correlations directly from historical observations, without relying on explicit flight or bounce models. To support robust learning and generalization, we collected a large-scale real-world dataset from players of varying skill levels and diverse ball cannon configurations. The combination of a high-capacity transformer architecture and extensive real-world data enables accurate long-horizon forecasting. Building on this capability, we introduce a plug-and-play sim-to-real transfer strategy, Swap Predictor at Deployment (SPAD), which replaces the physics-based simulator used during training with the proposed real-world-trained predictor at deployment, improving the sim-to-real transferability of the policy without requiring retraining. We demonstrate that this simple substitution effectively narrows the sim-to-real gap while preserving the efficiency and scalability of simulation-based training.

</details>


### [97] [Steering Multirobot Behavior via Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11489)
*Satyajeet Das,Darren Chiu,Shashank Hegde,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAE 是一种通过在推理阶段实时修改预训练策略中间激活值来调整机器人行为的框架，无需修改原模型权重，从而规避了灾难性遗忘并实现了多场景下的行为自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的机器人需要超出预训练策略范围的行为适配能力。传统的微调或重训练方法会导致灾难性遗忘，降低策略的原始基本性能。

Method: 提出一种名为 CLAE（闭环仿射激活编辑）的推理时框架。该方法通过在冻结的策略中训练稀疏自动编码器提取行为相关的潜在特征，并学习一个轻量级的基于强化学习（RL）的转向策略，在推理时对选定的激活值进行状态依赖的仿射编辑。

Result: 在多四旋翼导航任务中，CLAE 成功实现了：1. 对单个机器人速度配置文件的精确控制；2. 维持多智能体编队协同；3. 实现全新的避障或隐蔽行为（如躲避监控）。这些成果均在仿真和物理实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: CLAE 提供了一种在不修改预训练策略权重的情况下，实现机器人行为在线自适应调整的有效方案，成功解决了传统微调策略带来的灾难性遗忘问题，并展现了在复杂多智能体协同任务中的灵活性。

Abstract: Real-world robots need to adapt their behavior beyond the envelope of their pre-trained policy. Policy finetuning or retraining are options, but they risk catastrophic forgetting, degrading the pretrained policy's base performance. To combat this, we introduce CLAE: Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing, an inference-time framework for steering the behavior of a frozen policy by editing intermediate activations while keeping the base policy weights and downstream action head untouched. CLAE approaches behavior steering as a closed-loop problem whose outputs edit policy activations that adapt online to the robot state, environment, target behavior, and multi-robot context. It trains a sparse autoencoder over frozen-policy activations, selects behavior-relevant latent features via post-hoc probing, and learns a lightweight RL-based steering policy that applies state-dependent affine edits to selected latents during inference. We validate CLAE on a frozen multi-quadrotor navigation policy trained to perform a single task: navigating robots to a set of goal locations while avoiding obstacles. Through extensive simulations and physical tests, we show that while navigating to their goal positions, CLAE can 1. steer individual robot behavior by controlling each robot's velocity profile; 2. coordinate multirobot behavior by preserving a desired formation; and 3. produce entirely new behavior wherein robots are required to reduce their exposure to surveillance cameras in the environment.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Object Manipulation from Scratch via Contrastive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11525)
*Tongle Shen,Caleb Chuck,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对对比强化学习在操作任务中处理交互困难的问题，本文提出交互加权重采样（IWR）方法，通过聚焦交互过程有效捕捉动力学突变，显著提升了机器人在复杂操控任务下的样本效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对比强化学习（CRL）在处理机器人操纵（需要物体交互，如抓取、接触）的任务时，难以处理交互诱发的动力学突变，导致标准能量函数无法有效进行表示和规划。

Method: 将操作动力学建模为分段平滑马尔可夫过程，并引入“交互加权重采样”（Interaction-weighted Resampling, IWR）机制，在交互发生的前中后阶段进行重采样，以捕捉多模态和分段非线性的可达性结构。

Result: IWR 在多项交互密集型任务中表现优异，仿真环境下平均性能提升 19.8%；在真实世界空气曲棍球任务中，成功率从 25% 提高至 60%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 IWR 方法通过在交互关键时刻进行重采样，有效解决了对比强化学习（CRL）在处理物体交互时表现不佳的问题，在仿真及真实机器人任务中均取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has seen recent success in a wide variety of goal-conditioned robotics tasks by learning structured representations of the dynamics. However, despite its success in locomotion and simpler control domains, CRL often struggles in interaction-rich manipulation. We argue that a key source of this difficulty is object-centric interaction, such as contact or grasping, that induces distinct changes in the underlying dynamic modes. In this work, we formulate manipulation dynamics as a piecewise-smooth Markov process and show that interaction-induced mode changes create piecewise nonlinear reachability structures that are difficult for standard CRL energy functions to represent and plan over. Based on this analysis, we introduce Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR). IWR performs interaction-aware resampling around phases before, during, and after interactions, encouraging the learned representation to preserve the mode boundaries that determine future reachability to capture multi-modal and piecewise nonlinear reachability. Across interaction-centric environments, including 2D dynamic control, robotic manipulation, and robot air hockey, IWR improves both sample efficiency and overall performance over prior CRL methods, with 19.8% average improvement in simulation. Finally, using a sim-to-real pipeline with policies trained by IWR, we demonstrate the first real-world goal-conditioned robot air hockey agent capable of hitting goals, improving success from 25% to 60%. Project Page: IWR-arxiv.github.io.

</details>


### [99] [Adversarial Attacks on Learned Policies for Surgical Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11535)
*Shutong Jin,Ziyang Chen,Preethi Satish,Paavan Gupta,Florian T. Pokorny,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对机器人手术中基于学习的策略进行了对抗性攻击研究，通过 photometric 对抗扰动实验发现，现有主流手术机器人策略极易受攻击，导致手术成功率大幅下降，对未来手术机器人系统的安全性提出了挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨端到端视觉驱动的机器人手术策略是否容易受到对抗性攻击，以及这些攻击对患者安全的潜在威胁。

Method: 1. 提出两种威胁模式：干扰攻击（破坏策略执行）和转向攻击（将动作引向目标方向）。2. 设计了三种不同信息获取程度的对抗攻击算法。3. 引入了模拟自然光照变化的 photometric 对抗攻击。4. 在去腐和缝合任务中，针对 ACT、Diffusion Policy 和 Pi0 三种主流架构进行了560次物理实验。

Result: 实验结果显示，通过对抗性视觉扰动，手术子任务的成功率平均降低了61%，证明了现有先进策略在复杂手术场景下缺乏鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于学习的机器人手术策略在面对对抗性攻击时非常脆弱，即使是微小的视觉扰动也能显著降低手术成功率（平均下降61%），这凸显了在部署此类系统时安全性增强的紧迫性。

Abstract: Learning-based policies are being considered to augment the dexterity of human surgeons in robot-assisted surgery. Can the end-to-end mapping from visual observations to robot actions be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, potentially leading to patient injury? In this paper, we present the first study of adversarial threats to learning-based policies in surgical robotics. We investigate two threat modes: (a) disruptive attacks, where imperceptible visual perturbations interrupt policy execution, and (b) steering attacks, where such perturbations steer policy actions toward attacker-specified directions. We formulate three adversarial attack methods, each with increasing access to policy information, and evaluate their impact on two surgical subtasks: debridement and suturing. Our evaluation covers three end-to-end policy architectures: ACT, Diffusion Policy, and Pi0. In addition, we introduce a new class of photometric adversarial attacks that mimic natural visual changes, such as lighting variations, to generate effective yet visually plausible perturbations. Results from 560 physical experiments using phantoms for debridement and suturing suggest that state-of-the-art policies can be significantly disrupted, resulting in an average 61% reduction in surgical subtask success rates. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/adversary-surgery

</details>


### [100] [ConsistencyPlanner: Real-time Planning with Fast-Sampling Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11569)
*Qichao Zhang,Xing Fang,Jiaqi Fang,Zhenwen Cai,Jie Ling,Qiankun Yu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Consistency Planner，通过快采样一致性模型和异构特征融合技术，实现了自动驾驶在复杂场景下的实时、安全、多模态轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则方法缺乏灵活性，而现有的学习路径规划方法难以在处理动态多模态行为建模与实时性要求之间取得平衡，常导致驾驶决策迟疑或不安全。

Method: 提出 Consistency Planner 框架，包含两个核心技术：1) 利用快采样一致性模型（Consistency Models）进行高效多模态轨迹采样；2) 引入注意力增强解码器实现异构特征（场景与动作 Token）的动态融合。

Result: 在 Waymax 仿真器中表现优异，尤其是在复杂动态驾驶场景下，能够生成多样化的可行轨迹，安全性指标显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Consistency Planner 在保持实时性的同时，有效解决了复杂动态驾驶场景中多模态轨迹规划的挑战，显著提升了自动驾驶的安全性。

Abstract: Closed-loop planning in complex, real-world driving scenarios presents a critical challenge for autonomous driving systems. While traditional rule-based methods are interpretable, their predefined heuristics lack the adaptability for dynamic traffic environments. Learning-based approaches have shown considerable promise. Conversely, learning-based approaches, despite their promise, struggle to balance the modeling diverse and multimodal driving behaviors and real-time planning, often leading to indecisive or unsafe actions. To address this limitation, we propose Consistency Planner, a real-time planning framework with fast-sampling consistency models. Our approach is built upon two key technical contributions. Efficient Multimodal Sampling: We employ fast-sampling consistency models to generate a diverse set of plausible future trajectories. This enables efficient, real-time exploration of multimodal actions, overcoming the computational bottlenecks of previous iterative generative methods. Heterogeneous Feature Fusion: We introduce an attention-enhanced decoder that dynamically integrates heterogeneous input features (including scene feature and action token) into a cohesive representation for robust planning. Extensive evaluation in the Waymax simulator demonstrates superior performance in safety metrics compared to existing methods, with particularly strong results in challenging dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [101] [Distortion-Resilient Robotic Imitation Learning for Autonomous Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11577)
*Hao Wang,Fu-Zhao Ou,Shiqi Wang,Zhaolin Wan,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人线缆布线任务中因图像失真导致控制性能下降的问题，本文提出一种集成图像质量评估与置信度学习的模仿学习框架，显著提升了模型在劣质图像条件下的鲁棒性和控制决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中常见的图像信号失真极大制约了机器人线缆布线任务的精确度和可靠性，且现有的智能控制方法缺乏针对受损图像的专项优化及对图像质量信息的有效利用。

Method: 提出了一种包含三模块的机器人模仿学习框架：1.图像质量评估模块（提取质量特征）；2.基于置信度的学习机制（自适应优先处理高难度样本）；3.决策模块（输出离散技能或连续动作）。

Result: 实验证明，该框架能有效应对观测图像失真带来的影响，显著提升了机器人复杂控制任务中的学习效率与决策质量。

Conclusion: 通过引入图像质量评估和置信度学习机制，该框架能有效提升机器人系统在图像质量受损环境下的鲁棒性、决策可靠性及整体控制性能。

Abstract: The rapid development of intelligent control methodologies has endowed robots with powerful autonomous intelligence. Cable routing, a ubiquitous foundational task in industry, provides a rigorous benchmark for robotic dexterity and sequential decision-making. In these practical scenarios, image observation distortion frequently occurs. Samples characterized by low-quality image observations often hinder accurate model training, posing challenges to the reliability and accuracy of intelligent control systems. Nevertheless, no dedicated intelligent control solution has been proposed for scenarios of image signal distortion. Meanwhile, image quality information has not been sufficiently exploited to further enhance the performance of intelligent control methodologies. To this end, we propose a novel robotic imitation learning framework that comprises an image quality assessment module, a confidence-based learning mechanism, and a decision-making module, which is designed to maintain high performance even under distorted image observations. In the proposed framework, the image quality assessment module synergizes with the confidence-based learning mechanism to enhance the efficacy of the decision-making module. Specifically, the image quality assessment module is incorporated to extract image quality information from image observations, while the confidence-based learning mechanism adaptively prioritizes challenging samples to improve learning effectiveness. The decision-making module determines appropriate discrete skills or continuous actions. Experimental results demonstrate that our formulated framework enhances the overall performance of the decision-making module.

</details>


### [102] [LUCID: Learning Embodiment-Agnostic Intent Models from Unstructured Human Videos for Scalable Dexterous Robot Skill Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11628)
*Harsh Gupta,Guanya Shi,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LUCID通过解耦任务意图和具身控制，成功利用海量无结构视频赋予机器人泛化操作能力，摆脱了对昂贵结构化演示数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人学习高度依赖昂贵且特定于特定形态（embodiment）的结构化人工演示数据，无法在大规模数据上扩展。无结构互联网视频虽海量，却缺乏机器人动作标注，存在“视频-动作”缺失鸿沟。

Method: 提出了LUCID两阶段学习框架：1. 意图模型：利用互联网大规模视频学习短时程任务意图；2. 具身控制：将意图映射为具体机器人动作。此外，采用意图接口实现意图模型与具体控制器的解耦，支持跨形态机器人应用。

Result: 在5项现实世界操作任务（搅拌、擦拭、装箱、推T型物体、线缆布线）上均有出色表现，实现了零样本（zero-shot）迁移至新场景和新物体，且部分任务仅需少量自采集视频即可完成。

Conclusion: 通过将任务意图（intent）与具身控制（sensorimotor policy）解耦，LUCID证明了利用大规模无结构互联网视频进行机器人学习的高效性与泛化能力，为摆脱昂贵的人工标注演示数据提供了新路径。

Abstract: The most widely-adopted robot learning pipelines today learn skills from robot demonstrations or structured human data, which are expensive to collect and tied to specific embodiments. In contrast, unstructured human videos provide a scalable alternative. They contain diverse manipulation demonstrations across objects, scenes, and strategies, but are not directly connected to robot action. We propose LUCID, a two-stage framework that learns task intent from unstructured human videos drawn from internet-scale datasets and learns robot control in massively-parallel simulation. The intent model predicts short-horizon intent (what should happen next in the scene) from the current observation in closed loop. An embodiment-specific sensorimotor policy converts this intent into robot actions. The intent interface is shared across controllers, so the same intent model can be applied to different embodiments, from our primary dexterous hand to a parallel-jaw gripper. We evaluate LUCID on five real-world manipulation tasks: stirring, wiping, and binning supervised by only internet video, with zero-shot transfer to novel scenes and object instances; and push-T and cable routing supervised by 1 hr each of self-collected smartphone video. Project page: https://lucid-robot.github.io/.

</details>


### [103] [SAFER-Nav: Enhancing Safety for Visual Robot Navigation via Segmentation-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11636)
*Geonyeong Ko,Giung Lee,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对视觉导航模型在未知环境中易发生碰撞的问题，本文提出一种将可通行空间与障碍物几何结构显式建模并集成到策略中的新方法，相比现有基准模型提升了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉基础模型在未见过的环境或分布偏移条件下，虽然能保持目标导向性，但常因缺乏对环境几何结构的显式认知而导致安全性下降（碰撞）。

Method: 提出一种能够与多种RGB主干网络兼容的导航策略微调方法，通过显式建模障碍物边界及可通行自由空间结构来约束导航模型。

Result: 在多种机器人平台、室内场景及动态/静态障碍物测试中，该方法在保持目标到达成功率的同时，显著降低了碰撞率。

Conclusion: 通过直接将可通行空间与障碍物边界的显式几何先验引入导航策略，该方法显著提升了视觉导航在未知环境中的安全性，优于当前主流的ViNT和NoMaD及其变体。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation models, particularly foundation models, generate viable trajectories from RGB observations alone. However, even state-of-the-art transformer- and diffusion-based policies struggle to generalize in unfamiliar deployment environments containing unseen obstacles or shifted conditions. The resulting trajectories often remain goal-directed but unsafe. Existing efforts improve safety through external trajectory correction or internal geometric priors, yet the resulting policies are not trained to explicitly represent obstacle boundaries or traversable free-space structure. To address this, we propose a navigation model that incorporates these structures directly into the policy via fine-tuning and is designed to be compatible with diverse RGB-based backbones. Across multiple robot platforms, indoor environments, and static and dynamic obstacle scenarios, our method reduces collision frequency relative to ViNT, NoMaD, and their CARE-augmented variants while maintaining goal-reaching performance.

</details>


### [104] [Improving Human Diving Endurance with a Field-Deployable, Untethered Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11704)
*Zhihao Zhou,Zhenmeng Ju,Rui Yang,Chenxi Zhang,Zhihao Zhou,Ming Xu,Enhao Zheng,Dongjie Jiang,Lecheng Ruan,Jingeng Mai,Qining Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究开发了DiveMate水下外骨骼，通过自适应踢水辅助，在实地测试中成功将潜水续航时间提升近55%，并大幅降低了能耗与生理疲劳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体水下运动受高阻力及呼吸气体有限的限制，目前水下领域在外骨骼辅助方面尚属空白，缺乏提升水下续航能力的技术手段。

Method: 开发了一款名为“DiveMate”的野外作业型、非系留式水下外骨骼。该设备通过实地潜水测试，利用自适应踢水辅助功能评估其对人体生理能耗、呼吸气体消耗率以及运动规律的影响。

Result: 在自然潜水条件下，DiveMate使单位能量（呼吸气体）下的行驶距离增加了42.9%，潜水时长提升了54.9%，净气体消耗率降低了47.0%，同时显著降低了肌肉活动强度并改善了运动学的能量经济性。

Conclusion: 外骨骼辅助技术能显著提高人体潜水续航能力，不仅减少了生理消耗，还延长了潜水时间和距离，为未来水下辅助设备的设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Human endurance in underwater locomotion is fundamentally restricted by high energetic demands to overcome drag and the finite supply of self-contained breathing gas. While exoskeleton technology can reduce the metabolic cost of humans in terrestrial locomotion, its potential to enhance human endurance during underwater diving remains entirely unexplored. Here, we present DiveMate, a field-deployable, untethered exoskeleton designed to improve human diving endurance via adaptive kick assistance in real-world underwater environments. During naturalistic diving, DiveMate increases the travel distance using a given energy (breathing gas) by 42.9% and extends dive duration by 54.9% through reducing gas consumption rate. Marked reductions in muscle activation indicate a decrease in physiological exertion, with the net gas consumption rate decreasing by 47.0%. Kinematic characteristics and regularity improvements further underpin efficient energy economy. These results suggest that applying exoskeleton assistance is beneficial for improving human diving endurance and augmenting their ability to explore the aquatic world. This study extends the application frontier of exoskeletons and provides a potential reference for the design and assessment of future underwater assistive devices.

</details>


### [105] [Explore From Sketch: Accelerating UAV Exploration in Large-scale Environments with Prior Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11708)
*Tiancheng Lai,Yuman Gao,Xiangyu Li,Ruitian Pang,Xingpeng Wang,Siqi Shen,Mengke Zhang,Yin He,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准和分层视点规划框架，能有效利用不精确的先验地图优化无人机探索效率，显著降低了飞行距离并提升了复杂环境下的探索效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、复杂拓扑环境下的无人机自主探索效率较低，且现有方法难以有效利用通常存在误差或缺失的先验地图（如建筑图纸）进行全局导航引导。

Method: 1.2D-3D点云配准：提出利用GeoContext描述符进行候选检索、多帧验证机制剔除外点，并采用Scale-ICP进行精细配准，支持多假设输出以应对歧义；2.探索规划：采用分层视点规划策略，将局部视点附加到先验引导点，并通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）确定遍历顺序；3.不确定性处理：引入风险感知选择器评估序列风险，并结合带固定端点的旅行商问题（TSP）生成局部覆盖路径。

Result: 在基准测试中，相比当前主流方法，探索效率提升达34.2%，飞行距离减少37.9%；仿真与实地实验验证了其对先验地图不完整和形变的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过利用不精确的先验地图实现了鲁棒且高效的无人机自主探索，证明了即使在先验数据存在缺失和偏差的情况下，结合鲁棒的配准与分层规划策略也能显著提升探索性能。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration with UAVs in large-scale, topologically complex environments often suffers from low efficiency due to suboptimal scheduling and detours. Prior maps (e.g., construction drawings), although usually imprecise and flawed, are readily available in many scenarios and have the potential to provide global structural guidance. This paper presents a novel exploration framework that leverages sparse, unaligned, and even discrepant 2D prior maps for LiDAR-based UAV exploration. First, a robust 2D-3D point cloud registration pipeline is proposed to align LiDAR observations with prior maps. The registration pipeline combines a GeoContext descriptor for single-frame candidate retrieval, a multi-frame verification mechanism for coarse transformation estimation with outlier rejection, and a Scale-ICP algorithm for refinement. The registration module can handle map discrepancies and provide multiple hypotheses when geometric ambiguities arise. To effectively utilize the registration results for exploration planning, we further develop a hierarchical viewpoint planning strategy under localization uncertainties. The hierarchical strategy first spatially attaches local viewpoints to prior guidepoints and adopts a Monte Carlo Tree Search solver to determine their traversal sequence under each registration hypothesis. To mitigate registration uncertainty, a risk-aware selector evaluates prior sequences using confidence-weighted travel risk, and a fixed-endpoint traveling salesman problem is formulated to generate an efficient local coverage path under the selected prior guidance. Benchmark evaluations reveal up to 34.2% improvement in exploration efficiency and 37.9% reduction in flight distance compared to state-of-the-art methods, while extensive simulations and field experiments further demonstrate robustness to prior map incompleteness and deformations.

</details>


### [106] [TacCoRL: Integrating Tactile Feedback into VLA via Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11743)
*Siyu Ma,Yuqi Liang,Chang Yu,Yunuo Chen,Hao Su,Yixin Zhu,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacCoRL 是一种将触觉反馈注入 VLA 模型的框架，通过仿真与真实环境协同训练及基于仿真的 RL 优化，极大提升了机器人在接触密集任务中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言动作（VLA）模型在处理高接触复杂任务时，仅依靠视觉信息无法捕捉关键的局部接触状态，导致在接触瞬间容易失败。

Method: 1. 触觉融合：在 VLA 策略中引入触觉输入；2. 协同训练：结合仿真（用于RL优化）与真实数据（用于监督学习锚定）；3. 策略优化：利用仿真中基于任务奖励的强化学习（RL）来训练对接触状态敏感的动作响应；4. 闭环训练：无需大规模离线触觉预训练或高风险的真实世界 RL。

Result: 在四个双臂接触密集型任务中，TacCoRL 实现了 72.5% 的平均成功率，显著高于基线（50.0%），且实现了零在线真实环境 RL 下的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: TacCoRL 通过将触觉反馈集成到 VLA 模型中，并通过仿真强化学习对接触敏感行为进行优化，有效解决了机器人复杂接触任务中的动作失准问题，显著提升了成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide strong visual, language, and action priors for robot manipulation, but visual observations alone often miss the local contact state required for contact-rich tasks. We present TacCoRL, a scalable framework that injects Tactile feedback into VLA policies and improves them through sim-real Co-training and simulation-based reinforcement learning (RL), without requiring large-scale tactile pretraining or extensive real-world contact exploration. The key idea is not only adding touch as an input, but learning how contact readings should modulate action responses in near-failure states that are rare in demonstrations and risky to collect on hardware. We use a real-aligned simulator as a closed-loop training environment for contact interaction. Mixed simulated and real trajectories first warm-start tactile-conditioned actions in the pretrained policy. Reinforcement learning with verifiable task rewards then optimizes the policy using simulated contact rollouts. It reinforces tactile-conditioned actions that lead to task completion, while a supervised objective on real trajectories keeps the refined policy anchored to deployment visual, tactile, and action distributions. The resulting policy transfers directly to the real robot without privileged simulation state or online real-world RL. Across four bimanual contact-rich tasks, the final visuo-tactile policy achieves an average success rate of 72.5%, compared to baseline of 50.0%. Result videos and more details are available at https://tac-corl.github.io/

</details>


### [107] [Blind Dexterous Grasping via Real2Sim2Real Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11767)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiyan Huang,Zhe Xu,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需视觉、仅依赖触觉的灵巧手盲抓取框架，通过触觉传感器校准、预训练特征提取器和扩散策略蒸馏，有效缩小了从模拟环境到真实环境的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决灵巧手触觉盲抓取中存在的Sim-to-Real鸿沟问题，以及现有稀疏触觉传感器信号表达力不足的挑战。

Method: 1. Real2Sim触觉校准流水线：建立数字孪生模拟器以生成逼真的触觉信号；2. 布局感知触觉编码器：利用传感器几何先验进行自监督预训练，增强稀疏触觉信号的表达能力；3. 触觉条件下的扩散策略（Diffusion Policy）：通过在大规模模拟数据训练的对象特定RL专家进行蒸馏与聚合，提升泛化能力。

Result: 在LEAP Hand上通过20个物体（10个已知，10个未知）的测试，实现了27%的物理世界抓取成功率，且无需任何真实世界演示或视觉输入。消融实验证明了所提校准与编码器设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过接触点校准、几何感知表征学习以及基于扩散模型的策略聚合，该研究成功实现了仅依赖触觉的灵巧手盲抓取，为解决触觉域差异（Sim-to-Real）问题提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Blind grasping with a dexterous hand is a crucial manipulation capability. Nevertheless, learning such tactile-only policies for real robots remains challenging due to the tactile sim-to-real gap and the limited expressiveness of sparse tactile signals. To bridge this gap, we propose a framework for tactile-only blind grasping that is deployable on a physical multi-fingered robotic hand. Our approach combines three key components. First, we introduce a Real2Sim tactile calibration pipeline that constructs a contact-calibrated digital-twin simulator capable of reproducing real tactile signals. Second, we improve the expressiveness of sparse tactile observations using a layout-aware tactile encoder, which incorporates sensor-geometry priors through self-supervised pretraining. Third, to improve generalization to unseen objects, we train object-specific reinforcement-learning experts in the calibrated simulator and aggregate their successful grasp trajectories into a tactile-conditioned Diffusion Policy. We evaluate our method on a physical LEAP Hand equipped with distributed tactile sensing across 10 seen and 10 unseen objects. The deployed policy achieves a 27\% real-world grasp success rate across all 20 objects, without real-world grasping demonstrations or visual input. Simulation ablations show that layout-aware tactile pretraining improves grasping performance, while sensing-level evaluations confirm that Real2Sim calibration increases the consistency of tactile contact events between simulation and hardware. Together, these results suggest that contact-event calibration, geometry-aware tactile representation learning, and diffusion-based policy aggregation provide an effective path toward tactile-only blind grasping on real dexterous robotic hands. Project page:Dex-Blind-Grasp.github.io.

</details>


### [108] [Human-Guided Co-Manipulation of Carbon Fiber Plies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11818)
*Rami Ojanen,James Fant-Male,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了碳纤维铺层的人机协作操作，提出了一种结合语音、视觉追踪和柔顺力控的多模态控制方法，以解决柔性材料处理中的操控难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性材料（如碳纤维铺层）的自动化处理难度大，而全人工处理效率低且存在人体工学风险。人机协作（HRC）与协同操作（co-manipulation）是提升效率的有效途径，亟需探索高效的交互与控制手段。

Method: 在受控环境下对多种碳纤维铺层协作操作的控制方法进行了对比验证与评估，并提出了一种融合语音命令、基于视觉的手腕追踪以及具有柔顺控制的力控技术的组合方案。

Result: 研究表明，单一控制手段存在局限性，而所提出的多模态融合控制方案能够提供最优的操作体验与控制灵活性。

Conclusion: 通过多模态交互（语音、视觉手腕追踪、力控柔顺控制）组合，实现了对碳纤维铺层协作操作的高效、直观控制。

Abstract: The handling of flexible materials is a difficult task to fully automate due to the challenges caused by the deformability of these types of objects.
