To close these gaps, we present an open source benchmark platform that features (i) a unified controller interface, (ii) a closed-loop workload driver covering multiple workload patterns, and (iii) dual-metric SLO scoring that reports raw SLO (overall compliance) and steady-state SLO (compliance during stable operation) separately. Running six controllers across five cluster configurations and two load regimes (424 episodes), we find that controller rankings are strongly configuration-dependent: a deep reinforcement-learning winner under light workloads loses to a rule-based heuristic by nearly 29 percentage points once load intensifies, at roughly 500$\times$ the per-decision operational overhead. We further show that separating raw from steady-state SLOs exposes switching costs that a single aggregate score would otherwise conflate.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [SPEAR: A System for Post-Quantization Error-Adaptive Recovery Enabling Efficient Low-Bit LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11244)
*Hongyuan Liu,Yawei Li,Zhiqiang Que,Qinli Yang,Junming Shao,Guosheng Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SPEAR是一种针对LLM推理的误差自适应恢复系统，通过按Token动态补偿量化误差，成功以极小的额外代价解决了小模型4-bit量化导致的性能损失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态量化补偿方法无法处理输入依赖的量化误差，导致简单Token被过度修正而复杂Token修正不足，使得小参数量模型在4-bit量化下表现出与FP16明显的质量差距，限制了模型部署的经济性。

Method: 1. 使用基于CKA（中心核对齐）的熵感知诊断方法定位误差敏感层；
2. 引入轻量级误差补偿器（ECs），并通过每Token门控（Per-token gating）实现按需补偿；
3. 设计自适应算子融合调度（Adaptive kernel-fusion dispatch），利用对等规约内核及P2P双写技术优化计算与同步，并采用基于SLO（服务等级目标）的调度器确保延迟稳定性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的每通道（per-channel）量化设置下，SPEAR能够恢复W4与FP16之间56%-75%的困惑度（Perplexity）差距，且内存开销小于1%，推理延迟与现有的4-bit部署方案持平。

Conclusion: SPEAR 通过动态、细粒度的误差补偿方案，成功弥合了4-bit量化模型与FP16基准之间的性能差距，同时保持了极低的内存开销与延迟，为低比特LLM服务的高效部署提供了新路径。

Abstract: Efficient large language model (LLM) serving is increasingly constrained by deployment cost. Quantization is a key technique for reducing serving cost, yet even state-of-the-art 4-bit quantizers exhibit a noticeable quality gap from FP16, particularly for smaller models where low-bit serving is most beneficial. We identify a fundamental cause of this gap: quantization error is highly input-dependent and varies substantially across tokens, while existing post-quantization compensation methods are static and apply identical corrections to all inputs. As a result, easy tokens are over-corrected while hard tokens remain under-corrected.
  We present SPEAR, a system for post-quantization error-adaptive recovery that improves low-bit LLM serving. SPEAR introduces lightweight Error Compensators (ECs) modulated by per-token gates and places them only at the most error-sensitive layers identified through a CKA-guided entropy-aware diagnostic. This focuses a small parameter budget where it is most effective.
  Efficient deployment of ECs presents several systems challenges, including additional computation, tensor-parallel synchronization caused by input-dependent gating, and latency instability across configurations. SPEAR addresses these issues through adaptive kernel-fusion dispatch, combining an epilogue-integrated peer-reduction kernel with P2P dual-write to fuse the post-EC computation into low-bit GEMMs, and an SLO-constrained EC-aware scheduler for predictable serving performance.
  Across challenging per-channel quantization settings, SPEAR recovers 56-75% of the perplexity gap between W4 and FP16 while adding less than 1% model memory overhead and maintaining latency comparable to a widely used 4-bit serving deployment.

</details>


### [146] [A Fast Locality Simulator for GEMM Design-Space Exploration on Multi-Chiplet GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11716)
*Euijun Chung,Hyesoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对多芯粒GPU设计了精细的流量模拟器，发现CTA遍历顺序对GEMM的远程HBM流量影响巨大，并通过AI代理证实了通过优化遍历顺序可显著降低该流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多芯粒GPU架构中，远程HBM访问会严重影响性能和能效。由于GEMM在LLM中的重要性，如何针对其特定配置优化芯片间流量以最小化远程访问成为一个复杂且关键的问题。

Method: 提出并构建了一个快速、功能性、瓦片级（tile-level）的局部性模拟器，能够建模CTA调度、各芯粒L2缓存以及本地/远程HBM访问；并使用AI代理（agentic AI）在设计空间中进行优化探索。

Result: 实验表明，对于相同的GEMM维度，设计空间中远程流量的差异高达90倍；AI代理发现，在轮询分配下，2D块式（2D block-swizzle）CTA遍历顺序相比最优的1D遍历顺序，可将远程流量降低高达5.1倍。

Conclusion: 芯片间流量的最小化对于多芯粒GPU至关重要。研究证明，CTA遍历顺序是影响芯片间流量的关键设计杠杆，而AI代理利用仿真器反馈能够有效优化这一参数。

Abstract: Multi-chiplet GPUs split memory into local and remote HBM regions across a silicon interposer, and reducing the remote HBM traffic is crucial for the performance and energy efficiency of multi-chiplet GPUs. For general matrix multiplication (GEMM), the dominant operator in large language models (LLMs), the resulting inter-chiplet traffic depends strongly on kernel choices such as operand layout, CTA traversal order, and data placement, and the optimal strategy to minimize remote accesses is nontrivial. We present a fast, functional, tile-level locality simulator that models CTA scheduling, per-chiplet L2 caches, and local/remote HBM accesses to evaluate a full-size LLM GEMM configuration. Across representative LLM GEMMs, the simulator shows that remote traffic varies by up to 90x across the design space for the same GEMM dimensions. Moreover, using the simulator as feedback, an agentic AI discovers that a 2D block-swizzle CTA traversal reduces remote traffic over the best 1D traversal by up to 5.1x under round-robin placement, identifying CTA traversal order as a first-order, GEMM-dependent design knob for inter-chiplet traffic.

</details>


### [147] [Making Locality-aware GEMM Compatible with Page-Granularity Placement on Chiplet GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11718)
*Euijun Chung,Jae Hyung Ju,Hyesoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对多芯粒 GPU 内存访问不均匀的问题，本文提出 Chiplet-Contiguous Layout 方法。通过优化数据排布，在无需更改软硬件的情况下，显著降低了 LLM 任务中的跨芯片远程内存流量，提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多芯粒 GPU 的非均匀内存访问特性使得数据局部性对性能至关重要，但传统的页级别交错存储（interleaving）往往与最优的数据分布策略存在冲突，限制了 GEMM 操作的效率。

Method: 提出 Chiplet-Contiguous Layout，通过将芯粒局部数据进行连续化存储，使其可与现有的页粒度内存映射兼容，从而在不增加软硬件复杂性的前提下，实现对不同 GEMM 形状的局部性感知调度与数据放置。

Result: 在 Qwen 3 30B 和 Llama 3.1 70B 的 LLM 推理与训练任务中，该方法对比 4KB 交错存储，平均减少了 24.7 倍和 19.2 倍的远程 HBM 流量；对比粗粒度的局部感知策略，流量也分别降低了 4.1 倍和 2.1 倍。

Conclusion: Chiplet-Contiguous Layout 是一种无需修改操作系统或硬件的内存布局方案，有效提升了多芯粒 GPU 在 LLM 工作负载下的内存局部性，显著降低了跨芯粒远程内存访问流量。

Abstract: Multi-chiplet GPUs scale compute throughput and high-bandwidth memory (HBM) capacity, but their non-uniform memory system makes locality between chiplets and their data critical to the GPU's performance and energy efficiency. Locality-aware scheduling and data placement identify which data should reside near each chiplet. However, in general matrix multiplication (GEMM), locality-aware data placement often becomes incompatible with a fixed page-granularity data interleaving, since the optimal granularity for mapping data across chiplets varies widely across workloads. We propose Chiplet-Contiguous Layout, a global memory layout that stores chiplet-local data contiguously. Chiplet-Contiguous Layout enables locality-aware placement compatible with page-granularity placement across diverse large language model (LLM) GEMM shapes, without changes to the operating system or hardware. On representative LLM inference and training GEMMs from Qwen 3 30B and Llama 3.1 70B, Chiplet-Contiguous Layout on average reduces remote HBM traffic by 24.7x on Qwen and 19.2x on Llama over 4KB interleaving, and by 4.1x and 2.1x over coarse locality-aware placement.

</details>


### [148] [BenDi: An Energy-Efficient Quasi-Stochastic Systolic Architecture for Edge Bioelectronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12235)
*Bochen Ye,Yihan Pan,Shady Agwa,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BenDi，一种针对边缘生物信号监测的高能效、微型化 AI 硬件架构，通过准随机计算和多级优化大幅提升了硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于 ECG 等生物信号监测的 AI 模型（如 CNN）计算资源需求过高，难以在资源受限的边缘设备上实现长期的实时监测。

Method: 提出 BenDi 架构，主要技术手段包括：1. Bent-Pyramid 数据格式（用于准随机乘法）；2. DiP 脉动数据流；3. 硬件感知量化；4. 低电压运行（0.5V）。

Result: 在 22nm 工艺下（0.5V，100MHz），BenDi 相比现有的二值权重脉动阵列，面积减小 3.35 倍，能效提升 5 倍；在 MIT-BIH 和 Apnea-ECG 基准测试上，仅产生 1%~3.3% 的精度损失。

Conclusion: BenDi 是一种适用于边缘生物电子系统的低功耗、面积高效的准随机脉动架构。它通过多层次优化在牺牲极小精度（1%~3.3%）的前提下，实现了与先进架构相比显著的能效（5倍）和面积（3.35倍）优势，是资源受限医疗监控设备的理想选择。

Abstract: Continuous long-term monitoring and diagnosis of biomedical signals, such as electrocardiograms (ECGs), can help mitigate an increasing threat to public health. Artificial Intelligence (AI) models, such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), provide accurate monitoring and classification for relevant diseases; however, they require more computational resources than conventional AI hardware can typically afford, especially for a resource-constrained environment on the edge. In this work, we present BenDi, an energy-efficient quasi-stochastic systolic architecture for bioelectronic systems on the edge. BenDi leverages multiple levels of energy and power optimization, ranging from circuits to software quantization, including low supply voltage, the \underline{Ben}t-Pyramid data format for quasi-stochastic multiplication, the \underline{Di}P systolic dataflow, and hardware-aware quantization, to handle CNNs with high accuracy on the edge within limited hardware budgets. The hardware implementation results, using a commercial 22nm technology, show that BenDi architecture, at 0.5 Voltage and 100 MHz, offers 3.35x smaller area and 5x higher energy efficiency, compared to state-of-the-art binary-based weight-stationary systolic architectures. Regarding Bioelectronic edge systems, BenDi achieves an order-of-magnitude improvement in energy efficiency and another order-of-magnitude improvement in area efficiency, compared to its counterparts. This significant improvement comes at the cost of 1\% to 3.3\% accuracy loss on the MIT-BIH and Apnea-ECG benchmarks, respectively, compared with conventional computing using the 32-bit floating-point format.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Annealed Entropic Allocation for Ranking and Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11347)
*Xin Fei,Juergen Branke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种改进的预算分配框架，通过引入退火权重和鞍点近似，解决了传统算法在处理多竞争者时的切换不稳定问题，大幅提升了排序与选择任务在有限资源下的优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决排序与选择（Ranking and Selection）问题中，传统极大极小大偏差率目标在处理多个竞争者接近最优时出现的“硬切换”问题，提升有限预算下的分配效率。

Method: 提出退火熵分配（Annealed Entropic Allocation）框架，将非平滑的极大极小目标替换为加权log-sum-exp代理函数，结合软最小（soft-min）权重进行分配，并引入鞍点近似（saddlepoint approximation）作为亚指数修正，同时采用退火策略（将平滑参数退火至零）。

Result: 理论上证明了该代理函数一致收敛至硬最小函数，且分配映射在内部是连续的；数值实验显示该方法在Gaussian和指数分布实例中具备竞争力，特别是在竞争者近似平局时表现出色。

Conclusion: 该方法通过退火加权策略成功平滑了非平滑的极大极小目标，利用鞍点近似提升了有限预算下的区分度，并具备良好的收敛理论支撑，在复杂（尤其是多竞争者相近）场景下表现出优异性能。

Abstract: We propose Annealed Entropic Allocation, an annealed weighted soft-min framework for sequential budget allocation in ranking and selection. The central idea is to replace the non-smooth maximin large-deviation rate objective with a weighted log-sum-exp surrogate that aggregates challenger-specific pairwise scores through soft-min weights, mitigating hard switching when several challengers are nearly active. To improve finite-budget discrimination, we incorporate the saddlepoint approximation -- a sub-exponential correction derived from refined pairwise tail asymptotics. Because these corrections are sub-exponential and the smoothing parameter is annealed to zero, the surrogate preserves the same first-order large-deviation target as the classical maximin formulation. We show that the surrogate converges uniformly to the hard minimum, that the soft-min weights concentrate on the active challengers, and that, under fixed weights, the induced target allocation map is continuous on the simplex interior. Numerical experiments on Gaussian and exponential instances demonstrate competitive performance, especially when multiple challengers are nearly tied.

</details>


### [150] [Enhancing Spectral Embedding through Robust and Flexible Knowledge Transfer in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11570)
*Feiqing Huang,Zongqi Xia,Rong Ma,Tianxi Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对罕见病小样本数据，本文提出了一种新型谱嵌入方法，通过灵活地整合外部知识来学习低维特征，在弱对齐和高异质性条件下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对罕见病电子健康记录（EHR）数据样本量小但维度高的挑战，旨在寻求一种能有效融合大规模通用人群知识，且无需严格假设信号一一对应关系的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于谱的无监督表示学习框架，包含两个核心步骤：1. 从知识矩阵（利用更广泛人群数据构建）中识别并剔除无关成分；2. 采用基于投影的方法，分别重建“共享”成分与“异质”成分，从而在保留罕见病特有特征的同时有效借用外部先验信息。

Result: 通过模拟实验及多发性硬化症（MS）队列的真实数据验证，该方法在共享信号较弱及部分对齐的复杂场景下，特征提取性能显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过放松严格的对齐假设，利用迁移学习实现了在罕见病小样本数据下的稳健特征学习，在处理稀疏、高维且异质性强的罕见病EHR数据时具有显著优势。

Abstract: We propose a spectral-based, unsupervised representation learning framework to derive low-dimensional embeddings for clinical concepts and patients in rare disease cohorts from electronic health records, where data are high-dimensional but sample sizes are limited. To overcome this challenge, we incorporate a knowledge matrix extracted from a broader population that shares a partially overlapping subspace with the rare-disease cohort. Our method departs from existing approaches by relaxing restrictive one-to-one signal-alignment assumptions between the latent data matrix and knowledge matrix, allowing more flexible and realistic forms of structured sharing. We introduce a novel two-step spectral embedding procedure: first, we identify and remove irrelevant components from the knowledge matrix; then, we apply a projection-based method to separately recover shared and heterogeneous components. Simulations and an analysis of a real-world multiple sclerosis cohort show that the proposed method outperforms competing approaches, particularly in challenging scenarios where shared signals are weak and only partially aligned, as is common in rare-disease data.

</details>


### [151] [Renewable Lasso without Batch-Number Constraints: A Gradient-Enhanced Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11738)
*Junzhuo Gao,Ling Peng,Xu Guo,Heng Lian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对高维流式广义线性模型，提出了一种梯度增强的代理损失估计方法，不仅消除了对数据批次数量的限制，还通过高效的消息传递机制实现了通信受限的分布式环境下的精确参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高维广义线性模型（GLM）的流式数据在线估计研究通常受到批次数量的限制，在分布式场景下往往需要昂贵的通信成本或复杂的客户端计算，亟需更高效且不受批次约束的方法。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于“梯度增强代理损失（gradient-enhanced surrogate loss）”的非分布式在线估计方法；2. 将其扩展至主从架构的分布式流数据环境，仅通过交换梯度向量（汇总信息）进行通信；3. 通过改进优化方案，降低了客户端的计算负担，并推导了高维尺度下的非渐近误差界限。

Result: 在模拟的线性模型和逻辑回归模型中，以及真实数据应用中，该方法证明了相比现有可更新估计器具有更高的估计精度，且在无需限制批次数量的情况下保持稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法在非分布式和分布式高维在线估计中表现优异，突破了以往研究中对批次数量的严格限制，在精度上优于现有的可更新估计器。

Abstract: We study online estimation for high-dimensional generalized linear models with streaming data. First, for the non-distributed setting, we propose a gradient-enhanced surrogate loss that approximates the cumulative loss using only historical summaries, which modifies and improves upon the existing renewable estimation approach for the same model in the high-dimensional setting, and removes the batch-number constraint in previous studies. We then extend the method to distributed streaming data under the master-client architecture, where batches are partitioned across sites and only summaries (gradient vectors) are exchanged. Instead of directing applying the popular method of Jordan et al. (2019) to the surrogate quadratic loss, our adjusted approach does not require the clients to compute the full surrogate loss. We derive non-asymptotic error bounds under the high-dimensional scaling, without the stringent constraint on the number of batches in the previous studies. Simulation results under linear and logistic models, together with a real-data application, show improved accuracy over existing renewable estimators.

</details>


### [152] [Conformal Bayes under Label Shift: Post-Hoc Calibration vs. In-Training Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11865)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨了标签偏移下共形贝叶斯的两种校准策略，证明了通过重要性加权的事后校准与训练中适应方案均能实现统计有效性，其中训练中适应在存在偏差的训练场景下更具效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在标签偏移（即训练集和测试集标签分布不同）的情况下，传统的共形贝叶斯预测集可能失效，需要开发统计有效且几何高效的校准方法。

Method: 提出并对比两种应对标签偏移的机制：1. 事后校准：通过重要性加权的分位数调整共形阈值，保持参数后验不变；2. 训练中适应：通过对参数后验进行重要性加权，生成目标域的预测分布，并基于此构建HPD预测集。

Result: 两种方法均能在目标域实现名义覆盖率。实验证明，当训练分布存在偏差（lead-optimization）时，训练中适应方法是更有效的去偏算子，能优化预测区间的精密度。

Conclusion: 在标签偏移（label shift）下，共形贝叶斯（Conformal Bayes）可以通过事后校准（Post-hoc calibration）和训练中适应（In-training adaptation）两种方法恢复有效覆盖。在无偏训练中两者效果一致；在导向优化（lead-optimization）场景下，训练中适应表现出更好的去偏能力，在保证覆盖率的同时减小了预测区间宽度。

Abstract: Conformal Bayes combines Bayesian posterior predictives with conformal calibration to produce prediction sets that are both statistically valid and geometrically efficient. We study conformal Bayes under label shift from a unified perspective, identifying two complementary approaches that restore nominal target-domain coverage through importance-weighted conformal calibration but operate through independent mechanisms. \emph{Post-hoc calibration} tilts the posterior predictive toward the target domain and corrects the conformal threshold via an importance-weighted quantile, leaving the parameter posterior unchanged. \emph{In-training adaptation} tilts the parameter posterior itself to the target domain, producing a corrected predictive whose highest predictive density region serves as the highest predictive density (HPD) based prediction set under the fitted target predictive; efficiency is model-dependent and does not imply finite-sample conditional optimality. Two controlled experiments show that in an unbiased training regime both strategies achieve valid coverage equally, while in a lead-optimization regime in-training adaptation acts as a debiasing operator, reducing interval width at unchanged coverage.

</details>


### [153] [From Persistence to Survival: Hypothesis Testing, Effect Sizes and Vectorisation for Topological Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11911)
*Juliette Murris,Bernadette Stolz,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: STRAND 是一种将持久图（PDs）转化为生存分析框架的新方法，通过持久性生存函数实现了解释性分析、假设检验与高效向量化的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久同调图（Persistence Diagrams）不属于向量空间，现有的统计工具与下游预测模型之间缺乏有效衔接，且缺乏一种统一的框架来兼顾统计检验和特征表示。

Method: 将持久图中的特征持久性视为时间至事件数据，通过构建持久性生存函数 $S(t) = \mathbb{P}(p > t)$ 作为核心表示对象，进而推导出非参数两样本检验、可解释的效应量度量，以及 1-Wasserstein 稳定的特征向量用于下游预测。

Result: 在合成数据上验证了其校准性和统计效能；在 14 个图数据和 3D 点云实验中表现出极具竞争力的向量化能力；成功应用于 fMRI 脑功能连接分析。

Conclusion: STRAND是一个首创的框架，通过将持久同调图（PDs）视为生存数据，实现了单一表示下的统计推断（假设检验）与机器学习向量化，提供了一种兼具数学严谨性、可解释性和计算高效性的新视角。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams are common representations in topological data analysis, but they do not naturally live in a vector space, and the statistical tools developed for comparing them have largely evolved separately from those used for downstream prediction. We introduce STRAND (Survival Topological Representation ANalysis of Diagrams), which treats (collections of) PDs as survival data: each topological feature with persistence value $p = d - b$ is a fully observed time-to-event, and the persistence survival function $S(t) = \mathbb{P}(p > t)$ is the central object for comparing diagrams. From this single representation we derive (i) a non-parametric two-sample test with calibrated Type I error and high power from a small number of diagrams; (ii) interpretable effect sizes; and (iii) a 1-Wasserstein-stable feature vector for downstream machine learning. We validate calibration and power on synthetic manifolds with controlled topology, demonstrate competitive vectorisation across 14 graph and 3D point cloud benchmarks, and apply the method to study functional brain connectivity in fMRI/neuroscience data. To our knowledge, STRAND is the first method to provide hypothesis testing and vectorisation for persistence diagrams from a single coherent and interpretable representation.

</details>


### [154] [Phase Transitions in Attention: A Bayesian Theory of Copy Head Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12058)
*Itay Lavie,Kirsten Fischer,Andrey Lekov,Frederic Van Maele,Zohar Ringel,Moritz Helias*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯理论揭示了Transformer中注意力机制及其“复制子电路”突现的机理，发现Softmax注意力存在一阶相变，这解释了为什么大模型在训练中会突然获得上下文学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解释Transformer模型中“上下文学习”的关键机制及其注意力模式在训练过程中为何会突现。

Method: 1. 建立注意力特征学习的贝叶斯理论模型；
2. 简化单层Softmax注意力网络在复制任务下的后验概率，并降维至低维序参数空间；
3. 将该理论结果与线性注意力机制进行对比分析；
4. 通过贝叶斯采样和Adam优化器训练进行验证。

Result: Softmax注意力在训练过程中表现出“一阶相变”，而线性注意力则表现为“二阶相变”后平滑的演变（crossover）；推导出了注意力矩阵的闭式后验，证明了复制任务下的相变现象。

Conclusion: 该研究通过第一性原理提供了“复制子电路”突现的理论解释，指出Softmax注意力机制中存在一阶相变，这与大模型训练中观察到的能力突现现象一致。

Abstract: Attention is the key mechanism underlying in-context learning in transformers, and attention patterns have been observed empirically to emerge abruptly during training. We present a Bayesian theory of feature learning in attention; we then focus on how the copy subcircuit in the first layer of an induction head is learned by analyzing a single-layer softmax attention network trained on a copy task. We derive a closed-form posterior over the attention matrix and reduce it to a low-dimensional order parameter space. This reduction reveals a phase transition in the amount of training data, which we verify using both Bayesian sampling and standard training with Adam. We contrast our results with linear attention and find that softmax attention exhibits a \emph{first-order phase transition} while in linear attention an initial \emph{second-order phase transition} is followed by a smooth, continuous evolution toward the structured attention pattern (\emph{crossover}). Our work provides a first-principles theoretical account of the abrupt emergence of the copy subcircuit, reminiscent of the one observed in training large language models.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Restless bandits with imperfect binary feedback: PCL-indexability analysis and computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11192)
*José Niño-Mora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对带传感误差的休眠老虎机问题，本文提出了一个基于PCL的分析框架以计算Whittle/MP指标，并通过理论验证与数值实验证明了该策略的鲁棒性及其相较于传统方法的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机会频谱接入中存在传感误差（不完美反馈）的二进制潜状态休眠老虎机（Restless Bandits）问题，旨在克服以往研究中参数限制严苛的问题。

Method: 开发了一种基于部分守恒定律（PCL）的分析与计算框架，结合了确定性骨架分析、更新分解（renewal decompositions）和词组合学；对于无法完全解析验证的区域，提出了高效的数值方案来计算边际生产力（MP）指标。

Result: 成功推导出了多个阈值区间下折扣奖励和资源度量的易处理表达式，验证了PCL可指标性条件；计算实验表明，MP指标策略在广泛的参数范围内表现出色。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于MP指标的策略在处理具有二进制潜状态和不完美反馈的休眠老虎机问题时，通常优于传统的基准策略，且该方法不需要以往研究中所要求的严苛参数限制。

Abstract: We study restless bandits with binary latent states and imperfect binary feedback, motivated by opportunistic spectrum access with sensing errors. For the associated belief-state model, we develop a partial conservation laws (PCL)-based analytical and computational framework for establishing indexability and evaluating the Whittle index, building on a verification theorem for real-state discounted restless bandits. The framework analyzes the stochastic dynamics via an associated deterministic skeleton, renewal decompositions, and combinatorics on words. It yields tractable expressions for discounted reward and resource metrics in several threshold regimes, enabling full verification of the PCL-indexability conditions there. For the remaining regime, where a complete analytic verification is not achieved in this paper, we derive efficient numerical schemes for computing the relevant marginal metrics and the marginal productivity (MP) index, which equals the Whittle index when those conditions hold. Extensive computational experiments provide strong evidence that these conditions also hold in that regime across broad parameter ranges and without the stringent parameter restrictions imposed in prior work. The experiments further show that theMP index policy typically outperforms standard benchmark policies, often by a substantial margin.

</details>


### [156] [Physics-informed generative AI for semiconductor manufacturing: Enforcing hard physical constraints in generative models by construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11247)
*Yaser Mike Banad,Sarah Sharif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对半导体制造等对物理约束要求极高的领域，本文主张生成式AI必须在架构层面“内嵌”物理定律，而非仅仅依赖后处理过滤。作者提出了相关的技术路线与未来研究议程，确立了“物理内嵌”优于“后期筛选”的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式模型在物理系统中应用时，常因忽略硬性物理约束而产生不可用的“无效样本”。本文旨在探讨如何在生成模型的设计阶段直接植入物理规律，以解决物理约束系统的设计与制造问题。

Method: 本文通过跨学科综述，梳理了物理信息生成式人工智能（Physics-informed Generative AI）的架构工具包（如物理信息扩散模型、PDE约束变分模型、神经算子先验等），并提出了生成模型与物理模拟器集成的四种范式。

Result: 确立了生成模型与物理模拟器集成的四种集成模式；提出了以物理保真度基准测试、可微仿真基础设施和多模态物理设计基座模型为核心的研究议程。

Conclusion: 在物理约束严格的领域（如半导体制造），通过架构设计“内嵌”物理约束的生成模型，在性能和有效性上必然优于仅依靠后期筛选的模型。半导体制造是验证和推动这一范式的理想试验场。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to propose designs, data, and control actions for physical systems, yet many such systems are governed by hard physical constraints rather than by perceptual plausibility. Semiconductor manufacturing provides a demanding test case: generated masks, layouts, synthetic defect data, and process recipes must obey lithography, transport, reaction, and device-physics constraints, because physically invalid samples are not merely low quality but unusable. This Perspective argues that semiconductor manufacturing exposes a broader computational-science challenge, namely that generative AI for constrained physical domains must be physics-informed by construction, not corrected only through post-hoc filtering. We survey the emerging architectural toolkit, including physics-informed diffusion, PDE-constrained variational models, neural-operator priors, and conservation-law-respecting generative networks, and show how it connects to differentiable lithography, TCAD, process simulation, and autonomous experimentation. We identify four integration patterns between generative models and physics-based simulators, and we propose a research agenda centered on physics-fidelity benchmarks, differentiable simulator infrastructure, and multimodal foundation models for physical design and manufacturing. The central claim is analytical rather than rhetorical: where physical validity is the binding criterion of success, architectures that enforce it by construction should be expected to outperform those that filter for it after the fact, and the fab is the setting where this distinction is sharpest.

</details>


### [157] [To Intervene or Not: Guiding Inference-time Alignment with Probabilistic Model Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11201)
*Jin Gan,Xin Li,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有大模型推理时对齐因盲目采纳引导导致性能不稳的问题，作者提出了 BlendIn 框架，通过质量感知加权融合机制动态整合模型知识，实现了更高效、更可靠的输出对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法缺乏对引导质量的准确评估，盲目采纳可能导致性能下降（模型混乱）和过度干预。

Method: 提出 BlendIn 框架，将推理时对齐从二元决策优化为混合分布生成；引入质量感知度量系统来动态评估引导的可靠性，并据此对不同模型的贡献进行比例加权。

Result: 相比现有方法，BlendIn 能够有效保留有效引导、抑制不可靠建议，在复杂模型对上实现了高达 50% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: BlendIn 框架通过引入质量感知与加权融合机制，有效解决了推理时对齐中盲目采纳引导导致的性能不稳定和过度干预问题，显著提升了模型对齐的效率与效果。

Abstract: The wide deployment of LLMs has made model alignment necessary to make newly trained models safely and effectively respond to user instructions. Among different methods, inference-time alignment is often cheaper as it intervenes (i.e., offers guidances) only during output generation. Existing proposals apply guidances extracted from certain aligned models without properly assessing their reliability. Nonetheless, our systematic evaluation reveals that guidance effectiveness varies drastically across models; since ineffective guidances lead to further confusion and thus further interventions, the resulting excessive interventions typically indicate poor performance. To make interventions more effective and thus more efficient, we introduce BlendIn, an inference-time alignment framework that shifts from binary decisions to creating hybrid distributions integrating both models' knowledge. BlendIn stabilizes inference-time alignment by performing quality-aware alignment and proportionally weighting each model's contribution based on reliability. Compared with existing works, it preserves beneficial guidance while downweighting unreliable suggestions. BlendIn provides both diagnostic signals and mitigation strategies for misaligned guidance, achieving consistent and up to 50% performance improvement on challenging model pairs. Our code is available at: https://github.com/DecayingSeart/BlendIn.

</details>


### [158] [Dual-Stance Evaluation of Sycophancy: The Structure of Agreement and the Limits of Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11205)
*Matthew James Buchan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过双立场评估发现，LLM虽然在内部表示中区分了谄媚与事实认同，但现有的激活引导技术无法实现二者的解耦，导致在减少谄媚行为时不可避免地牺牲了事实的正确性，揭示了模型表示可读与可控之间的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导研究多关注行为改变，但未深入探讨谄媚（sycophancy）消除是否会误伤模型对客观事实的认同，即模型在消除讨好人类倾向的同时，是否会导致模型丧失对客观事实的判断力。

Method: 引入“双立场评估”（dual-stance evaluation）框架，对比模型对事实正确/错误观点以及谄媚/反逆向观点的认同偏差；应用质心差引导（centroid-difference steering）在Llama-3-8B-Instruct模型上进行实验。

Result: 发现谄媚行为和事实认同在模型内部表现为几何上不同的子空间，但标准的质心差引导方向在这两个方向上的投影几乎一致，导致引导过程在消除谄媚的同时，非预期地降低了对事实的认同度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了“表征可读性”与“干预可写性”之间的根本鸿沟：即使模型在几何上区分了谄媚行为和事实认同，现有的激活引导（Activation Steering）技术仍无法精细化地仅消除谄媚而不损害事实准确性。

Abstract: Activation steering can shift LLM behaviour, but standard evaluations do not typically test whether a sycophancy-reduction direction also suppresses agreement with factually correct statements. We introduce dual-stance evaluation, which tests both stances of each topic, and apply it to centroid-difference steering on Llama-3-8B-Instruct. We find a dissociation: the model represents sycophantic and factual agreement in geometrically distinct subspaces, yet the steering direction projects equally onto both and cannot differentially target either. The direction accordingly reduces agreement with factually correct statements (e.g. that the Earth is round) as well as sycophantic ones. All other static properties of the two activation groups are matched, suggesting the behavioural dissociation arises from generation dynamics or from finer-grained structure that residual-stream analysis cannot resolve. The pattern illustrates a general gap: representations that are readable from activations may not be writable through them.

</details>


### [159] [Few-Shot Resampling for Scalable Statistically-Sound Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11235)
*Leonardo Pellegrina,Fabio Vandin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FewRS 是一种新型重采样评估框架，通过数学推导出的严格偏差界限，大幅减少了计算所需的重采样次数，实现了大规模数据集统计显著性验证的高效率与高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于重采样的数据挖掘结果统计显著性评估方法需要处理数千个数据集，计算成本极高，在大规模数据集或计算密集型任务中不可行。

Method: 提出 FewRS 方法，基于对数据挖掘结果质量统计量的上确界偏差（supremum deviation）的新型界限推导（novel bound），从而将所需的重采样次数降低至极小规模。

Result: 在模式挖掘和网络分析等任务中，FewRS 相比现有先进方法将运行时间缩短了最多两个数量级，同时维持了高统计功效。

Conclusion: FewRS 是一种高效且具有统计严谨性的重采样评估方法，能够通过极少量的重采样数据集实现对大规模数据挖掘结果的显著性验证，显著提升了计算效率并保持了高统计功效。

Abstract: A key step in knowledge discovery is the evaluation of data mining results. In several applications, including pattern mining, graph analysis, and others, this step includes the evaluation of the statistical significance of the results, to avoid spurious discoveries due only to noise or random fluctuations in the data. While specialized procedures have been developed for some specific applications, resampling-based approaches are widely used, in particular for complex analyses where analytical results cannot be derived. However, current resampling-based approaches require the generation and analysis of thousands of resampled datasets, and are therefore impractical for large datasets or computationally intensive analyses.
  In this paper, we introduce FewRS, a simple and effective resampling-based approach to assess the statistical significance of data mining results with rigorous guarantees on the probability of false discoveries. Our approach can be used in every situation where resampling-based approaches are applied. FewRS builds on our derivation of a novel bound to the supremum deviation of test statistics representing the quality of data mining results. We prove that FewRS needs to generate and analyze an extremely small number of resampled datasets, leading to a highly scalable approach with wide applicability. We test our approach on common tasks such as pattern mining and network analysis. In all cases, our approach results in a reduction of up to two orders of magnitude in running time compared to the state of the art, while preserving high statistical power, enabling the statistical validation of data mining results on large-scale real-world datasets.

</details>


### [160] [ProHiFlo: Hierarchical Flow Matching with Functional Guidance for De Novo Protein Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11243)
*Chuanzhen Wang,Meade Cleti,Pete Jano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProHiFlo 是一种创新的层次化流匹配方法，通过从粗到细的生成策略和功能引导机制，在大幅提升蛋白质生成效率的同时，在复杂功能设计任务中超越了 RFDiffusion 等现有 SOTA 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型和流匹配生成方法多为单分辨率，推理计算昂贵，且缺乏对特定功能约束的有效整合手段。

Method: 提出了一个层次化流匹配框架：1. 采用从粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的生成策略，先建模骨架几何结构再细化至全原子；2. 引入基于预训练模型的功能引导机制（functional guidance），无需重训练即可执行约束生成；3. 设计了自适应 SE(3)-等变架构以实现高效的多尺度处理。

Result: 在无条件生成、基序支架（motif scaffolding）和功能设计任务中达到SOTA水平。采样步数减少了4倍；在酶活性位点支架任务中，成功率达到58.9%（作为对比，RFDiffusion为41.2%）。

Conclusion: ProHiFlo 证明了层次化流匹配（hierarchical flow matching）在蛋白质从头设计中的有效性，通过从粗到细的多尺度建模显著降低了推理成本，并结合功能引导机制，实现了兼顾效率与任务成功率的蛋白质生成。

Abstract: De novo protein generation has transformative potential in therapeutic design, enzyme engineering, and synthetic biology. While diffusion-based and flow matching approaches have achieved progress, they typically operate at single resolution and lack mechanisms for incorporating functional constraints. We introduce ProHiFlo, a hierarchical flow matching framework with three innovations: (1) coarse-to-fine generation that models backbone geometry before refining to all-atom coordinates, reducing computational cost while maintaining accuracy; (2) functional guidance leveraging pretrained predictors to steer generation toward desired properties without retraining; (3) adaptive SE(3)-equivariant architecture for efficient multi-scale processing. Experiments on unconditional generation, motif scaffolding, and functional design demonstrate state-ofthe-art performance while requiring 4 fewer sampling steps. On enzyme active site scaffolding, ProHiFlo achieves 58.9% success rate compared to 41.2% for RFDiffusion.

</details>


### [161] [Mechanical Field Networks: Structured Neural Dynamics for Multivariate Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11251)
*Xingji Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MF-Net 是一种基于“共享场”的新型递归动力学建模框架，通过将变量交互融入演化机制，在实现高精度时序预测的同时，能够直接从模型中读取变量间的潜在交互结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多变量动力系统建模方法在“可解释性”与“灵活性”之间往往难以兼顾，且交互结构通常需要预先指定或隐含在复杂的动态变化中，难以直观解读。

Method: 提出 MF-Net 模型，将所有变量表示为共享场状态（Shared Field State），并通过可学习的“机械转换定律”（Mechanical Transition）更新场状态。该转换过程依赖于可学习的变量间关系，这些关系既驱动预测，又能直接提取出潜在的交互结构。

Result: 在 Lorenz-96 等系统测试中，MF-Net 在 8 步预测中达到 0.798 的 R² 指数，且能够完美恢复局部耦合结构（Precision@K 为 1.000）。在真实神经记录和生态时间序列数据中，同样表现出良好的预测准确性与结构可解释性。

Conclusion: MF-Net 提供了一个结构可读的动态建模框架，能够通过前向演化学习变量间的交互关系，并在多种动力学系统和真实数据中展现了优异的预测性能与结构捕捉能力。

Abstract: Many multivariate dynamical systems are observed only through trajectories, leaving the mechanisms governing their joint dynamics hidden. Existing approaches can impose interpretable dynamics or learn flexible state transitions, yet the resulting interaction structure is typically either specified in advance or left implicit within the learned dynamics. We introduce MF-Net, a recurrent dynamical model that represents all variables in a shared field state and updates this state through a learned relation law. Each variable carries a field component, and these components evolve jointly through a learnable mechanical transition. Here, mechanical refers to the relation-to-motion organization of the transition, where learned relations shape state-dependent flows, field responses, and motion tendencies that move the field state forward. The resulting structure is part of the rollout itself: learned relations influence how the field moves, and the same internal quantities support both forecasting and structural readout. Across known-law interaction systems, chaotic benchmarks, real neural recordings, and ecological time series, MF-Net achieves competitive short- and medium-horizon forecasting while retaining inspectable structural readout. On the 40-dimensional Lorenz--96 testbed, MF-Net achieves an eight-step $R^2$ of $0.798\pm0.018$; across five seeds, its learned relation matrix recovers the local coupling support with a local/nonlocal strength ratio of $19.80\pm1.00$ and Precision@$K$ of $1.000\pm0.000$. MF-Net provides a structure-readable dynamical modeling framework in which learned relations are trained through forward evolution and, on real data, interpreted as functional predictive couplings under appropriate observational limits.

</details>


### [162] [Signed Compression Progress on a Sealed Audit is Goodhart-Resistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11417)
*Ayush Mittal,Dhruv Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探讨了“压缩进度作为内在动机”的有效性，证明了只要使用密封独立测试集（sealed-audit），智能体通过压缩进度获得的内在奖励就等同于实际性能的提升，从而从算法上杜绝了对奖励机制的投机性操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对压缩进度作为内在动机时常面临的“奖励操纵”（如噪声干扰或在自身数据上评估导致的正反馈偏差）问题，旨在从理论上严格定义并证明什么是“可信”的改进奖励。

Method: 通过数学框架证明了累积奖励与审计性能提升的级数关系（telescoping）；使用Lean 4实现了核心理论证明；并在ARC-TGI网格转换任务上进行了实验验证，对比了多种奖励机制的鲁棒性。

Result: 理论上证明了当奖励定义为固定密封评估集的损失差时，其累积奖励存在严谨的性能上界；实验显示该方法能有效抵御clip-farming、流泄露和noisy-TV等常见的奖励操纵策略。

Conclusion: 证明了基于密封审计（sealed-audit）的压缩进度奖励是一种“可信”的内在激励，能够确保奖励与实际性能提升紧密耦合，并指出了避免奖励作弊（如利用噪声或模型过拟合）的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Compression progress is a long-standing proposal for intrinsic motivation: reward an agent when its world model becomes better at predicting or compressing experience. The folk claim is that this reward is "credible" because it is paid only for learning. We make this precise and prove it. If intrinsic reward is the signed decrease of a fixed sealed-audit loss, r_t = E(theta_{t-1}) - E(theta_t), then cumulative reward telescopes exactly to endpoint audit improvement, so no policy can push reward up indefinitely while true audit performance stagnates or degrades. For finite audit panels the same result holds with a sharp false-positive budget: cumulative empirical reward is at most true audit improvement plus 2 Delta_n(F, delta), the uniform audit deviation of the model class. This is horizon-free: adaptivity over time costs nothing once the sealed panel uniformly controls the class.
  The theorem also identifies the failure modes: the guarantee disappears if progress is clipped, scored on the agent's own stream, exposed to a high-capacity model on a reusable panel, or applied to a neural class that makes Delta_n vacuous. We give a Lean 4 mechanization of the structural core (telescoping, the finite-audit bound, finite Gibbs, and the entropy floor) and an experiment suite on ARC-TGI grid-transformation generators with adaptive holdout attacks. Experiments confirm the theory: finite-audit deviation scales as n^{-0.527}; signed progress resists clip-farming, stream leakage, and noisy-TV curiosity; naive reusable audits are exploitable by black-box scalar feedback, while standard release defenses keep the attack below the 2 Delta_n threshold. Signed compression progress on a sealed audit is an accounting signal of genuine improvement.

</details>


### [163] [Bernstein-Schur Kernels: Random Features by Sketched Modulation and Radial Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11255)
*Taha Bouhsine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Bernstein--Schur核类，提出了一种结合草图与径向特征采样的双重随机特征构造方法，实现了高效、可控的核近似，并提供了严格的理论误差边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决Bernstein--Schur核类在机器学习中因既非平稳又非点积形式，导致现有通用随机特征方法（Bochner采样或多项式草图）无法直接应用的问题。

Method: 提出了双重随机化方案：针对有限特征积（调制部分）使用草图（Sketching），针对完全单调径向因子采用Bernstein--Widder尺度采样结合高斯随机傅里叶特征（RFF）。通过这种组合，在保持计算效率（维度为Dm）的同时规避了传统方法（Bochner采样或多项式草图）的局限性。

Result: 在保持调制部分精确的前提下，推导了无偏性估计；确立了基于内核和调制Gram矩阵特征值及内在维度的算子范数误差界；实验验证了该方法在如Yat核等复杂核函数上的有效性，且在固定特征预算下展现了最优方差表现。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入双重随机化方法，成功为Bernstein--Schur核类提供了一种高效的随机特征映射构造。证明了该估计器具有良好的无偏性、方差性质及算子范数界，且性能依赖于内在维度（Intrinsic Dimension）而非全数据维度。其在处理复杂非平稳核函数及提升核岭回归稳定性方面表现优异。

Abstract: Bernstein--Schur kernels are products of a finite-feature kernel (one with an explicit finite-dimensional feature map) and a completely monotone shift-invariant kernel: nonstationary kernels that fall between the shift-invariant and dot-product templates random features usually exploit, so in general neither Bochner sampling nor polynomial sketching applies to the full kernel directly. We give one random-feature construction for the whole class that \emph{randomizes both factors: it sketches the finite modulation and randomizes the completely monotone radial factor, sampling the latter's one-dimensional Bernstein--Widder scale and then applying Gaussian random Fourier features (whose frequency is still $d$-dimensional). The feature dimension is then $Dm$, set by the sketch size $m$ and the radial-draw count $D$, free of the $O(d^2)$ size of the exact modulation feature. Keeping the modulation \emph{exact} is the analyzable limit ($m\to\infty$): there we prove unbiasedness, an exact variance for the recommended flat estimator, an expected matrix-Bernstein operator-norm bound (with a matching high-probability tail) controlled by the top eigenvalues of the kernel and modulation Gram matrices together with an intrinsic dimension rather than the crude $N\max_{ij}$ entrywise route, and a deterministic relative-spectral kernel-ridge stability result. By conditioning on the sketch, the doubly-randomized estimator inherits the same intrinsic-dimension operator-norm guarantee plus a single additive sketch term, tunable by $m$ independently of $D$. The motivating instance is the biased $yat$-kernel $k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^\top x+b)^2/(\|w-x\|^2+\varepsilon)$, $b\ge0$, whose family span contains the inverse-multiquadric kernel by finite differences in $b$; for it the radial mixture is the IMQ spectral sampler, and one frequency per scale is variance-optimal at a fixed radial-feature budget.

</details>


### [164] [Loss Landscape Diagnosis for Gradient-Based Gray-Scott System Inversion: Disentangling the Roles of PINN Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11258)
*Yan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将直接PDE反向传播与PINN进行对比，解析了PINN成功的原因：神经网络通过隐式编码PDE动力学平滑了病态的损失平面，而非简单地拟合数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨为什么反应-扩散系统的参数反演在直接反向传播PDE时容易失败，并评估在物理信息神经网络（PINN）中，神经网络与物理约束在解决此问题中各自的分工与作用。

Method: 采用直接通过展开（unrolled）的Gray-Scott仿真结构进行反向传播的方法，将该方法作为一种诊断工具，通过可视化损失平面几何形状（平坦高原与尖锐悬崖），分析优化失败的原因，并将其作为PINN的消融实验进行分层解构分析。

Result: 直接反向传播PDE导致优化失败，原因是损失平面存在梯度消失的平坦高原和对应分岔边界的尖锐悬崖；相比之下，PINN通过网络隐式编码全PDE动力学，使得损失函数在参数上呈二次型，从而平滑了损失平面。

Conclusion: 通过对比纯梯度反向传播模拟和PINN，明确了PINN中神经网络的作用在于隐式编码了全PDE动力学从而平滑了损失平面，而神经网络本身无法解决参数空间的病态问题（即无法修复不适定参数子空间）。研究为PINN的设计提供了重要的理论指导。

Abstract: Gradient-based inversion of reaction-diffusion systems is typically approached via surrogate models or physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), while the most direct route, backpropagation through the PDE's structure itself, has largely been avoided. We pursue this direct route as a diagnostic probe, backpropagating a steady-state loss through unrolled Gray-Scott simulation to recover its parameters, with no surrogate or neural-network augmentation. Optimization fails to converge, and plotting the landscape directly locates the failure in its geometry -- flat plateaus with no gradient signal, bounded by sharp cliffs that align with bifurcation boundaries -- a structure that recurs across loss functions and is inherited however the gradients are routed to parameters. Reading this minimal setup as an ablation of PINN, we disentangle each component's role: with the neural network fixed, the residual loss is quadratic in the PDE parameters and yields a smooth landscape, so it alone already avoids the pathology, by implicitly encoding the full PDE dynamics across all initial conditions. The neural network, for its part, cannot repair an ill-posed parameter subspace, and so serves only to complete the observed data -- a division of labor not previously made explicit. These findings carry concrete design implications for PINN-type methods and a broader heuristic on when added dimensions actually help.

</details>


### [165] [CRUMB: Efficient Prior Fitted Network Inference via Distributionally Matched Context Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11473)
*Jamie Heredge,Mattia J. Villani,Pranav Deshpande,Akshay Seshadri,Niraj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对先验拟合网络 (PFN) 在大规模离线数据集上推理的计算瓶颈，CRUMB 引入了一种高效的上下文选择机制，通过基于 MMD 的数据子集化显著降低了计算开销，且保持了高预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先验拟合网络 (PFN) 在处理大规模训练数据集时，由于自注意力机制的二次复杂度，导致推理成本过高，无法直接应用。

Method: 提出 CRUMB 推理框架，包含三个阶段：1) 对测试查询进行聚类；2) 通过贪婪最小化最大平均差异 (MMD) 为每个簇选择分布匹配的训练子集；3) 在缩减后的上下文上执行 PFN 推理。

Result: 在 TabArena 基准测试的三个 PFN 架构上，CRUMB 的性能优于现有的上下文选择策略，并展示了对协变量漂移的良好韧性。

Conclusion: CRUMB 是一种无需重训练的架构无关推理包装器，通过基于 MMD 的子集选择有效解决了 PFN 在大数据集上面临的推理开销问题，同时提升了应对协变量漂移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Prior-fitted networks (PFNs) are a promising class of tabular foundation models that perform in-context learning, whereby the entire labelled training set is supplied as context, and predictions for test queries are produced in a single forward pass. However, the quadratically scaling self-attention mechanism in many PFN architectures makes inference prohibitive for very large training datasets. We propose CRUMB (Clustered Retrieval Using Minimised-MMD Batching), a three-stage inference wrapper that (i) clusters the test queries, (ii) selects a small, distributionally matched training subset for each cluster by greedily minimising the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and (iii) runs exact PFN inference on each reduced-context batch. CRUMB is architecture-agnostic and requires no retraining. On the 51-dataset TabArena benchmark, evaluated across three PFN architectures (TabPFNv2, TabICLv1, TabICLv2), we show that CRUMB outperforms similar state-of-the-art context selection strategies. We also show that CRUMB is resilient to covariate drift, as the MMD-minimisation step naturally helps align the training context distribution to match the current test batch distributions.

</details>


### [166] [PermDoRA -- Understanding Adapter Interference in Language Models: Limits of Parameter-Space Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11262)
*Gowtham Sivaramakrishnan,Sarvesha Kumar Kombaiah Seetha,Kishan Gupta Balaji,Santhosh Baradwaj Vaduvur Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究挑战了“适配器干扰源于参数空间重叠”的假设，证明了几何优化（如正交性）不能有效解决多域适配器干扰问题，干扰的本质更可能源于非线性表示空间的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大型语言模型（LLM）多域访问控制中的适配器组合干扰问题，验证“通过强制参数正交性或方向独立性可减轻干扰”这一假设。

Method: 提出了 DoRA-RBAC，这是一种基于权重分解（Weight-Decomposed）低秩适配的层次化组合框架；对比了传统的欧几里得空间合并方法与基于黎曼几何（Riemannian-inspired）的定向平均合并策略。

Result: 实验表明，虽然单域性能与标准 LoRA 相当，但在多域场景下，几何感知合并策略相比标准平均法并未表现出优势；此外，诊断分析显示，适配器更新的角度对齐和正交性对于预测组合性能的指标效力很弱。

Conclusion: 适配器干扰（adapter interference）主要并非由参数空间的几何性质（如线性更新的重叠或正交性）决定，而是与共享非线性表示空间中的交互作用有关。因此，追求参数正交性的几何合并策略在提升多域性能方面无明显优势。

Abstract: Access control in large language models (LLMs) requires modular mechanisms to enable domain-specific behavior without retraining or cross-domain interference. A common hypothesis is that interference during adapter composition arises from overlap in linear parameter updates, suggesting that enforcing orthogonality or directional independence should improve multi-domain performance. We test this hypothesis using DoRA-RBAC, a hierarchical adapter composition framework based on weight-decomposed low-rank adaptation. We compare conventional Euclidean merging with a geometry-aware Riemannian-inspired merging strategy that approximates the Frechet mean via normalized directional averaging across multiple QA benchmarks (GPQA, PubMedQA, SimpleQA, WMDP) on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B. Our results show that while single-domain performance matches LoRA, geometry-aware merging provides no consistent advantage over standard averaging in multi-domain settings.Diagnostic analysis further reveals that angular alignment and orthogonality of adapter updates are weak predictors of composition performance. These findings suggest that adapter interference is not governed primarily by parameter-space geometry, but is instead consistent with interactions in shared nonlinear representations.

</details>


### [167] [Range-Aware Bayesian Optimization for Discovering Diverse Designs within Target Property Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11574)
*Shengli Jiang,Jason Wu,Charles M. Schroeder,Michael A. Webb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“范围感知”贝叶斯优化框架，通过直接优化落入目标范围的候选方案，高效地实现了材料设计中多样化、规格符合性方案的搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料和产品设计中，实际需求通常落在某个“可接受范围”内，而非追求单一的最优解。此外，为了平衡成本、工艺性或鲁棒性等难以量化的考量，获取多样化的可行解集比寻找单一最优解更具实践价值。现有方法在处理范围约束和多样性获取方面尚有不足。

Method: 提出了一种“范围感知”（range-aware）的贝叶斯优化（BO）框架。该方法通过一种新的采集函数，直接对候选方案满足目标范围的后验概率进行评分，并支持在共享候选空间中同时追求多个不同的规范。

Result: 在基准测试中，该方法比标准BO及现有的目标搜索方法能够恢复更大、更多样的有效设计集合。在聚合物合成反应条件优化和寡聚物光学吸收带设计两个案例研究中，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 基于范围的贝叶斯优化框架为规范驱动的设计提供了一个实用且样本高效的解决方案，特别是在需要设计灵活性和方案多样性的场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: In many materials and product design problems, desirable candidates exhibit properties that fall within an acceptable range rather than achieve a single optimum. Recovering multiple, distinct solutions that satisfy such specifications is also practically valuable, as some candidates may be preferred for reasons of cost, processability, or robustness that are difficult to encode directly in an objective function. Here, we develop a range-aware Bayesian optimization (BO) framework in which the acquisition function directly scores the posterior probability that a candidate satisfies a target range. The framework naturally extends to parallel pursuit of multiple distinct specifications over a shared candidate space. Across benchmark tasks, range-aware acquisition consistently recovers larger and more diverse sets of valid designs than standard BO baselines and recent goal-seeking methods. Its utility is further demonstrated in two practically motivated design case studies involving optimizing reaction conditions for polymer synthesis and sequence-defined oligomer discovery for prescribed optical absorption bands, supported by quantum chemical calculations. These results suggest that range-aware BO can provide a practical and sample-efficient foundation for specification-driven design, particularly when design flexibility and solution diversity are important considerations.

</details>


### [168] [Seeing Before Colliding: Anticipatory Safe RL with Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11266)
*Samuel Tetteh,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统约束强化学习反应式安全机制滞后的问题，本文通过引入预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）作为预测性成本信号，实现了对潜在事故的预警和即时约束优化，在高速竞速任务中显著提升了安全性与性能的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的约束强化学习（Constrained-RL）成本信号多为“反应式”，即在事故发生后才触发。在高速竞速场景中，这种滞后的安全机制无法有效防止不可逆的碰撞。

Method: 提出了 VLM-Safe-RL 框架，包含四个核心组件：(i) 去耦双路径 CLIP 以保留 CMDP 因式分解；(ii) VLM-Lagrange，引入基于 VLM 的每步预测性成本项；(iii) 置信度门控（Confidence Gating），基于 CLIP 边际逻辑噪声模型的贝叶斯最优权重调整；(iv) VLMPPOLag 复合算法。

Result: 在 Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2 基准测试中，VLMPPOLag 是唯一能在保持较高回报（$J_r{\approx}40$）的同时将成本控制在预算内的方案；对比五个基准算法均有不同程度的失效。在 MetaDrive Medium 等任务中验证了其泛化能力，显著降低了灾难发生率。

Conclusion: 这是首个将冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）信号作为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）拉格朗日更新中预测性成本项的工作。VLMPPOLag 在复杂竞速场景中能有效平衡性能与安全性，克服了传统反应式成本方案在高速下的滞后性。

Abstract: The cost signal that constrained-RL algorithms optimize against is almost always reactive: the simulator emits a non-zero cost only after a collision has begun, and the Lagrange multiplier of PPO-Lagrangian grows only after the episode budget has been exceeded. At race speeds, where collisions are instantaneous and irreversible, any safety mechanism that waits for cost to accumulate is structurally too late. We present VLM-Safe-RL, a framework that integrates a frozen vision-language model into the CMDP Lagrangian update as an anticipatory cost term. The framework comprises four contributions: (i) Decoupled Dual-Path CLIP, independent reward/cost paths that respect the CMDP's factorization; (ii) VLM-Lagrange, an augmented multiplier update that incorporates a per-step VLM cost as an anticipatory term; (iii) Confidence Gating, a Bayes-optimal weight derived from a logistic noise model on the CLIP margin; and (iv) VLMPPOLag, the composed algorithm. On Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2, our principal evaluation ($n{=}5$ seeds, $10^{6}$ steps, budget $d_{\text{lim}}{=}25$) VLMPPOLag$+$Conf is the only configuration in our default budget comparison that simultaneously retains substantive return ($J_r{\approx}40$) and holds cost within budget on a majority of seeds; the five constraint-aware baselines (PPOLag, CPO, CPPOPID, CPO-CLG, PPOLag-RND) each fail at least one requirement. The mechanism generalizes to held-out MetaDrive Medium (catastrophe rate $41\%{\to}26\%$, 95\% bootstrap CI $[-26,-5]$\,pp) and shows directionally consistent transfer to Bullet Safety-Gym; we report honestly where it does not (MetaDrive Easy/Hard, Qwen2-VL backbone) and trace the Hard failure to a Lagrangian-regulation pathology rather than the VLM signal itself. To our knowledge, this is the first work to use frozen VLM signals as an anticipatory cost term inside the CMDP Lagrangian update.

</details>


### [169] [Tree-Structured Orthonormal Decomposition of the Aitchison Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11646)
*Daisuke Yamada,Qijun Zhang,Travis Pence,Barbara B. Bendlin,Federico Rey,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对具有分层结构的组合数据，提出 PolyILR 方法，通过对 Aitchison 切空间进行通用的规范正交分解，解决了现有方法在处理复杂树结构时的局限性，有效提升了数据分析的稳定性和解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理具有分层结构（如分类学、系统发育树）的组合数据时，存在忽略结构信息、舍弃 Aitchison 几何特性或仅限于二叉树等局限性。

Method: 提出 PolyILR，构建了一种与任意树拓扑对齐的 Aitchison 切空间的规范正交分解方法，通过在每个内部节点定义加权局部几何结构，并将其提升为全局正交基来实现。

Result: 在微生物组和单细胞基准测试中，PolyILR 产生了稳定且具有解释性的特征，支持多尺度分辨率的推断。

Conclusion: PolyILR 提供了一种通用的、基于树结构的组合数据分析框架，在微生物组和单细胞数据分析中表现出卓越的解释性和稳定性，并揭示了与 Softmax 分类器的理论联系。

Abstract: Compositional data -- vectors encoding relative proportions -- arise across scientific domains, including ecology, geochemistry, and genomics. The features in these data often come with known hierarchical structure (e.g., taxonomies, phylogenies, ontologies), yet existing methods either ignore this structure, discard the intrinsic Aitchison geometry, are designed for binary trees, or yield incomplete coordinate systems. We describe PolyILR, a canonical orthonormal decomposition of the Aitchison tangent space aligned with any tree topology. Our construction defines a weighted local geometry at each internal node capturing full branching structure, then lifts these to a global orthonormal basis where every coordinate corresponds to a specific tree location. On microbiome and single-cell benchmarks, PolyILR yields stable, interpretable features and enables inference at multiscale tree resolution. We also establish a novel theoretical connection to softmax classifiers, suggesting possible applications to probabilistic modeling.

</details>


### [170] [A prior-free blind detection of information leakage from model predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11267)
*Laurence A. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文针对“仅有模型输出”的黑盒场景，提出了一种检测数据泄漏的决策论框架，通过将泄漏分类为三种类型并分别设计检测策略，实现了高效的审计，但也揭示了在模型表现与真实性能相当时，特定类型泄漏无法彻底检测的理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习科学研究中，数据泄漏（data leakage）是导致可重复性问题的核心，但现有的检测工具高度依赖训练代码、额外数据或领域知识，缺少针对“仅持有模型输出”场景的审计方法。

Method: 构建了一个基于决策论（decision-theoretic）的诊断框架，将泄漏检测定义为预测风险/结果规律的函数，结合适当评分规则（proper scoring rules）和决策曲线分析，实现了仅利用模型输出（预测值和目标值）的检测方法。

Result: 定义了泄漏的“三分类”：失准（miscalibrated）、广义校准（broad-calibrated）和确定性（deterministic），并针对每种类型构建了相应的检测器；在UK Biobank上测试表明，检测器能在秒级时间判定泄漏，且识别出具有显著影响的泄漏阈值。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于模型预测结果的“三分类”泄漏诊断框架（校准偏差、广义校准、确定性），并证明了在缺乏外部性能上限的情况下，对于与真实模型性能一致的“广义校准”泄漏是无法检测的。在UK Biobank数据集上的验证表明，该方法可检测出具有实际影响的泄漏，且在低于特定阈值时，泄漏对结论的影响微乎其微。

Abstract: Data leakage -- contamination of a model with information unavailable at baseline -- is the dominant reproducibility failure in machine-learning-based science, yet detection tools require training code, external data, or domain expertise. None operates on the artifact an auditor most often holds: the model's output. We ask what can be decided about leakage from predictions and outcomes alone. We give a decision-theoretic framework in which leakage diagnostics are functionals of the predicted-risk/outcome law, parameterized by a threshold-weighting linked to proper scoring rules and decision-curve analysis. We prove a sharp impossibility: a recalibrated leak matching an honest model's calibration and discrimination is indistinguishable from honest performance by \emph{any} function of the predictions, so the broad class is detectable only against an externally supplied ceiling on achievable discrimination. We then prove what leakage cannot hide: a near-deterministic subgroup -- the signature of a near-label leak -- produces a sustained unit-purity head that no legitimate predictor of a non-deterministic outcome can manufacture, yielding a prior-free test. These results organize leakage into a trichotomy -- miscalibrated, broad-calibrated, and deterministic -- each with a matched detector and failure mode. We validate on UK Biobank using time-windowed comorbidity leakage with known, graded severity, measuring a detection floor of $Δ\cstar \approx 0.007$ on this endpoint, below which residual leakage is undetectable from output and too small to alter conclusions. The numerical floor is cohort- and endpoint-specific; the structural lesson is general: output-only detection fails where residual leakage is indistinguishable from an honestly stronger predictor. The test returns a verdict on a prediction vector in under a second on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [171] [Capacity-Constrained Online Convex Optimization with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11711)
*Alexander Ryabchenko,Idan Attias,Daniel M. Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线学习中因跟踪资源受限导致的反馈丢失问题，通过引入调度策略和加权FTRL算法，在硬容量限制下实现了针对一阶和赌博机反馈的高效延迟OCO优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线学习假设可以跟踪所有待处理轮次，但在现实应用中跟踪资源有限，未被跟踪的反馈会永久丢失。因此，需要研究在硬容量约束（最大同时跟踪C轮）下的延迟OCO问题。

Method: 引入“半先知（semi-clairvoyant）”延迟模型；通过调度器（scheduler）将问题约简为“加权延迟OCO”问题；提出“延迟加权FTRL（Delayed-Weighted FTRL）”及其赌博机（bandit）变体算法。

Result: 提出了第一个针对容量受限OCO的遗憾界；对于一阶反馈，当容量C=Ω(log T)时可恢复标准延迟OCO的速率；对于赌博机反馈，遗憾界受(1+σ_max/C)项调节，确保了即使容量不足时遗憾仍呈亚线性增长。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了容量受限下的延迟在线凸优化（OCO）问题，为有限跟踪资源下的学习提供了理论基础，并证明了在线性损失和强凸损失下实现亚线性遗憾的可行性。

Abstract: Online learning with delayed feedback typically assumes that the learner can track all pending rounds until their feedback arrives. In practice, tracking resources are finite, and feedback from untracked rounds is permanently lost. In this paper, we study delayed online convex optimization (OCO) under a hard capacity constraint, where at most $C$ pending rounds can be tracked at any time. To model delay information, we introduce a semi-clairvoyant model that refines the clairvoyant assumption from prior work: rather than requiring delays to be known at prediction time, the learner observes delay expirations online, consistent with the classical unconstrained delayed setting. Our approach proceeds via a reduction to a novel ``delayed and weighted'' OCO problem, using a scheduler that randomizes tracking decisions and importance-weights the resulting observations. For this base problem, we propose and analyze Delayed-Weighted FTRL and its bandit analogue, establishing regret bounds that explicitly characterize the interaction between time-varying weights and delayed feedback. Combining these base learners with our schedulers yields the first regret guarantees for capacity-constrained OCO under convex and strongly convex losses, for both first-order and bandit feedback. For first-order feedback, capacity $C = Ω(\log T)$ suffices to recover standard delayed OCO rates up to logarithmic factors. For bandit feedback, the regret rates are modulated by powers of $(1 + σ_{\text{max}}/C)$, where $σ_{\text{max}}$ is the maximum number of pending observations at any time. This allows the regret bound to degrade gracefully when $C < σ_{\text{max}}$, while remaining sublinear.

</details>


### [172] [LakeFM: Toward a Foundation Model for Aquatic Ecosystems Using Irregular Multivariate Multi-depth Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11268)
*Abhilash Neog,Sepideh Fatemi,Medha Sawhney,Kazi Sajeed Mehrab,Aanish Pradhan,Bennett J. McAfee,Emma Marchisin,Arka Daw,Robert Ladwig,Cayelan C. Carey,Paul Hanson,Anuj Karpatne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LakeFM 是一款针对湖泊系统的基础模型，通过在大规模异质生态数据集上预训练，攻克了现有算法在非规则采样和多湖泊泛化方面的局限，实现了更准确、物理可解释的湖泊动态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习方法在处理湖泊时间序列数据时，往往依赖规则的时间与深度采样，且难以在具有不同特征变量、深度和观测模式的异质湖泊之间实现有效泛化，无法满足水质监测和生态健康评估的需求。

Method: 提出了一种名为 LakeFM 的湖泊系统基础模型。该模型通过在包含模拟数据和观测数据的大规模生态学数据集上进行预训练，学习湖泊的通用特征表示，从而克服现有模型对规则采样和特定湖泊变量的依赖。

Result: LakeFM 在湖泊动态预测任务中表现出优于现有时间序列基础模型及传统模型的性能，并能生成符合物理规律的预测结果，证明了其在捕捉异质湖泊特征方面的有效性。

Conclusion: LakeFM 证明了预训练基础模型在处理具有异质性和非规则采样特征的湖泊生态时间序列数据方面的有效性，能够实现更具泛化性和物理一致性的精准预测。

Abstract: Understanding and forecasting lake dynamics is critical for monitoring water quality and ecosystem health across lakes and reservoirs. While machine learning methods have been recently applied to ecological time-series data, existing works assume regular sampling in time and depth, and struggle to generalize across lakes with heterogeneous variables, depths, and observation patterns. To address these limitations, we introduce \textsc{LakeFM}, a foundation model for aquatic systems, pre-trained on large-scale ecological datasets comprising both simulated and observed lakes. Through extensive empirical evaluation, we show that \textsc{LakeFM} learns meaningful representations spanning broader lake-level characteristics, and achieves competitive or often superior-forecasting performance compared to existing time-series foundation and non-foundation models, while producing physically plausible predictions consistent with real-world lake dynamics.

</details>


### [173] [Online Shift Detection and Conformal Adaptation for Deployed Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11949)
*Jun Wen Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对安全分类器的在线偏移监测与自适应弃权机制，通过序列统计检测偏移并利用共形预测恢复错误率，解决了对抗攻击下的性能衰退与权重坍塌挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的安全分类器在面对分布偏移或对抗性攻击时，性能会发生衰退，导致实际错误率超出预期；现有监测方案缺乏针对性且在处理模型输出时易出现权重坍塌等问题。

Method: 1. 应用校准的序列统计量进行在线分布偏移检测；2. 结合共形弃权层（Conformal Abstention Layer）动态调整决策阈值；3. 利用加权共形预测（Weighted Conformal Prediction）恢复覆盖率，并在高维空间采用PCA降维处理以解决权重坍塌问题。

Result: 系统以39.5步的平均延迟达到86.6%的有效检测率。在合成偏移、真实时间轴越狱及GCG对抗攻击中均保持有效。通过实验发现，高维嵌入空间会导致权重分配坍塌，而将数据PCA降维至32维可显著恢复Llama Guard等模型的覆盖率（分别提升33pp和21pp）。

Conclusion: 针对已部署的安全分类器，在线分布偏移监测与共形弃权（Conformal Abstention）调整是维持目标错误率的有效方案。研究证明，“一刀切”的监测策略不可行，必须根据分类器和分布偏移的具体类型量身定制监控方案；同时，通过降低特征维度（如PCA）可缓解加权共形预测中的权重坍塌问题。

Abstract: We present an online monitoring system for distributional shift in deployed safety classifiers, using calibrated sequential statistics to detect when a classifier has moved out of distribution. Upon detection, a conformal abstention layer adapts decision thresholds to recover a target error rate epsilon=0.1. In a pre-registered factorial evaluation (4 classifiers x 5 shift conditions x 20 seeds x 2 window sizes, 800 cells), the system achieves 86.6% valid detection (693/800, 95% CI [84.1%, 88.8%]) with mean latency of 39.5 steps. Detection holds across three ground-truth regimes: synthetic onset (86.6%), real temporal jailbreaks (85%, 17/20), and GCG adversarial attacks. Weighted conformal prediction recovers up to 39 pp of lost coverage for DeBERTa (ESS=46/300) but collapses for all other classifiers (ESS~300): logistic density ratio estimation achieves perfect source/target separability in high-dimensional embedding spaces, clipping all importance weights to the floor. DeBERTa shows a gradient from effective correction (paraphrase, ESS=46) to near-total collapse (adversarial suffix, ESS=206). PCA to 32 dimensions breaks the collapse, recovering 33 pp for Llama Guard and 21 pp for ShieldGemma. Variance decomposition reveals classifier (eta^2=0.243), shift type (eta^2=0.237), and their interaction (eta^2=0.185) all contribute substantially to detection latency variance (all p<0.001), indicating per-classifier monitoring profiles are necessary.

</details>


### [174] [DeMix: Debugging Training Data with Mixed Data Error Types by Investigating Influence Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11616)
*Jiale Deng,Yanyan Shen,Xiaogang Shi,Chai Junjun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对训练数据中混合错误难以诊断的问题，DeMix框架通过影响力向量建模和干预式学习，实现了对数据错误类型的高效检测与联合诊断，显著提升了数据质量和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据清洗和归因方法无法有效解决混合错误类型（如标签错误、特征错误、虚假关联）的识别和修复问题，且难以同时进行错误检测与类型诊断，限制了模型性能的优化。

Method: 提出了DeMix框架，将数据调试建模为多标签分类问题。核心方法包括：1) 利用“影响力向量”（influence vectors）捕捉训练样本对验证集预测的影响模式；2) 采用干预式学习策略（intervention-based learning strategy）训练分类器，使其能够从影响力向量中提取特定错误类型的固有特征，从而实现错误样本及其类型的联合诊断。

Result: 在表格数据预测、推荐系统和LLM对齐等11个任务上的实验表明，DeMix在数据调试F1得分上提升了22.61%，修复数据后的模型性能提升了9.32%，优于现有主流方法。

Conclusion: DeMix是一种高效的训练数据调试框架，通过利用影响力向量捕捉不同错误类型的行为模式，实现了对标签错误、特征错误和虚假关联的准确诊断与修复，显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: High-quality training data is essential for the success of machine learning models. However, real-world datasets often contain mixed types of errors arising from systematic flaws in data preparation pipelines, including label errors, feature errors, and spurious correlations. Effective debugging of training data requires both detecting erroneous samples and identifying their specific error types to enable targeted repair, yet existing data cleaning and attribution methods fail to adequately address this dual requirement. In this paper, we propose DeMix, a novel framework that simultaneously diagnoses erroneous samples and their error types. Our key insight is that different error types produce distinct patterns on model behavior. DeMix captures such error-specific patterns by influence vectors that characterize how each training sample affects model predictions across all validation samples. We formulate training data debugging as a multi-label classification problem where a classifier is developed to predict error types directly from influence vectors. We further introduce an intervention-based learning strategy that guides the classifier to capture invariant rationales specific to each error type, ensuring the learned classifier generalizes effectively. Empirical evaluations on 11 tasks across tabular data prediction, recommendation systems, and LLM alignment demonstrate that DeMix significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving a 22.61% improvement in data debugging F1-score and a 9.32% gain in task model performance after data repair. Code is available at: https://github.com/SJTU-DMTai/DeMix.

</details>


### [175] [Quantifying Subliminal Behavioral Transfer Ratios in Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11270)
*Uwe Konig,Hamza Kazmi,Ruizhe Li,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究量化了语言模型蒸馏中的“潜意识学习”现象，证实蒸馏会同步传递教师模型的有害越狱能力，且不同模型在迁移强度和模式上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在蒸馏过程中是否存在“潜意识学习”现象，即：在旨在迁移良性行为的过程中，是否会同步传递教师模型未被明确要求但存在的有害（越狱）特征。

Method: 通过对Llama-2-7B-Chat和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct在不同强度下进行引导（steering），使用纯良性数据对学生模型进行蒸馏，并通过GPT-4对学生模型进行越狱测试（100个JailbreakBench提示词）来量化潜意识行为的迁移比例。

Result: 实验证实该迁移现象是稳健且显著的。具体而言，Llama-2在特定强度下表现出明显的迁移阈值（α > -0.15时，τ在0.25-0.32之间），而Qwen2.5则呈现出持续性的高水平迁移（τ最高达0.61）。

Conclusion: 蒸馏过程中不仅传递了教师模型的良性行为，还会不可避免地“潜意识”传递其潜藏的有害特征（越狱能力）。研究发现，不同模型架构表现出不同的迁移模式：Llama-2存在阈值效应（突发性迁移），而Qwen2.5表现为更为连续且高强度的迁移。

Abstract: Distillation of a language model intended to transfer benign behavior to a student model may also transfer undesirable characteristics, if they are present in the teacher model, a phenomenon known as subliminal learning. While qualitative evidence supports the existence of this effect, its magnitude has not been systematically characterized. This study quantifies subliminal behavioral transfer ratios by steering two teacher models (Llama-2-7B-Chat and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) at varying steering strengths and distilling student models using only benign data. Evaluation on 100 JailbreakBench prompts with GPT-4.1, serving as the evaluator, indicates that transfer is robust but exhibits distinct scaling behaviors. Llama-2 demonstrates a sharp threshold ($τ= {0.25,0.32} \ \text{beyond} \ α= -0.15$), whereas Qwen2.5 displays continuous and higher levels of transfer ($τ$ up to $0.61$).

</details>


### [176] [Efficient Multinomial Logistic Bandit via Frequent Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11968)
*Linzhe He,Yu-Jie Zhang,Sifan Yang,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多项式逻辑带宽（MLogB）在高维下计算复杂的问题，本文提出了基于矩阵草图的 EOFD-MLogB 算法，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持了与原算法相当的遗憾性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MLogB 算法（如 OFUL-MLogB）在高维环境下存在极高的计算复杂度和存储开销（$\mathcal{O}(K^3d^3)$ 时间与 $\mathcal{O}(K^2d^2)$ 空间），限制了其在大规模应用中的推广。

Method: 提出 EOFD-MLogB 算法，利用频繁方向（Frequent Directions）矩阵草图技术处理累积的海森矩阵；将复杂的约束在线牛顿更新简化为一维求根问题，并将谱范数计算简化为 $K \times K$ 特征值计算。

Result: 将单轮时间复杂度优化至 $\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K)^2)$，空间复杂度优化至 $\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K))$（其中 $m \ll d$）；证明了其遗憾界为 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\Delta_T(Kd\ln\Delta_T+m)\sqrt{T})$，在海森矩阵近似低秩时表现接近原算法。

Conclusion: EOFD-MLogB 通过矩阵草图技术成功解决了 MLogB 在高维场景下的计算瓶颈，在保持近似最优遗憾界的同时，显著降低了单轮时间与空间复杂度。

Abstract: This paper studies efficient online algorithms for multinomial logistic bandits (MLogB), where the feedback distribution over $K+1$ outcomes follows a multinomial logistic model of $d$-dimensional action vectors. A representative UCB-type algorithm, OFUL-MLogB, achieves a regret bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(Kd\sqrt{T})$, but still requires $\mathcal{O}(K^3d^3)$ time and $\mathcal{O}(K^2d^2)$ space per round due to parameter estimation and optimistic reward construction, which is prohibitive in high-dimensional settings. To address this limitation, we propose EOFD-MLogB, which integrates frequent directions matrix sketching into OFUL-MLogB. By maintaining a low-rank SVD sketch of the accumulated Hessian, constrained online Newton updates in parameter estimation and $Kd \times K$ spectral-norm computations in the reward bonus are reduced to one-dimensional root-finding tasks and $K \times K$ eigenvalue computations, respectively. This yields dominant per-round time complexity $\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K)^2)$ and space complexity $\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K))$, where $m \ll d$ is the sketch size. We further prove a regret bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(Δ_T(Kd\lnΔ_T+m)\sqrt{T})$, where the sketching error factor $Δ_T$ is controlled by the $m$-truncated spectral tail of the Hessian. Thus, when the Hessian is approximately low-rank, the regret is close to that of OFUL-MLogB. Experiments validate the computational efficiency and competitive performance.

</details>


### [177] [Federated continual learning: A comprehensive survey on lifelong and privacy-preserving learning over distributed and non-stationary data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11272)
*Masoume Gholizade,Fabrizio Ruffini,Pietro Ducange,Francesco Marcelloni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了联邦持续学习（FCL），探讨了其在非平稳数据环境下的长期适应性与隐私保护，并针对现有方法的分类、评估指标及未来面临的挑战进行了系统性总结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习（FL）大多假设数据是静态的，但在实际应用（如医疗、物联网）中，数据流通常是非平稳的，导致模型性能下降且发生灾难性遗忘；而持续学习（CL）虽能解决数据分布演变问题，但缺乏对联邦学习场景下隐私保护、通信限制和客户端异构性的考虑。因此需要 FCL 来弥补这一鸿沟。

Method: 本文是一篇综合性综述，通过形式化定义 FCL 问题，提出多维分类法对现有文献进行梳理，分析传统联邦学习在非稳健环境下的局限性，并探讨了相关应用领域、评估指标及未来挑战。

Result: 明确了 FCL 的定义与特性；建立了分类框架；总结了 FCL 的应用场景、性能评估标准，并指出了该领域目前存在的核心瓶颈与研究方向。

Conclusion: FCL 是实现稳健、适应性强且保护隐私的分布式学习的关键。未来的研究重点应在于解决极端异构性下的时间漂移、设计高效的内存机制以及建立标准化的评估基准。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative and privacy-preserving model training across distributed clients, but most existing FL systems implicitly assume data stationarity. In real-world settings-such as healthcare, industrial IoT (IIOT), cybersecurity, and smart cities-data streams are inherently non-stationary, leading classical FL methods to suffer from performance degradation, instability, and catastrophic forgetting.
  Continual Learning (CL) addresses learning under evolving data distributions but has been largely studied in centralized settings, overlooking key constraints of federated systems, including privacy, limited communication, and client heterogeneity. Federated Continual Learning (FCL) emerges at the intersection of FL and CL, aiming to support lifelong, adaptive, and privacy-aware learning over distributed and non-stationary data.
  This survey provides a comprehensive and systematic overview of FCL. We first present a formal definition of the FCL problem and clarify its distinctive characteristics. We then analyze the limitations of classical FL under non-stationary conditions, highlighting how CL principles support long-term adaptation. To organize the rapidly growing literature, we propose a multi-dimensional taxonomy of FCL approaches. Furthermore, we review representative application domains and data modalities, summarize commonly used evaluation metrics, and discuss experimental perspectives for assessing long-term performance and forgetting. Finally, we highlight key open challenges, including handling extreme heterogeneity under temporal drift, designing scalable and privacy-preserving memory mechanisms, and establishing standardized benchmarks. This survey aims to serve as a reference and a roadmap for advancing FCL toward robust and deployable real-world systems.

</details>


### [178] [What Uncertainties Do We Need for Dynamical Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11988)
*Yusuf Sale,Christopher Bülte,Felix Czaja,Joshua Stiller,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了动态系统中偶然性与认知性不确定性的本质区别，旨在为动态系统建模中的不确定性量化提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机器学习对不确定性的研究多集中于监督学习或生成模型，而针对动态系统（dynamical systems）中不确定性建模的系统性研究较少。因此，本文旨在澄清动态系统中不确定性的种类及其建模需求。

Method: 通过对动态系统中的不确定性来源进行分类与理论分析，从机器学习的角度探讨了这两种不确定性的本质差异及其在不同任务目标下的表现。

Result: 明确了动态系统中不确定性的来源及其分类，为后续针对动态系统的鲁棒性建模和决策任务提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: 该文指出针对动态系统的机器学习建模，必须明确区分并准确表征偶然性（aleatoric）和认知性（epistemic）不确定性，并强调了根据具体任务的应用场景调整不确定性量化目标的重要性。

Abstract: The distinction between aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty has received considerable attention in machine learning research, mainly in the context of supervised learning but also in other settings such as generative modeling. In this paper, we offer a machine learning perspective on uncertainty modeling for dynamical systems, which has been studied much less so far. In particular, we ask: what uncertainties do we need for dynamical systems? We discuss sources of uncertainty, clarify their nature (aleatoric or epistemic), and consider how the objectives of representing and quantifying uncertainty vary across different tasks.

</details>


### [179] [RoVE: Rotary Value Embeddings Attention for Relative Position-dependent Value Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11275)
*Alejandro García-Castellanos,Maurice Weiler,Erik J Bekkers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoVE 通过将 RoPE 的旋转机制扩展到 Value 向量，使注意力机制具备位置感知能力，从而转化为一种更高效的“注意力卷积”，在提升模型长距离建模性能的同时无需引入额外参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出标准的 RoPE 仅使 Attention 分数（Query-Key）具有位置相对性，但 Value 通道保持位置盲态（即 Value 向量不因其与 Query 的距离而改变），从而限制了模型处理长距离依赖的能力。

Method: 提出 RoVE (Rotary Value Embeddings)，这是一种无需新增参数的修改方法，通过在 Value 向量上与 Key 向量同步应用旋转操作，使 Value 路径也具备位置敏感性。

Result: 在 124M 和 354M 参数量的 GPT-2 模型上，RoVE 在小样本学习、OOD 困惑度以及长上下文检索任务中表现优于 RoPE，尤其是在需要长距离聚合的任务上效果显著。

Conclusion: RoVE 将 RoPE 注意力机制转化为“注意力卷积”（attentive convolution），不仅在理论上统一了多个领域的类似操作，且在 124M 和 354M 的 GPT-2 模型上证明了其在小样本学习、分布外泛化及长距离依赖任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) make attention scores position-relative but leave the value pathway position-blind: the message sent by a value token is the same regardless of its distance from the query. We propose RoVE, a parameter-free modification that makes values position-sensitive by rotating them simultaneously with keys, and show that it turns RoPE attention into attentive convolution. This new perspective unifies several independent formulations of the same operation across computer vision, robotics, and modern LLM architectures. Trained 124M and 354M GPT-2 models show consistent empirical gains over RoPE on few-shot in-context learning, out-of-distribution perplexity, and long-context retrieval, with the clearest improvements on tasks that require long-range aggregation.

</details>


### [180] [Least-Action-Guided Diffusion for Physical Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11277)
*Zhongxin Yang,Yuanwei Bin,Xiang I. A. Yang,Shiyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAPG是一种在推理阶段利用最小作用量原理引导扩散模型进行物理一致性修正的框架，解决了生成模型在计算物理中外推时的不一致性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在计算物理领域的外推能力受限，训练分布外的预测常出现物理一致性缺失的问题，且传统的训练时残差惩罚方法往往难以平衡。

Method: 提出了一种名为LAPG的框架，结合了条件评分扩散模型（Conditional Score-based Diffusion Model）与基于作用量的物理引导评分。该方法分为两步：首先由评分模型生成分布内建议，随后利用基于作用量的变分先验，在推理阶段将建议朝外推目标条件进行修正。

Result: 在常微分方程（ODE）和偏微分方程（PDE）系统（如自由落体、弹簧质量系统、点涡系统及翼型流场）的外推测试中，LAPG在降低相位漂移、保持耗散衰减及提升空气动力学预测精度方面均表现出优于物理约束基准模型的效果。

Conclusion: LAPG通过将最小作用量原理转化为推理阶段的修正机制，有效地提升了生成模型在计算物理领域进行外推时的物理一致性和预测精度，优于传统的训练时物理约束方法。

Abstract: Reliable extrapolation remains a central challenge for generative models in computational physics, because models trained over finite ranges of time, parameters, or geometries may produce physically inconsistent predictions outside the training distribution. We introduce a least-action-principle-guided diffusion, LAPG, a framework that promotes physical consistency during inference rather than relying solely on constraints imposed during training. The method combines a conditional score-based diffusion model with an action-derived physical guidance score. In the first stage, the learned score model generates an in-distribution proposal; in the second, an action-based variational prior refines this proposal toward the target out-of-distribution condition. This formulation turns the principle of least action into a differentiable inference-time correction mechanism and provides an alternative to pointwise residual penalties that often require empirical loss balancing.
  We evaluate LAPG on representative ordinary- and partial-differential-equation systems, including free fall, conservative and dissipative spring-mass dynamics, interacting point vortices, and potential flow over parameterized airfoils. In temporal, parameter, and geometric extrapolation tests, LAPG reduces phase drift, preserves dissipative decay, captures vortex motion, and improves the lift response of airfoil flows compared with training-time physics-informed baselines.

</details>


### [181] [FreeBridge: Variational Schrödinger Bridges for Cellular Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11286)
*Xurui Wang,Qin Ren,Jun Ma,Haibin Ling,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高通量成像中无法直接观测细胞连续轨迹的问题，FreeBridge 提出了一种基于薛定谔桥的空间约束模型，通过几何基础正则化实现了更符合生物学逻辑的单细胞扰动路径预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于高通量成像通常涉及细胞化学固定（无法追踪单细胞连续轨迹），单细胞扰动建模沦为仅基于端点分布的随机传输推断。现有的生成模型虽能实现端点对齐，但无法保证中间演化路径的生物合理性，常出现偏离观察到的细胞形态流形的路径。

Method: 提出 FreeBridge 框架，这是一种基于薛定谔桥（Schrödinger Bridge）的单细胞转换建模方法。核心在于：1. 将实例分割后的单细胞表征定义为原子状态，建立固定细胞流形；2. 利用经验潜空间支持正则化（empirical latent support regularization）对随机传输施加几何约束，确保中间过程在已知细胞形态范围内进行。

Result: 在 BBBC021、RxRx1、JUMP 数据集上测试表明，FreeBridge 在保持端点准确性的同时，显著降低了中间态支持度违规（intermediate support violations），证明了其在捕捉生物学轨迹方面的优越性。

Conclusion: 实验证明，FreeBridge 模型不仅在终端保真度和作用机制（MoA）保留方面表现出竞争力，还能有效减少生物学上无法解释的中间轨迹偏移。该研究强调了在单细胞扰动建模中引入几何约束（Geometric Grounding）对于实现具有生物解释性的动态推断至关重要。
