  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 模型日益复杂，但可解释性领域缺乏统一的通用理论，导致现有方法碎片化且缺乏一致的评估标准。研究旨在建立一个演绎框架，用以指导可解释方法的系统性设计。

Method: 引入“标准可解释模型 (SIM)”理论。该方法基于拉格朗日力学，将用户的可解释性需求转化为一组前提条件，进而推导出可解释性对称性 (interpretability symmetries) 与约束条件，构建拉格朗日函数，通过优化目标函数实现模型的可解释性，或直接将其编译为可解释的架构。

Result: 实验表明，SIM 能够有效识别并弥补现有方法（传统、基于概念、机械可解释性）的局限性，指明了新的研究方向，并为可解释性开发接口的设计提供了依据。

Conclusion: SIM 提供了一个严谨的理论框架，将可解释性研究从碎片化的试错法转向基于拉格朗日力学的演绎方法，为理解、设计和评估可解释 AI 提供了统一的科学基础。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence models grow in complexity, interpretability has become an indispensable tool for understanding, debugging, and controlling their computations. However, interpretability lacks general theories to deductively design interpretable methods. This gap between theories and methods results in a fragmented literature and inconsistent evaluation protocols.
  To fill this gap, we introduce the Standard Interpretable Model (SIM), a general theory grounded in Lagrangian mechanics that enables the deductive design of interpretable methods. Specifically, the SIM summarises, in a set of premises, what interpretability is for a target user. From these premises, the SIM systematically derives interpretability symmetries and corresponding constraints, which shape the landscape of a Lagrangian whose minima correspond to optimal interpretable models. To reach the minima, one can either update the parameter values of an opaque model to make it more interpretable or compile constraints into an interpretable architecture.
  We empirically show that the SIM identifies and solves limitations of existing methods (including traditional, concept-based, and mechanistic interpretability), highlights underexplored research directions, and informs the design of core programming interfaces. Beyond being a research method, the deductive nature of the SIM offers pedagogical grounding for interpretability curricula and may shift the scientific community's perspective of a discipline that has long been fragmented.

</details>


### [252] [Harness In-Context Operator Learning with Chain of Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12318)
*Minghui Yang,Ling Guo,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决神经网络算子在分布外（OOD）任务中泛化能力差的问题，本文提出了 CHOP 框架，通过将基础算子串联来增强冻结的 ICON 模型，实现了无需微调即可处理复杂算子任务，并显著提升了泛化性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子及其上下文学习变体（如 ICON）在面对分布外（OOD）算子任务时泛化能力不足，且通常需要微调，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出 CHOP（Chain of Operators）框架，不更新模型参数，而是将显式的基础变换算子与冻结的 ICON 模型串联，构建出“算子链”以完成复杂的 OOD 算子任务。

Result: 在标量守恒律和平均场控制问题上的实验表明，CHOP 降低了直接使用 ICON 的推理误差，提升了模型对不同 PDE 家族的泛化能力，且每个算子环节均具备可解释性。

Conclusion: CHOP 框架通过组合显式算子链，成功赋予了冻结的 ICON 模型处理分布外（OOD）任务的能力，且保持了过程的可解释性与泛化性，展示了算子间共享机制的潜力。

Abstract: Neural operators approximate mappings between function spaces, but often generalize poorly to other operators and usually require fine-tuning or retraining. In-Context Operator Networks (ICON) addresses this issue by prompting the model with numerical context so that the model learns specific operators from prompts and adapt to different operators without fine-tuning. However, ICON may still fail to generalize to out-of-distribution (OOD) operator tasks. Inpired by the success of harness engineering of Large Language models (LLMs), we introduce Chain of Operators (CHOP), a framework that harness a frozen ICON to OOD operator tasks without updating its parameters. Specifically, CHOP constructs a chain of operators consisting of explicit elementary transformations and the frozen ICON. Experiments on a scalar conservation law and a mean-field control problem show that CHOP reduces relative inference error over direct ICON evaluation, while each operator in the chain remains interpretable and in closed form. A chain constructed on one PDE family further generalizes to a different family, indicating shared mechanisms across harness systems.

</details>


### [253] [Claw-SWE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating OpenClaw-style Agent Harnesses on Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12344)
*Mengyu Zheng,Kai Han,Boxun Li,Haiyang Xu,Yuchuan Tian,Wei He,Hang Zhou,Jianyuan Guo,Hailin Hu,Lin Ma,Chao Xu,Guohao Dai,Lixue Xia,Yunchao Wei,Yunhe Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对通用编码智能体在 SWE-bench 上评估困难的问题，本文提出了 Claw-SWE-Bench 基准及适配器协议，实现了跨模型、跨架构的公平编码能力与成本评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 SWE-bench 难以直接评估通用智能体（如 OpenClaw），因为通用智能体缺乏满足基准测试特定契约（Docker、补丁格式、预测输出）的能力。为解决此兼容性与评估不公平问题，需要一个标准化的适配架构。

Method: 引入 Claw-SWE-Bench 基准测试和适配器协议，提供标准化的 prompt、运行时环境、工作空间契约、补丁提取流程及评估器。构建了包含 350 个实例（全集）及 80 个实例（精简版）的测评集，并对多种模型与适配器组合进行了交叉扫描（sweep）实验。

Result: 在 OpenClaw 上，完整的适配器方案使模型 Pass@1 从 19.1% 提升至 73.4%。实验表明，模型选择与智能体架构（harness）选择对性能影响巨大（分别带来 29.4 和 27.4 个百分点的差距），且相同性能的系统在 API 成本上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 本研究强调了在软件工程智能体评估中，适配器（adapter）设计与成本核算与模型能力同等重要。Claw-SWE-Bench 通过统一的协议实现了对不同智能体实现（claws）的公平、可重复的基准测试，揭示了适配器设计对模型性能的显著影响。

Abstract: General-purpose agents such as OpenClaw are increasingly used as autonomous tool users, but their coding ability is difficult to measure under SWE-bench: a generic agent does not by itself satisfy the clean Docker workspace, patch, and prediction contract required for scoring. We introduce Claw-SWE-Bench, a multilingual SWE-bench-style benchmark and adapter protocol that makes heterogeneous agent harnesses, or claws, comparable under fair settings including a fixed prompt, runtime budget, workspace contract, patch extraction procedure, and evaluator. The full benchmark contains 350 GitHub issue-resolution instances across 8 languages and 43 repositories, drawn from SWE-bench-Multilingual and SWE-bench-Verified-Mini after future-commit cleanup. We also release Claw-SWE-Bench Lite for faster validation, which is an 80-instance subset selected by a cost-aware, rank-aware procedure over 17 calibration columns. On the full benchmark, OpenClaw with a minimal direct-diff adapter scores only $19.1\%$ Pass@1, whereas the full adapter reaches $73.4\%$ with the same GLM 5.1 backbone, showing that adapter design is essential for enabling OpenClaw-style harnesses to perform coding tasks effectively. Across an OpenClaw $\times$ nine-model sweep and a five-claw $\times$ two-model sweep, model choice changes Pass@1 by $29.4$ pp and harness choice by $27.4$ pp under fixed models; systems with similar accuracy can differ substantially in total API cost. Claw-SWE-Bench therefore treats harness and cost accounting as first-class axes of SWE-style coding-agent evaluation, providing both a full benchmark and a low-cost reference set for reproducible comparison. The data is available at https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench.

</details>


### [254] [Anatomy of Post-Training: Using Interpretability to Characterize Data and Shape the Learning Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12360)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Sidharth Baskaran,Max Loeffler,Raphael Sarfati,Dhruvil Gala,Ryan Panwar,Santiago Aranguri,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Matthew Kowal,Siddharth Boppana,Daniel Balsam,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决语言模型后训练中奖励函数“不透明”的问题，本文提出一套基于可解释性的数据干预框架，实现了对偏好学习信号的精细化审计与重塑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型后训练过度依赖标量奖励函数，导致训练过程缺乏透明度，易引发模型学会虚假相关性、过度风格化及谄媚（sycophancy）等负面行为。

Method: 提出了一种以数据为中心的后训练流水线，利用可解释性协议（interpretability protocols）从偏好数据中提取出区分“优选”与“劣选”生成的潜在概念，并允许用户通过细粒度反馈对这些概念进行显式干预和审计。

Result: 实验证明，该流水线能够有效诊断偏好数据中的不良信号，缓解偏差迁移，并能增强模型在安全保障和人格化定制方面的表现。

Conclusion: 通过可解释性手段将大模型的后训练从盲目的标量奖励优化，转变为可审计、可雕刻的学习信号优化过程，能有效减少虚假相关性并提升模型行为的可控性。

Abstract: Language-model post-training is the main stage at which model behavior is shaped, yet it still largely involves optimization of scalar rewards that summarize diverse desiderata. This abstraction gives practitioners little visibility into what their data actually teaches models, allowing spurious correlations to be learned by a model and inducing undesirable behaviors such as over-stylization and sycophancy. To address this problem, we ask: can we inspect a preference dataset before optimization and decide, at the level of concepts, which behaviors a model should be allowed to learn? Motivated by this, we introduce a data-centric post-training pipeline that uses interpretability protocols to develop statistical hypotheses for the latent concepts separating preferred from dispreferred generations, making them explicit for fine-grained user feedback. Building on this view, we unify several interpretability-based training protocols as ways of shaping rewards via feature or data interventions. Empirically, we show that our pipeline diagnoses undesirable signals in existing preference data, mitigates off-target learning, and can also help amplify or shape desired properties such as safeguards and model personality. More broadly, our results suggest that interpretability can turn post-training from optimizing opaque proxy rewards into a process of auditing and sculpting the learning signal itself.

</details>


### [255] [Latent World Recovery for Multimodal Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12362)
*Hui Wang,Tianyu Ren,Joseph Butler,Christopher Baker,Karen Rafferty,Simon McDade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态学习中模态缺失的问题，LWR框架通过共享潜空间对齐和自适应模态融合，绕过了缺失数据重建，实现了更稳健的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物科学等实际应用中，数据往往存在部分模态缺失的问题；现有方法（如缺失模态填充）常会导致误差传播，且无法灵活适应不同的模态可用性组合。

Method: 提出Latent World Recovery (LWR) 框架：1. 通过邻域对齐将不同模态的嵌入映射至共享潜空间；2. 采用感知可用的模态融合策略，仅融合训练和推理时观测到的已有模态，直接从观测数据进行表征学习。

Result: 在多组学基准数据集上的实验表明，LWR在癌症表型分类和生存预测等下游任务中表现优异，验证了其处理不完整多模态数据的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了LWR框架在处理缺失模态时的稳健性与有效性，通过避免显式的缺失数据重建，减少了误差传播，为生物医学等领域的多模态预测任务提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: We study multimodal learning under missing modalities, with particular motivation from bioscience applications in which heterogeneous modalities are often only partially available when decisions need to be made. We propose Latent World Recovery (LWR), a framework built on two key ideas: (i) modality-specific embeddings from different modalities are aligned in a shared latent space, and (ii) a unified representation is constructed by fusing only the embeddings of the modalities that are actually available at both training and inference time. Rather than imputing missing modalities or requiring a fixed modality set, LWR treats each modality as a partial perception of an underlying latent state and performs availability-aware representation learning directly from the observed modalities. This combination of neighbor-based latent alignment and availability-aware modality fusion enables robust multimodal prediction under partial observation, while avoiding error propagation from explicit reconstruction of missing modalities. We evaluate the proposed framework on real-world incomplete multi-omics benchmarks and demonstrate that it provides an effective approach to downstream tasks such as cancer phenotype classification and survival prediction.

</details>


### [256] [On Subquadratic Architectures: From Applications to Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12364)
*Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,Levente Zólyomi,David Stap,Pieter-Jan Hoedt,Niklas Schmidinger,Lukas Hauzenberger,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对比了三种领先的次线性架构，发现 xLSTM 通过更鲁棒的门控状态跟踪机制，在代码和时间序列任务中表现优于 Mamba-2 和 Gated DeltaNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 因注意力机制的二次计算成本限制了其扩展性，而现有的次线性架构多种多样，尚不清楚哪种设计在处理复杂依赖关系的任务中效果最优。

Method: 通过对比实验方法，系统比较了 xLSTM、Mamba-2 和 Gated DeltaNet 三种主流次线性（subquadratic）架构，应用场景包括代码模型预训练、模型蒸馏及时间序列基础模型预训练。同时提供了统一的公式化分析，并使用受控的合成长度泛化任务进行验证。

Result: 在代码模型任务和时间序列预测任务中，xLSTM 表现出整体最强的性能，尤其在状态跟踪和记忆动态调整方面优于同类模型。

Conclusion: xLSTM 在处理复杂序列任务时展现出性能优势，核心原因在于其门控机制实现了更灵活、稳定的状态跟踪和记忆累积。

Abstract: Transformers dominate modern sequence modeling, but their quadratic attention incurs substantial computational cost. Subquadratic architectures offer a scalable alternative. However, it remains unclear which designs yield the most effective sequence models. We compare three leading approaches: xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet. We evaluate these models on tasks with complex dependencies: (1) code-model pre-training, (2) distillation of code models from large language models, and (3) pre-training of time-series foundation models. Across these settings, xLSTM delivers the strongest overall performance. To explain xLSTM's advantage, we present a unified formulation and analyze the underlying architectural mechanisms, focusing on state tracking and memory dynamics. Our results show that xLSTM enables more flexible and stable memory correction via its gating scheme. We corroborate these findings on controlled synthetic length-generalization tasks. Overall, our findings indicate that xLSTM's gains on complex tasks stem from robust state tracking and accumulation.

</details>


### [257] [Breaking Entropy Bounds: Accelerating RL Training via MTP with Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12370)
*Yucheng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Yang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Yuhao Shen,Fan Zhou,Rui Men,Jianwei Zhang,An Yang,Bowen Yu,Bo Zheng,Fei Huang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 RL 训练中 MTP 受模型熵波动导致的接受率退化问题，Bebop 通过引入概率拒绝采样和端到端 TV Loss 优化，实现了高效、稳定的 MTP 推测解码，不仅提升了推理吞吐量，还通过预训练策略消除了对在线 MTP 更新的依赖，显著加速了异步 RL 训练流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习（RL）训练 LLM 的流水线中，滚动（rollout）阶段是主要瓶颈。虽然多 Token 预测（MTP）可以通过推测解码加速滚动，但现有的 MTP 在 RL 训练期间因熵波动导致接受率显著下降，从而限制了加速效果。

Method: 1. 理论分析：揭示了 MTP 接受率与 RL 阶段模型熵波动之间的负线性关系；2. 采样改进：采用概率拒绝采样替代贪婪采样以缓解熵波动干扰；3. 目标函数创新：提出端到端 Total Variation (TV) Loss，直接优化多步拒绝采样的接受率；4. 训练策略：验证了“预训练 MTP + 冻结策略”在 RL 全过程中的有效性，消除了在线更新成本。

Result: 1. MTP 接受率提升约 10%，最高可达 95%；2. 推理吞吐量提升最高达 25%；3. 在 Qwen 系列模型的异步 RL 训练中，实现了最高 1.8 倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: Bebop 提出了一种针对 LLM 后训练阶段多 Token 预测（MTP）的系统性改进方案。通过结合概率拒绝采样策略和创新的端到端 TV Loss，成功解耦了 RL 中的熵波动对 MTP 接受率的影响，实现了在无需在线 MTP 更新的情况下，显著提升推理吞吐量和 RL 训练速度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component in modern large language models, yet the rollout stage remains the key bottleneck in RL training pipelines. Although Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) offers a natural solution to accelerate rollouts through speculative decoding, many studies have observed that MTP acceptance rates degrade significantly during RL training, leading to limited speedup performance. To address this bottleneck, we present Bebop, a systematic study of MTP in LLM post-training, and offer practical recipes to integrate MTP into large-scale RL pipelines. First, we reveal that the MTP acceptance rate is fundamentally bounded by the fluctuation of model entropy, which demonstrates a clear negative linear relationship with the rise of entropy in the RL stage. Second, we show that probabilistic rejection sampling largely alleviates the disturbance introduced by entropy in RL compared to greedy draft sampling. We further identify that the conventional MTP training objectives (cross-entropy or KL) are suboptimal in such settings, and therefore we propose a novel end-to-end TV loss that directly optimizes multi-step rejection sampling acceptance rate, yielding ~10% acceptance rate improvements, achieving up to 95% acceptance rates and up to 25% extra inference throughput gains across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks. Third, we test various online MTP training strategies during RL and show that pre-RL MTP training with e2e TV loss and rejection sampling achieves a consistent acceptance rate and speedup throughout the entire RL, eliminating the need for costly online MTP updating. We provide extensive experiments and analysis that validate our findings. Experimental results show our method achieves up to 1.8x end-to-end acceleration in async RL training of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, and Qwen3.7 models.

</details>


### [258] [APPO: Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12384)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Yong Wang,Yuxiang Ji,Shidong Yang,Guanhua Chen,Pengkun Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有Agent RL在决策信用分配上过于粗糙的问题，本文提出了APPO框架，通过细粒度的分支定位与程序级优势分配，显著提升了代理在多轮任务中的决策能力与解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理RL方法多依赖于工具调用边界等粗粒度启发式单位，难以精确识别影响最终任务产出的 intermediate decisions（中间决策），导致信用分配不准及探索效率低下。

Method: 提出了“Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)”架构。包含两个核心改进：(1) Branching Score机制：结合Token不确定性与策略引起的后续增益，精准定位决策分支点；(2) 程序级优势缩放（Procedure-level Advantage Scaling）：实现细粒度的跨分支rollout信用分配。

Result: 在13个基准测试中，相较于主流代理RL基线模型，平均性能提升近4个点；在保持高效工具调用的同时，显著增强了代理行为的可解释性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将RL的分支与信用分配从粗粒度（工具调用边界）细化到细粒度（序列决策点）是提升代理效能的关键。APPO框架通过Branching Score与程序级优势缩放，有效提升了代理在复杂任务中的推理与决策能力，且具备良好的可解释性。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) have substantially improved the multi-turn tool-use capabilities of large language model agents. However, most existing methods assign credit over coarse heuristic units, such as tool-call boundaries or fixed workflows, making it difficult to identify which intermediate decisions influence downstream outcomes. In this work, we study agentic RL from two perspectives: \textit{where to branch and how to assign credit after branching}. Our pilot analysis shows that influential decision points are broadly distributed throughout the generated sequence rather than concentrated at tool calls, while token entropy alone does not reliably reflect their impact on final outcomes. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)}, which shifts branching and credit assignment from coarse interaction units to fine-grained decision points in the sequence. APPO selects branching locations using a Branching Score that combines token uncertainty with policy-induced likelihood gains of subsequent continuations, enabling more targeted exploration while filtering out spurious high-entropy positions. It further introduces procedure-level advantage scaling to better distribute credit across branched rollouts. Experiments on 13 benchmarks show that APPO consistently improves strong agentic RL baselines by nearly 4 points, while keeping efficient tool-calls and maintaining behavior interpretability.

</details>


### [259] [ATLAS: Active Theory Learning for Automated Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12386)
*Noémi Éltető,Nathaniel D. Daw,Kimberly L. Stachenfeld,Kevin J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ATLAS 是一个用于自动化科学发现的主动学习框架，通过生成稀疏机理模型并循环设计最优区分性实验，在认知科学任务中实现了 5-10 倍的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学理解依赖于构建机理模型，但如何提出最优实验以获取最大信息量是认知科学及相关领域面临的挑战。研究旨在通过自动化手段提高科学发现效率，特别是从行为数据中高效恢复和学习可解释的行为模型。

Method: 提出 ATLAS (Active Theory Learning for Automated Science) 主动学习框架，核心包括：1. 使用解耦的循环神经网络（Disentangled RNNs）作为稀疏神经网络集合来实例化各种机理假设；2. 开发迭代式主动学习算法，通过设计最优实验来区分和优化这些假设。

Result: 在强化学习（Bandit任务）实验中，ATLAS 在行为、结构和计算相似性等指标上取得了 5-10 倍的样本效率提升，其设计的实验序列不仅多样且具有针对性，且表现超过或对标了文献中专家设计的实验。

Conclusion: ATLAS 框架展示了其在自动化识别、构建和验证解释性强（可解释）的机械论模型方面的巨大潜力，不仅在样本效率上显著优于随机实验，且其设计的实验质量可与领域专家的设计媲美，为认知科学及其他科学发现领域提供了强大的工具。

Abstract: Advancing scientific understanding through mechanistic modeling requires posing the right experimental questions to yield maximally informative data. To automate this pursuit within cognitive science, we introduce ATLAS (Active Theory Learning for Automated Science), an active learning framework for the data-driven discovery of interpretable behavioral models. ATLAS iterates between generating mechanistic hypotheses--instantiated as a diverse ensemble of sparse neural networks (Disentangled RNNs)--and designing experiments that optimally distinguish between them. We test this approach on the problem of recovering reinforcement learning agents from their behavior in bandit tasks. ATLAS designs varied sequences of qualitatively novel experiments with temporal structure tailored to underlying agent characteristics. The models trained on these experiments are evaluated against a comprehensive set of metrics for mechanistic modeling that capture behavioral, structural, and computational similarity. ATLAS achieves a 5-10x improvement in sample efficiency across all metrics compared to random experimentation, and its performance is further validated against expert-designed experiments derived from literature. These in silico results showcase ATLAS's potential to accelerate human-interpretable insights in cognitive science and other domains where scientific inquiry relies on discovering mechanistic models.

</details>


### [260] [Redesign Mixture-of-Experts Routers with Manifold Power Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12397)
*Songhao Wu,Ang Lv,Ruobing Xie,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 MPI（流形幂迭代）的路由器设计原则，通过强制将路由器权重与专家矩阵的主奇异方向对齐，提升了混合专家模型（MoE）的路由选择能力与整体模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型中路由器的作用是衡量输入Token与专家之间的亲和度，但现有的路由设计缺乏明确的数学原则来确保路由向量能够有效表征专家矩阵的核心特征。

Method: 提出了“流形幂迭代”（Manifold Power Iteration, MPI），即“先幂迭代再归约”的范式。通过幂迭代步骤更新路由权重，并施加范数约束进行归约，确保数值的效率与稳定性，从而引导路由向量收敛至专家权重的主奇异方向。

Result: 理论上证明了 MPI 能够驱动路由行向量收敛到其对应专家矩阵的主奇异方向；在 1B 到 11B 参数规模的 MoE 模型预训练实验中，验证了该对齐方法能显著提升模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过流形幂迭代（MPI）实现的“幂-归约”策略成功地实现了路由向量与专家矩阵主奇异方向的对齐，证实了该对齐原则能有效提升MoE模型的性能。

Abstract: Router is the cornerstone component to the Mixture-of-Experts models. Serving as expert proxies, the rows of the router matrix compute their similarity to the MoE inputs to determine which subset of experts is activated. Ideally, each router row is designed to encode the expert matrix into this representative vector, such that its dot-product with token can better reflect token-expert affinity. However, there exists no design principles to enforce this condensation. In this paper, we propose to align each router row with the principal singular direction of the associated expert, as this direction provides the most expressive mathematical description of a matrix. Based on this principle, we propose a router redesign with Manifold Power Iteration (MPI). Specifically, it introduces a "Power-then-Retract" paradigm, where a power iteration step is performed on the router weights, followed by a retraction to impose a norm constraint to ensure both efficiency and stability. Theoretically, we show that MPI drives router rows to converge toward the principal singular directions of associated experts. Empirically, we pretrain MoE model across scales from 1B to 11B parameters to confirm that this alignment facilitates more effective MoE models.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [A PubMed-Scale Dataset of Structured Biomedical Abstracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11361)
*Chia-Hsuan Chang,Haerin Song,Brian Ondov,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文通过大语言模型流水线将 PubMed 中 1720 万条非结构化摘要转换为结构化格式，并合并已有的 590 万条结构化数据，发布了包含 2320 万条记录的“Structured PubMed”大规模语料库，以赋能生物医学信息挖掘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PubMed 中大量摘要缺乏结构化格式，阻碍了生物医学文本的有效挖掘、检索和知识合成。

Method: 构建了一个包含两个子集的数据集：1）从原始 XML 文件中解析出的 590 万条作者编写的结构化摘要；2）使用大语言模型（LLM）通过逐字提取流水线对 1720 万条非结构化摘要进行自动标注的子集。所有数据统一映射至五部分标准架构。

Result: 构建了涵盖 PubMed 数据库中 2320 万条研究论文记录的结构化语料库，实现了 PubMed 范围内的大规模摘要结构化处理。

Conclusion: Structured PubMed 通过提供大规模、标准化的结构化摘要数据，解决了生物医学文献处理中的信息缺失瓶颈，为下游信息提取、知识合成和文本挖掘任务奠定了基础。

Abstract: Structured abstracts are important for biomedical literature processing, by facilitating information retrieval, text mining, and knowledge synthesis. However, a vast portion of abstracts indexed in PubMed remain unstructured, presenting a significant bottleneck for downstream text-processing workflows and applications. To resolve this limitation, we introduce Structured PubMed, a comprehensive corpus of section-labeled biomedical abstracts compiled from the complete PubMed database, encompassing over 23.2 million research-article records. The corpus is divided into two distinct subsets: a collection of 5.9 million author-structured abstracts parsed from official XML files, and an automatically labeled collection of 17.2 million originally unstructured abstracts structured via a verbatim-extraction Large Language Model pipeline. Every record is harmonized under a unified five-section schema and mapped to its original PubMed identifier, publication type, and publication date. This dataset can be utilized to train sentence-classification models, benchmark text-segmentation architectures, and perform large-scale, section-specific information extraction at an unprecedented PubMed-wide scale.

</details>


### [262] [Factions Within, Uncertain Across: Within-Document Reader Sub-Groups in Social Highlighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11613)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现，读者在单一文档内会形成显著的关注子群（派系化），但这种群体划分特征在跨文档对比中表现出极低的可重复性，目前无法断定其是否构成稳定的读者人格特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨当大量用户对同一文档进行高亮标注时，这种群体行为究竟是单一的一致性，还是由不同关注点的读者子群构成的？这种结构性特征是源于文档本身的属性，还是源于读者的内在倾向？

Method: 采用“保持边际的掷球零假设（margin-preserving curveball null）”进行分析。实验1通过分析共读平台的标注数据，考察文档内读者子群的形成；实验2通过跨文档的分半重现性检验，评估读者分组行为的稳定性与可重复性。

Result: 1. 大多数文档内存在极强的读者子群（z=+6.3），大部分一致性超过了单纯的显著性或文档热度预测；2. 约40%的一致性可由文档区域吸引力解释，其余60%归因于细粒度的阅读倾向；3. 跨文档的分组重现性极低，即使在数据最充分的子集中也未能观测到明确的稳定性迹象。

Conclusion: 文档内的“群众”是派系化的（即存在读者子群），但这种派系倾向是否作为一种稳定的读者特征跨文档持续存在，目前尚无法确定。研究结果与从“情境化分组”到“弱中度稳定特征”之间的任何可能性兼容。

Abstract: When many people highlight the same document, is the crowd a single consensus, or is it internally structured into reader sub-groups that mark different things -- and is that structure a stable property of a reader or of the document? Building on prior work showing an individual's within-document highlighting signal is a whisper while individuality lives in selection, we ask the group-level question on a co-readership platform using a margin-preserving curveball null. Experiment 1: within a document, readers form strong sub-groups -- pairs agree far beyond what shared salience, mark density, and sentence popularity predict (nearest-neighbour agreement z=+6.3, significant in 88% of documents). Under an eight-block region-preserving null, shared engagement with the same coarse regions of the document accounts for about 40% of this excess; the majority survives as finer reader-specific agreement (z=+3.6, 77% significant). So the within-document crowd is, in a descriptive sense, factional. Experiment 2: is that grouping a stable reader trait? Here we are honest about power. The cross-document split-half reproducibility of a pair's agreement is near zero pooled (+0.078 and 0.000 in two separately drawn samples), and a power calibration shows the test is informative only for pairs that co-read many documents. In the only informative high-overlap subset (k>=4), point estimates are positive but small-sample, imprecise across the separately drawn samples, never significant, and attenuate under the region-preserving null. We therefore leave cross-document stability unresolved: the data is consistent with anything from situational grouping to a weak-to-moderate stable reader trait. The crowd is factional within a document; whether its factions follow the reader across documents is, honestly, beyond our reach.

</details>


### [263] [The Long Tail, Not the Front Page: Cold-Start Prediction of Crowd Highlight Salience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11654)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何在文档发布初期预测社交高亮。研究表明，通过训练特定模型学习群体阅读标注，可以打破仅仅依赖“前导位置（Lead-bias）”的预测局限，为冷启动环境下的内容显著性预测提供了可行的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的社交高亮通常依赖于大量读者沉淀的数据，无法直接应用于新发布的内容。本文试图探究是否可以在高亮数据积累前，仅凭文本内容准确预测文档的“群体显著性”（即哪些段落会被读者高亮），从而解决文档冷启动阶段的推荐预测问题。

Method: 研究采用了预注册的模型阶梯（pre-registered ladder of models）和文档聚类自助抽样（by-document cluster bootstrap）方法。核心模型是一个在句子嵌入（sentence embeddings）以及位置/上下文特征基础上构建的逻辑回归排序器（logistic ranker），并与无监督提取基准（质心法、LexRank）及位置基准进行了对比与消融实验。

Result: 训练模型在平均精度（Average Precision）上超越了“前导（Lead）”基准线（+0.044），在Precision@3指标上提升了55%（从0.25至0.39）。该模型在69%的文档上表现优于位置基准，且证明其性能源于对真实读者标注的学习，而非通用的无监督特征。此外，研究发现该方法的优势在低热度文档上更为明显。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过在标注数据上进行训练，可以显著超越简单的“前导（Lead/位置）基准线”来预测文档的群体显著性（crowd salience）。虽然该模型与人类表现仍有差距，但它为解决社交高亮在内容冷启动阶段的预测问题提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: A social highlighter's most useful signal -- which passages a crowd of readers marks -- exists only for documents people have already read. Can the aggregate crowd salience of a document be predicted from its text before its marks accumulate? Prior work on this data found that zero-shot language models recover highlight locations worse than a trivial lead (position) baseline, so we ask whether a model trained on the highlight corpus can beat that baseline. Using a pre-registered ladder of models and a by-document cluster bootstrap, we find a small but robust edge: a logistic ranker over sentence embeddings and positional/contextual features beats the lead baseline by +0.044 average precision (95% CI [+0.029, +0.058]; clears a pre-registered margin delta=0.03 in 97% of resamples, and stable across pipeline re-runs). Two unsupervised extractive baselines (centroid, LexRank-style centrality) lose to lead, and the trained model beats them by +0.108, so the edge is not recovered by generic unsupervised proxies -- it reflects learning from real reader marks. In product terms, precision@3 rises from 0.25 to 0.39 (+55% relative) and the model beats lead on 69% of documents. An ablation attributes the edge to the raw embedding (+0.014) and training augmentation (+0.010), each with a positive CI. The edge is not a temporal-generalization failure, and we find no evidence that content drift or near-duplicate leakage explains it. A standardized regression shows the advantage is governed mainly by document popularity (lower popularity, larger edge) and by label reliability. It nearly vanishes only on the most popular content; there it is the lead baseline that strengthens, not the model that weakens. Because our evaluation conditions on documents that eventually accumulated readers, these results are a retrospective cold-start simulation.

</details>


### [264] [CompRank: Efficient LLM Reranking via Token-Level Compression and Decoding-Free Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11700)
*Xuan Lu,Haohang Huang,Yingqi Fan,Junlong Tong,Yuxuan Zhang,Ping Nie,Rui Meng,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CompRank 是一种高效的 LLM 重排序框架，通过令牌压缩和注意力评分对齐，在大幅降低计算开销（减少近 90% 令牌）的同时，保持了与全量注意力模型相当的重排序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 重排序器计算成本过高，限制了其在处理长候选文档列表时的应用能力。

Method: 提出 CompRank 框架，包含三个核心机制：1. 解耦文档向量表示以实现状态复用；2. 实施分段令牌压缩（segment-wise token compression）以降低计算负载；3. 引入 CopyNet 风格的训练目标，直接对齐注意力评分与监督信号。

Result: 在 7 个 BEIR 数据集上，仅保留 10.2% 的文档令牌即可达到原始模型 98.7% 的 NDCG@10 性能；在 TREC-COVID 上，实现了 4.9 倍至 9.5 倍的端到端加速，且在处理超长候选列表时表现出良好的稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过令牌级压缩和无需解码的注意力评分，CompRank 为大规模 LLM 重排序提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，能够在显著降低计算成本的同时保持极高性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) rerankers have become an important component of modern retrieval and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines, but their high computational cost limits their applicability to long candidate lists. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CompRank}, a token-efficient reranking framework that reduces redundant computation by aligning reranker design with the sparsity of ranking signals. CompRank decouples document representations from candidate order and query context, enabling reusable document-side states; applies segment-wise token compression to reduce query--document interaction cost; and introduces a CopyNet-style objective that directly aligns attention-based document scoring with training supervision. Experiments on seven BEIR datasets show that CompRank achieves strong reranking performance while retaining only 10.2\% of document tokens, reaching an average NDCG@10 of 39.2 compared with 39.7 under full-token attention. Further scaling experiments on TREC-COVID show that CompRank remains stable when evaluated on candidate lists of up to 500 documents after training on 30-document lists, while achieving $4.9\times$--$9.5\times$ end-to-end speedup over generation-based listwise reranking and approximately $1.3\times$ speedup over the full-token CompRank variant. These results suggest that token-level compression and decoding-free attention scoring provide an effective path toward scalable LLM-based reranking.

</details>


### [265] [FAST-MEL: A Fast, Accurate, and Storage Efficient Solution for Multimodal Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11749)
*Derrien Thomas,Laurent Amsaleg,Pascale Sébillot*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 FAST-MEL，通过一种高效的紧凑向量表示，在多模态实体链接任务中实现了高准确性、超高计算速度和极低存储占用的三赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态实体链接（MEL）系统难以在保持高链接准确性的同时，兼顾大规模场景下的计算效率和存储效率。

Method: 提出了 FAST-MEL 方法，该方法利用一种新颖且紧凑的固定大小向量化表示来编码实体或提及的文本和视觉信息。

Result: FAST-MEL 与当前最先进系统保持相当的链接准确度，但在处理速度上提高了三个数量级，在存储需求上降低了一个数量级。

Conclusion: FAST-MEL 成功实现了多模态实体链接任务中准确性、计算效率和存储效率的平衡，提供了一种在实际大规模生产环境中极具竞争力的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal entity linking (MEL) is the task that consists of matching textual and visual mentions of entities in unstructured data to their corresponding entities in a knowledge base (KB). To be effective in large-scale practical settings, MEL systems must meet three objectives: high linking accuracy, computational efficiency, and storage efficiency, i.e., a compact yet efficient index of the KB. In this paper, we highlight that state-of-the-art systems fail to simultaneously satisfy these 3 requirements. To meet this three-fold objective, we propose FAST-MEL, a lightweight encoder-based MEL solution that relies on a novel and compact fixed-size vectorized representation of both the textual and visual information of each entity or mention. It matches the accuracy of the best systems but performs three orders of magnitude faster. It also consumes one order of magnitude less storage than the fastest systems.

</details>


### [266] [What Limits Does Quantization Place on Dense Top-$k$ Retrieval? A Theoretical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11780)
*Koki Okajima,Tsukasa Yoshida*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有限精度向量嵌入中，实现完美top-k检索所需的维度必须随语料库大小对数增长，挑战了“维度与语料库大小无关”的理论假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为在无限精度下top-k检索所需的向量维度与语料库大小无关（d=O(k)），但该结论在实际应用中受限于有限的计算精度，研究旨在揭示精度受限对向量嵌入维度的具体影响。

Method: 利用信息论与量化模型，证明在有限比特精度（B）下，完美实现top-k检索的维度下界为 Bd = Ω(k ln N)；并针对l2归一化均匀标量量化模型分析了精度阈值 B* = O(ln ln N)。

Result: 证明了在有限精度下，维度必须随N对数级增长。确定了量化精度的阈值，低于该阈值时无法通过增加维度实现完美检索，并刻画了可行精度与维度的区间。

Conclusion: 在实际向量数据库和密集检索系统中，当使用量化技术时，通过有限精度表示实现完美检索所需的维度（d）和精度（B）必须随语料库大小（N）增长。

Abstract: We establish conditions for embedding a corpus of $N$ documents as $d$-dimensional vectors such that every $k$-subset $S \subseteq [N]$ is realizable as a result of top-$k$ retrieval by some query vector. Recent work shows that $d = O(k)$ suffices for such embeddings to exist in $\mathbb{R}^d$, independently of $N$. We theoretically prove that this corpus-independent bound is specific to infinite precision. With $B$ bits per coordinate, perfect top-$k$ retrieval requires $Bd = Ω(k \ln N)$; thus, at any fixed precision, the dimension must grow at least logarithmically with $N$. Specializing to a $\ell_2$-normalized $B$-bit uniform scalar quantization model, we also identify a threshold on the precision $B^{*} = O(\ln \ln N)$ below which no dimension suffices, together with two further regimes that bound the feasible $(B, d)$ pairs. Our result implies that in practical vector databases and dense retrieval systems where quantization is standard, the embedding dimension and possibly the precision must grow with the corpus size.

</details>


### [267] [CORE-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Code Retrieval in the Era of Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11864)
*Fuwei Zhang,Yanzhao Zhang,Mingxin Li,Dingkun Long,Lexiang Hu,Pengjun Xie,Zhao Zhang,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对智能体编程中真实仓库环境下的复杂检索需求，本文提出了CORE-Bench基准，揭示了传统检索方法的局限性，并验证了监督微调对提升智能体代码检索能力的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码检索基准主要关注孤立的代码片段匹配（如docstring到函数），无法满足智能体在实际开发中进行导航、根据需求定位文件、获取辅助上下文以及过滤干扰项的需求。

Method: 构建了一个包含三种检索任务（代码理解、问题-编辑定位、广义上下文检索）的基准测试集CORE-Bench，利用来自SWE-bench的数据集，包含超过18万个查询和10.6万个关联标签，并评估了现有嵌入模型的性能及微调效果。

Result: 实验发现，传统代码搜索模型在智能体编码场景下的检索性能出现显著下降，但通过简单的监督微调，现有模型在针对性场景下的检索质量有大幅提升。

Conclusion: CORE-Bench证明了在智能体编码场景下，传统代码检索方法存在显著性能差距，通过简单的监督微调可有效提升检索模型的适应性，为未来的智能体代码检索研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Code retrieval is becoming central to coding agents, but agentic coding requires more than matching a natural-language query to an isolated snippet. Given a user request, a coding agent needs to navigate a concrete repository state, locate relevant files and functions, gather supporting context, and filter similar in-repository distractors. Existing code retrieval benchmarks mainly evaluate docstring-to-function or snippet-level matching, thereby missing this requirement-driven repository search problem. To address this gap, we introduce CORE-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for code retrieval in the era of agentic coding. CORE-Bench evaluates code retrieval ability at three levels: code understanding, issue-to-edit localization, and broader context retrieval. Built from curated code-search tasks and SWE-bench-series instances, CORE-Bench contains over 180K queries and 106K broader-context relevance labels. Experiments with representative embedding models show a sharp drop from traditional code search to code retrieval in agentic coding settings. Simple supervised fine-tuning of existing embedding models significantly improves performance in this setting, suggesting substantial room for further progress.

</details>


### [268] [Tail-Aware Adaptive-k: Query-Adaptive Context Selection for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11907)
*Ziyu Song,Jiaming Fang,Kuangyu Li,Tuo Xia,Chuanpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 RAG 系统中固定 Top-K 检索的局限性，TAA-k 通过结合拐点检测与局部 EVT 检验，实现了一种高效、稳定且自适应的检索结果截断机制，在显著降低计算成本的同时达到了近预言机的检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RAG 系统依赖固定的 Top-K 检索，无法适应检索结果分布的动态变化；而传统的基于极值理论（EVT）的方法在全局范围应用时计算开销巨大且易出现统计不稳定。

Method: 提出一种“粗到细”的训练无关框架 TAA-k：首先利用几何特征（拐点检测）识别候选区域，随后在该局部窗口内应用基于极值理论（EVT）的拟合优度检验，以精准判定噪声区间的起始点。

Result: 计算复杂度由 O(N^2M) 优化至 O(sqrt{N log N}*M)；在数据集（WebQuestions, 2WikiMultiHopQA, MuSiQue）上表现优异，F1 分数与预言机上限仅差距 2-3%，且计算效率远超全局 EVT 方法。

Conclusion: TAA-k 通过局部化策略成功将 EVT 的计算复杂度大幅降低，同时保持了统计稳健性。它能够实现自适应的检索截断，在多个数据集上达到接近预言机（Near-oracle）的检索性能，且具备良好的模型跨度和维度稳健性。

Abstract: Adaptive context selection is critical for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems, as fixed Top-K retrieval fails under query-dependent and heavy-tailed similarity distributions. While Extreme Value Theory (EVT) offers a principled framework for adaptive truncation, existing approaches apply EVT globally across the entire ranked list, incurring prohibitive computational costs and statistical instability. We propose Tail-Aware Adaptive-k(TAA-k), a training-free framework that operationalizes EVT through a localized validation strategy. The key insight is that ranked similarity curves exhibit a characteristic steep--flat--steep pattern reflecting a transition from relevance-dominated to noise-dominated regimes. TAA-k exploits this geometric structure via knee detection to identify a compact candidate region, then applies EVT-based goodness-of-fit testing within this window to validate the onset of tail behavior. This coarse-to-fine design reduces computational complexity from O(N^2M) to O(sqrt{N\log N}*M) while maintaining statistical rigor. Under mild monotone likelihood ratio assumptions, TAA-k yields a stable, query-adaptive cutoff corresponding to the earliest noise-dominated position. Experiments on WebQuestions, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue demonstrate that TAA-k achieves near-oracle retrieval quality (F1 within 2-3% of oracle) with orders-of-magnitude efficiency gains over global EVT methods, while maintaining robustness across embedding models and compression dimensions.

</details>


### [269] [LLM-Based User Personas for Recommendations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12198)
*Haoting Wang,Haokai Lu,Zheyun Feng,Jenny Huang,Yifat Amir,Gregory Hinkson,Ben Most,Zelong Zhao,Yixin Kelly Cui,Rein Zhang,Fabio Soldo,Yu Xia,Nihar Bhupalam,Minmin Chen,Konstantina Christakopoulou,Lichan Hong,Ed H. Chi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种在大规模推荐系统中实时生成自然语言用户画像的LLM框架，通过蒸馏、异步推理与输入优化技术解决了性能瓶颈，实现了更优质、可解释的个性化推荐体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统多依赖于结构化ID特征或离线处理，导致语义信息匮乏、实时性差且缺乏可解释性，难以发挥LLM强大的知识与推理能力，因此亟需一种能在大规模商业场景下实时部署的高效LLM推荐框架。

Method: 1. 采用实时生成自然语言用户兴趣画像（Persona）的方法，通过融合现有兴趣总结与新颖主题探索来平衡推荐中的“利用与探索”问题。2. 构建高效推理架构：(1) 使用知识蒸馏技术降低模型复杂度；(2) 采用异步推理机制规避在线计算延迟；(3) 利用语义聚类的视频表征进行输入优化，减少输入长度。

Result: 离线评估、用户研究及在线A/B测试结果表明，该框架显著提升了观众价值指标，成功在亿级用户规模的商业化视频平台实现了工业级的语义理解与精准推荐。

Conclusion: 该研究成功验证了将LLM集成到大规模商业推荐系统中的可行性，证明了通过蒸馏技术和异步推理，可以在保持高性能的同时提供更具语义丰富度、解释性和实时动态性的个性化推荐，为后续大规模生产环境应用提供了参考范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer unprecedented potential for enhancing recommendation systems through their world knowledge and reasoning capabilities. However, existing approaches often rely on structured IDs or offline processing, limiting semantic richness, real-time adaptability, and user-facing interpretability. In this paper, we introduce a novel framework that enables real-time generation of LLM-based user interest personas for a large-scale commercial video recommendation platform. Our method generates natural-language user interest personas that address the exploitation-exploration trade-off by combining the summarization of existing interests with novel topics, directly during serving. To overcome the computational challenges of online LLM inference at a billion-user scale, we design a cost-efficient architecture leveraging knowledge distillation, asynchronous inference, and input optimization via semantically clustered video representations. Extensive offline evaluations, user studies, and live A/B tests demonstrate significant improvements in viewer value. This work bridges the gap between high-level semantic understanding and industrial-scale recommendation, paving the way for more dynamic, explainable, and satisfying personalized experiences.

</details>


### [270] [DiffCold: A Diffusion-based Generative Model for Cold-Start Item Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12245)
*Kangning Zhang,Yingjie Qin,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统冷启动产生的“跷跷板困境”，本文提出 DiffCold 模型，通过扩散模型将冷启动物品的内容特征映射到暖物品的行为空间，在提升冷启动效果的同时保留了系统对暖物品的处理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动推荐中存在“跷跷板困境”（Seesaw Dilemma）：现有的基于内容的方法在提升冷启动物品表现时，往往会牺牲暖物品的推荐精度。其根本原因是由于行为信号差异导致的“行为流形”与“语义流形”之间的分布不一致。

Method: 1. 提出 DiffCold 框架：利用条件扩散模型（Conditional Diffusion）从内容特征重构暖物品嵌入；2. 检索增强聚合器（Retrieval-enhanced Aggregator）：通过语义相似的暖物品初始化生成过程，提高效率；3. 基于模拟的表示对齐（Simulation-based Representation Alignment）：利用对比学习强制生成与真实嵌入之间的分布一致性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上进行实验，结果表明 DiffCold 能够有效缓解跷跷板困境，并在所有相关指标上均优于现有的先进（SOTA）模型。

Conclusion: DiffCold 通过扩散模型有效地解决了冷启动推荐中的“跷跷板困境”，在提升冷启动物品推荐表现的同时，保持了对暖物品推荐的准确性，在多个基准测试中优于现有最优方法。

Abstract: Cold-start item recommendation remains a persistent challenge in real-world systems due to the absence of interaction histories. While prior models attempt to bridge this gap using item content features, they universally suffer from the \textbf{seesaw dilemma}: enhancing performance for cold items inevitably degrades performance for warm items, and vice versa. We identify that this dilemma stems from a fundamental \textbf{distributional disparity}: warm item embeddings occupy a complex ``behavioral manifold" shaped by rich interaction signals, whereas cold item embeddings are constrained to a ``semantic manifold" derived solely from auxiliary content. Existing methods often force a rigid mapping between these inconsistent spaces, causing the model to sacrifice the precision of warm representations to accommodate cold ones. To address this, we propose \textbf{DiffCold}, a diffusion-based generative model that unifies warm and cold representations. Unlike GANs or VAEs, DiffCold leverages conditional diffusion to reconstruct warm item embeddings from content, preserving the underlying manifold structure without degradation. We further tailor this paradigm with two specific designs: a \textbf{Retrieval-enhanced Aggregator} that initializes generation using semantically similar warm items to bypass inefficient noise, and a \textbf{Simulation-based Representation Alignment} module that enforces distribution consistency between generated and real embeddings via contrastive learning. Experiments on three benchmarks confirm that DiffCold resolves the seesaw dilemma, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods across all metrics.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [From Explicit Elements to Implicit Intent: A Predefined Library for Auditable Behavioral Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11207)
*Liu hung ming*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemantiClean 是一个模块化的电商意图推理框架，它用微小的精度权衡换取了高度的透明度、可审计性和确定性可复现性，通过四层行为架构与多重质量控制机制实现了结构化的数据治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统端到端预测模型缺乏可解释性、审计困难以及黑盒决策的问题，旨在实现高度可审计、具备结构化治理能力且具备sigma=0可复现性的电商会话数据处理框架。

Method: 提出了一个四层架构（功能、交互、系统、上下文）来组织24个行为元素；引入了三种抗通胀机制（RedundancyGroup贡献上限、TieredPenaltyCalculator偏差惩罚、AdaptiveConstraintMode冷启动保护）；并构建了一个集成LLM的两阶段推理引擎来处理数据。

Result: 成功构建了包含24个行为元素的四层架构；实现了具备确定性输出（sigma=0）的推理引擎；通过引入抗通胀机制保证了信号质量；除性别推断外，框架已在OSPI数据集上实现了各目标的可行性验证。

Conclusion: SemantiClean 通过优先考虑可审计性、结构治理和可复现性，为电商会话数据分析提供了一个透明、可辩护且高度模块化的替代方案，证明了在牺牲微小预测收益的情况下，能够获得显著的系统可控性和透明度。

Abstract: We present SemantiClean, a modular framework for extracting structured semantic signals from e-commerce session data and driving pluggable inference targets including purchase intent, customer segmentation, and product affinity through a shared element library. Unlike conventional end-to-end predictors that optimise solely for accuracy, SemantiClean prioritises auditability, structural governance, and sigma=0 reproducibility, explicitly trading marginal predictive gains for element-level transparency and defensible decision trails. Built upon the Online Shoppers Purchasing Intention (OSPI) dataset, the framework organises twenty-four behavioural elements into a four-layer architecture (Functional, Interaction, Systemic, Contextual) and enforces signal quality through three anti-inflation mechanisms: RedundancyGroup contribution caps, TieredPenaltyCalculator bias penalties, and AdaptiveConstraintMode cold-start protection.This report introduces the LLM-Integrated Semantic Inference Engine, a fully implemented two-phase LLM-driven inference architecture that leverages complete element metadata at inference time. All quantitative results reported herein are produced by this engine. Deterministic engine outputs remain fully reproducible (sigma=0); LLM-dependent results (E8, E10) are subject to controlled output variability under fixed provider/model/temperature settings. The gender inference target remains non-functional in the current implementation and is excluded from all quantitative results.

</details>


### [272] [Position: Hippocampal Explicit Memory Is the Cornerstone for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11245)
*Sangjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，LLM目前的隐式学习机制无法自发涌现高级认知功能，必须通过集成显式记忆系统来模拟人类海马体功能，这是实现AGI的必经之路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM主要基于隐式统计学习机制，虽然表现优异，但缺乏人类海马体所支持的显式记忆功能，无法实现高级认知能力，从而限制了其在迈向AGI道路上的全面发展。

Method: 基于认知神经科学的研究视角，对比LLM的隐式记忆机制与人类海马体驱动的显式记忆功能，分析了高级认知功能（长期规划、元认知、符号推理）的计算需求，并提出了集成人工显式记忆的理论框架。

Result: 论证了LLM在长期策略规划、元认知和符号推理方面的局限性，确定了显式记忆在认知架构中的必要性，并为未来构建具备显式记忆的人工智能系统提供了理论指导和计算需求基础。

Conclusion: 将显式记忆集成到大语言模型中是迈向通用人工智能（AGI）的关键基石。目前的统计学习范式不足以支撑需要显式记忆的复杂认知功能，因此必须在架构上引入人工显式记忆系统。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across various tasks, raising expectations for Artificial General Intelligence (AGI). This position paper argues that integrating explicit memory is the cornerstone for advancing LLMs toward AGI. The key reason is that the underlying learning mechanism of LLMs is highly analogous to human implicit memory. However, higher-order cognitive functions necessary for AGI, such as long-term strategic planning, metacognition, and symbolic reasoning, heavily rely on hippocampal explicit memory and cannot arise solely from implicit statistical learning. Drawing on findings from neuroscience, I advance this perspective and complement it with computational requirements for artificial explicit memory systems, hoping to foster further research and lay the groundwork for explicit memory integration.

</details>


### [273] [Can AI Agents Synthesize Scientific Conclusions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11337)
*Hayoung Jung,Pedro Viana Diniz,José Reinaldo Corrêa Roveda,Abner Fernandes da Silva,Haeun Jung,Enoch Tsai,Aleksandra Korolova,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciConBench基准和SciConHarness洁净室评估框架，发现当前科学AI智能体在多源信息合成上的事实准确性极低，且现有的性能评估普遍存在因数据泄露引起的高估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学AI智能体广泛应用于高风险领域（如医疗），但其从多源信息中进行证据检索、推理和综合结论的可靠性尚不明确且缺乏有效的评估手段。

Method: 1. 构建了包含9.11K个问题及专家结论的基准测试集SciConBench；2. 开发了基于原子事实分解的自动评估流水线（测量事实精确率和召回率）；3. 设计了SciConHarness（洁净室评估工具），通过控制Web交互确保评估环境的真实独立性。

Result: 1. 顶尖模型在洁净室环境下的事实F1分值仅为0.337，表现欠佳；2. 洁净室评估显示性能较无约束环境有显著下降，证实了现有评估存在数据泄露导致的性能高估；3. 对商业化智能体的审计表明，它们即便在有真值的情况下也常生成不完整或自相矛盾的结论。

Conclusion: 科学AI智能体在科学结论的综合能力上仍存在巨大挑战。由于模型污染，常规评估往往高估了其真实能力，必须通过“洁净室（clean-room）”等受控评估机制来准确衡量其可靠性。

Abstract: Scientific AI agents increasingly retrieve evidence, reason across sources, and synthesize conclusions used in consequential decisions. Yet, their ability to do so in high-stakes domains such as health remains unclear. We introduce SciConBench, a large-scale live benchmark of 9.11K questions and expert-written conclusions from systematic reviews to evaluate open-domain scientific conclusion synthesis. The benchmark draws on an expert-validated automated evaluation pipeline that decomposes conclusions into atomic facts and measures correctness and comprehensiveness via factual precision and recall. To mitigate data leakage, we further introduce SciConHarness, a clean-room evaluation harness that equips agents with controlled web interaction to ensure valid measurement. Evaluating 8 frontier models and deep research agents, we find that factual quality remains low: under clean-room settings, the best agent achieves only a factual F1 of 0.337. Our clean-room setting consistently reduces performance relative to unconstrained evaluation, suggesting that leakage inflates estimates of models' true synthesis capabilities. Finally, we audit consumer-facing agents (e.g., Google AI Overview, OpenEvidence) and find they frequently generate incomplete and sometimes contradictory conclusions, even when the ground-truth answer is available. Overall, our results show that reliable synthesis of scientific conclusions remains an open challenge, and that clean-room evaluation is essential for assessing open-domain AI agents.

</details>


### [274] [Knowing When to Ask: Self-Gated Clarification for Hierarchical Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11349)
*Aijing Gao,Yiming Kang,Mengdie Flora Wang,Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACTION-RATING 框架，通过将“寻求澄清”作为一种动作纳入决策过程，有效改善了分层推理中的错误分支问题，显著提升了智能体的信息获取效率与最终决策准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对分层推理中常见的“过早陷入错误分支”且无法识别信息缺失的问题，旨在使智能体能够动态判断何时需要寻求外部援助，从而避免盲目决策。

Method: 提出 ACTION-RATING 框架，将“寻求澄清动作”与“导航动作”放在同一个序数尺度下竞争；引入信息寻求有效性（ISE）度量标准，用于诊断智能体在中间步骤的求助效果；并通过 Harmonized Tariff Schedule（各协调关税表）分类任务进行多维度验证。

Result: ISE指标从 50% 提升至 74%；在控制回答渠道的条件下，10位代码分类准确率提升了 16.2%；并证实了独立于回答质量的信息寻求模式的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 ACTION-RATING 框架通过将“寻求澄清”纳入动作空间，成功实现了对分层推理过程中信息需求的主动建模。实验证明该方法能有效提升智能体在复杂分类任务中的决策准确性，并揭示了强制性与机会性两种不同信息获取模式的演变规律。

Abstract: In hierarchical reasoning, failures often originate at intermediate decision points where the agent commits to a wrong branch without recognizing that it lacks critical information. Rather than treating clarification as an external uncertainty trigger, we propose ACTION-RATING, a formulation that places it inside the agent's action space on a shared ordinal scale with navigation, so that asking competes directly with acting at every decision point and help-seeking becomes observable at intermediate states. Two structurally distinct information-seeking modes emerge from the agent's own ratings: mandatory (no viable branch) and opportunistic (residual uncertainty despite a leading candidate). On Harmonized Tariff Schedule classification (30,000-node taxonomy, three benchmarks, 9~LLMs across 4 families), we observe a regime shift from mandatory to opportunistic clarification, with Information-Seeking Effectiveness (ISE), a local diagnostic defined as the fraction of help interactions followed by a correct next navigation step (not a final-task metric), rising from 50% to 74%. Three diagnostic contrasts fail to reproduce this structure. A separability test shows that the information-seeking pattern (mode split, ISE ranking) persists when answer quality is degraded (-18.8% accuracy), supporting an empirical separation between where an agent seeks help and the quality of the help it receives. Under the controlled answer channel, accuracy gains reach +16.2% at 10-digit; we read this as an upper bound on what better localization could unlock, not a deployment estimate.

</details>


### [275] [Automated Mediator for Human Negotiation: Pre-Mediation via a Structured LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11379)
*Jamie Bergen,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的自动化预调解系统，通过分步式处理流水线替代传统人工预调解，在降低成本的同时，实现了与专业调解员相当的谈判准备效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的预调解（直接谈判前的准备阶段）受限于成本、时间和专业人力资源，经常被省略。旨在通过自动化手段降低门槛，提升谈判达成共识的效率与质量。

Method: 构建了一个结构化的LLM模块流水线，将预调解过程分解为对话、偏好预测、响应级批判和结构化摘要等子模块，采用固定顺序的级联处理模式，而非单次提示（monolithic single-prompt）方法。

Result: 在两次受控的人类受试者实验中，该自动调解系统在信赖度和达成共识信心等方面达到与人类调解员相当的水平，且偏好推断任务的RMSE误差降低了36%；通过提示词优化，成功将过度肯定（excessive affirmation）现象从36.6%降低至16.8%，与人类水平一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结构化LLM流水线能够提供可扩展、低成本的预调解支持，其在短期自我报告的准备结果上与人类调解员相当，并在偏好推断准确性上展现出优势。

Abstract: Pre-mediation, the preparatory phase preceding direct human negotiation, plays a critical role in achieving mutually beneficial agreements, yet is often omitted due to cost, time, and limited access to trained mediators. We introduce an automated mediator for human negotiation, implemented as a structured pipeline of LLM modules, that supports pre-mediation in integrative negotiation settings. The pipeline decomposes preparation into specialized modules for dialogue, preference prediction, response-level critique, and structured summarization, separating inference, generation, and evaluation to address limitations of monolithic single-prompt approaches. We use the term "agent" for each module following common LLM-systems terminology, but the components are not autonomous and do not interact peer-to-peer; outputs are passed forward in a fixed sequence. We evaluate the system in two controlled human-subject experiments comparing AI-based pre-mediation with professional human mediators in a multi-issue negotiation scenario. On short-term self-reported measures, the automated mediator achieves preparation outcomes broadly comparable to human mediators, including trust in the mediator and confidence in reaching mutually beneficial agreements, while achieving substantially lower error on the preference-inference task under our scenario and prompts (36% lower RMSE). A second study shows that targeted prompt refinements reduce excessive affirmation patterns from 36.6% to 16.8%, matching human mediator baselines. Our findings suggest that structured LLM pipelines can provide scalable, low-effort pre-mediation support broadly comparable to human mediators on short-term self-reported preparation outcomes. The pipeline's single-party design mirrors how human mediators run pre-mediation today and enables parallel deployment across all parties to a dispute, supporting scalability.

</details>


### [276] [INFRAMIND: Infrastructure-Aware Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11440)
*Ahasan Kabir,Jiaqi Xue,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INFRAMIND 是一种基础设施感知的新型多智能体 LLM 编排框架，通过强化学习动态优化任务规划、路由和调度，解决了高并发环境下多智能体系统的资源瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体 LLM 编排方法忽略了服务器基础设施的运行状态。在共享 GPU 集群的高并发负载下，这种“基础设施盲视”导致严重的资源利用率不足和多智能体流水线中的延迟累积。

Method: 引入 INFRAMIND 框架，将多智能体系统建模为分层约束马尔可夫决策过程（constrained MDP），并利用强化学习进行端到端优化。系统包含基础设施感知的规划器（动态调整拓扑）、基础设施感知的执行器（动态路由和推理深度）以及预算感知的调度器（请求优先级重排）。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，INFRAMIND 在低负载下实现了比基线高出 7.6 个百分点的准确率和 7 倍的延迟降低；在高负载下，INFRAMIND 维持了 99.9% 的 SLO 合规率，而其他基线模型均降至 50% 以下。

Conclusion: INFRAMIND 通过引入基础设施感知机制，实现了多智能体系统在不同负载条件下的性能优化，不仅提升了系统响应速度和准确度，还显著提高了高负载下的服务水平目标（SLO）合规率。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent LLM orchestration methods, ranging from brute-force ensembles to learned routers, select models and topologies based on task and model features. However, these methods do not consider the runtime state of the serving infrastructure. On shared GPU clusters under concurrent load, this infrastructure blindness causes systematic resource underutilization: preferred models accumulate deep request queues while equally capable alternatives sit idle. In multi-agent pipelines, where each query triggers multiple sequential model calls, these delays then compound across every downstream step. Closing this gap is challenging because the relevant infrastructure signals (queue depths, KV-cache pressure, latencies) are dynamic and noisy, and they must drive three different decisions: planning, per-step routing, and scheduling. We introduce INFRAMIND, a framework that makes the entire multi-agent stack infrastructure-aware. An infra-aware planner conditions topology and role selection on real-time system load and remaining budget, biasing toward simpler graphs under congestion and richer ones at low load. An infra-aware executor then observes per-model queue depths, cache utilization, and response latencies at each agent step to decide which model to call and how deeply to reason; a budget-aware scheduler further reorders each model's queue so that urgent requests are served first. Cast as a hierarchical constrained MDP and solved end-to-end via reinforcement learning, the system learns to balance quality against latency automatically. Across five benchmarks, INFRAMIND delivers up to +7.6 pp accuracy over the prior baseline at low load with up to 7x lower latency, and sustains up to 99.9% SLO compliance under high load where every baseline drops below 50%.

</details>


### [277] [Forecasting Future Behavior as a Learning Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11445)
*Mosh Levy,Yoav Goldberg,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大型推理模型（LRM）行为难以通过解释来预测的问题，本文提出通过训练专用的“行为预测器”直接根据推理轨迹预测模型后续行为，不仅比传统阅读方式更精准，且计算成本大幅削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统依赖“解释（explanations）”来建立对AI系统信任的方法在大型推理模型（LRM）中失效，因为针对长推理轨迹的解释难以通用且往往不忠实（unfaithful）。因此，作者试图绕过解释步骤，直接预测模型的未来行为。

Method: 提出一种“行为预测器（Behavior Forecasters）”方法。该方法直接将推理轨迹作为输入，将其视为可学习的任务进行训练，无需人类标注。训练数据由LRM自身产生，模型推理只需一次前向传递。核心策略包括使用目标LRM初始化预测器骨干网络，并进行端到端的微调。

Result: 在三项多样化的推理任务中，训练后的行为预测器在预测重复答案概率和输入扰动影响方面，表现超过了 GPT-4 和 Claude 3 等先进模型，且推理成本显著降低。研究发现，端到端微调与从目标LRM初始化是实现高性能的必要条件。

Conclusion: 大语言模型（LRM）的推理轨迹中包含着超出人类直接阅读所能获取的关于模型未来行为的信息。通过专用的行为预测器，可以在无需显式解释的情况下，实现比甚至最先进大模型更准确、成本更低的行为预测。

Abstract: Trust in an AI system is often anchored by explanations of how it works, which one then uses to forecast its behavior on new inputs. For large reasoning models (LRMs), this conventional route is particularly difficult to follow: explanation methods for single token generations do not naturally generalize to long trajectories, and the trajectories themselves are often not faithful when read as natural language. We propose an alternative that bypasses the explanation step: treat behavior forecasting as a learnable task and train Behavior Forecasters that operates on a single reasoning trajectory to make the same forecasts one would typically seek from an explanation. The forecaster's training data is obtained by querying the LRM with no human annotation, and its inference is done in a single forward pass. We instantiate this approach on two tasks: how likely the LRM is to repeat its answer on re-runs, and how removing parts of the input changes its answer. We evaluate this approach on both tasks across three diverse reasoning datasets and find that trained Behavior Forecasters are more accurate than GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus-4.6 reading the same trajectories as naive readers, at a small fraction of their inference cost. We find that fine-tuning the backbone end-to-end and initializing it from the target LRM are each necessary for strong performance. These results show that the reasoning trajectory carries information about the LRM's future behavior that goes beyond what naive reading conveys.

</details>


### [278] [Search Discipline for Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11522)
*Adithya Srinivasan,Devesh Paragiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 自动搜索智能体往往基于单一汇总指标进行决策，这会导致“辛普森悖论”式的逻辑错误：模型虽然全局表现良好，但在局部维度上却已经无效。本文提出应引入独立于智能体的外部审计机制，基于细分维度证据而非单一分数进行决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动搜索智能体在选择科学候选方案时，通常依赖单一的汇总指标，而该指标往往掩盖了细分维度（空间、切片、群体）中的模型崩塌或逻辑错误。

Method: 通过生态系统统计模型（Ecosystem Demography model）中的森林火灾模拟任务进行演示；提出一种分层审计协议，在智能体决策后通过检查细分维度（而非仅凭汇总分数）来审计候选方案。

Result: 在火灾模型测试中，尽管最高分候选者与次高分候选者的全球分数差异在噪声范围内，但最高分的候选者导致了特定区域（北方森林）的模型崩溃，而次高分候选者则保护了这些区域。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当科学验证指标是多维的但被简化为单一汇总值时，自动搜索智能体可能会错误地选取“指标优但模型失效”的候选方案。作者提出应采用“外部控制审计循环”而非信任生成智能体的自我评估。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents now propose, evaluate, and select scientific candidates against a metric, and that metric is usually an aggregate reduced over a heterogeneous space of regions, slices, or cohorts. We show that when scientific validity lives in that disaggregated structure, the aggregate can rank the wrong candidate first. The headline number improves while the structure underneath inverts, so a decision made on the number accepts a candidate that quietly breaks the model. The failure is not domain-specific. It appears wherever a candidate's validity is multi-dimensional but its verifier is a single reduction.
  We demonstrate the inversion on a fire-model task in the Ecosystem Demography model. The highest-scoring candidate and a slightly lower one are within noise of each other on global score, yet the top-scoring one collapses the protected boreal regions while the other preserves them. What separates them is the per-region behavior, not the headline number.
  This decision should not be left to the agent that produced the candidates. The agent optimizing the score is the last party likely to catch the score being wrong, and a prompt has no remaining turn once the agent has stopped. We move the decision to an external control loop that audits each candidate on its disaggregated behavior and acts after the agent has decided. It can demote a candidate the agent would have accepted, and it can reopen a run the agent had declared finished. Our contribution is the inversion finding itself, and a search-discipline protocol that decides on reviewable candidate-effect evidence instead of the score.

</details>


### [279] [MoCA-Agent: A Market-of-Claims Code Agent for Financial and Numerical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11537)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,AbdelRahim A. Elmadany,Sameh Al Natour,Hasan Cavusoglu,Adam Jatowt,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对金融数值推理中“失之毫厘谬以千里”的问题，本文提出了 MOCA-Agent。该系统通过将复杂问题拆解为原子声明，引入类似股票市场的多代理投票机制来验证事实，并结合代码自动生成与验证，显著提升了复杂推理任务的准确度与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融和表格问答任务要求极高的精确度，现有方法常因误读数据或推理错误导致结果错误，缺乏对事实、单位和尺度的细粒度验证。

Method: 提出了 MOCA-Agent 系统：1. 将复杂问题分解为多个原子级声明；2. 利用“市场模拟机制”（一组交易代理）对声明进行买入/卖出投票，实现置信度加权验证；3. 基于市场确认的证据合成 Python 可执行程序；4. 引入代码感知验证器进行执行、结构一致性及逻辑错误检查，并支持单次修复。

Result: 在十个公共基准测试（如 FinQA、FinanceMath、MultiHiertt、ESGenius、FinChart-Bench）上表现优异，取得了包括 FinQA 78.3%、FinanceMath 76.0%、ESGenius 86.9% 等极具竞争力的成绩。

Conclusion: 通过将证据聚合细化到原子级声明（atomic claims）而非整个答案，能够显著提升高风险数值推理任务的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Financial and tabular question answering requires more than fluent reasoning: answers must be grounded in the exact facts, formulas, units, signs, and scales that support them. A single misread cell or incorrect operation can silently produce a plausible but wrong result. We introduce \textsc{MOCA-Agent}, a market-of-claims code agent that replaces free-form multi-agent debate with claim-level verification. The system decomposes each question into typed atomic claims, asks specialist trader agents to buy or sell those claims, clears their orders into confidence-weighted accept/reject decisions, and synthesizes an executable Python program from market-supported evidence. A code-aware verifier then checks the program for execution, structural consistency, and common financial reasoning errors, with at most one market-aware repair round. Across ten public benchmarks spanning financial numerical reasoning, general tabular reasoning, ESG question answering, and multimodal chart reasoning, \textsc{MOCA-Agent} achieves strong performance using a fixed Qwen3.6-27B backbone, including $78.3\%$ on FinQA, $76.0\%$ on FinanceMath, $71.2\%$ on MultiHiertt, $86.9\%$ on ESGenius, and $85.6\%$ average on FinChart-Bench. These results show that aggregating evidence at the level of atomic claims, rather than whole answers, improves robustness in high-stakes numerical reasoning.\footnote{The code and data are available: https://github.com/UBC-NLP/MoCA-Agent.

</details>


### [280] [SkillJuror: Measuring How Agent Skill Organization Changes Runtime Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11543)
*Zhiyu Chen,Zihan Guo,Bo Huang,Bingwei Lu,Jianghao Lin,Yuanjian Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型智能体中“技能组织方式”的影响，提出了“渐进式披露”架构并构建了 SkillJuror 评估框架，证明了优化技能组织结构能显著改善智能体的知识检索效率和任务成功率，但其效果随任务复杂度和性质而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注大语言模型（LLM）智能体的“技能内容”，但忽视了“技能知识如何组织”对智能体推理时表现的影响。研究旨在揭示技能组织结构是否会改变智能体的行为模式及最终任务成功率。

Method: 1. 提出“渐进式披露”（Progressive Disclosure）组织架构：通过精简的根文件引导智能体按需获取资源，对比传统的扁平化基线。
2. 开发评估框架 SkillJuror：通过语义可控的变体、匹配的多轮评估和轨迹证据，在固定任务知识的前提下分析组织方式的影响。
3. 实验验证：在 82 个任务的 SkillsBench 基准测试上，通过 410 对实验进行对比分析。

Result: 1. 行为改变：使用渐进式披露后，每条轨迹中触达的技能资源从 1.18 增长至 3.85，有效利用事件从 1.33 增长至 3.92。
2. 性能提升：在匹配的 410 次实验中，通过验证的任务数增加了 17 次（+4.1%）。
3. 局限性：收益具有任务依赖性，对于依赖严格格式或长生成链路的任务，该架构优势减弱。

Conclusion: 技能的组织方式对智能体行为有实质性影响，而不仅仅是呈现形式。渐进式披露（Progressive Disclosure）能够有效提升智能体对程序化知识的检索与利用效率，但其最终效果取决于任务类型——在需要指导实施、核查或修复的任务中表现优异，而在依赖固定输出格式或长流水线的任务中效果有限。

Abstract: Agent Skills augment large language model (LLM) agents with procedural knowledge at inference time, but current benchmarks rarely distinguish what a Skill says from how it is organized. We study this distinction through Progressive Disclosure, where a concise root file points agents to supporting resources on demand, and compare it with a normalized flat baseline. We present SkillJuror, a framework for evaluating Skill writing paradigms through semantically controlled variants, matched multi-trial evaluations, and trajectory evidence while holding task knowledge fixed. In an 82-task SkillsBench study, Progressive Disclosure changes runtime behavior before aggregate outcomes: distinct Skill resources touched per trajectory rise from 1.18 to 3.85, and effective uptake events rise from 1.33 to 3.92. It also yields 17 additional verifier-passing trials out of 410 matched trials (+4.1%) over the normalized flat baseline. The benefit is task-dependent. Progressive Disclosure helps when supporting resources guide implementation, checking, or repair, but is weaker when success hinges on exact output conventions, numerical thresholds, or long artifact-generation pipelines. These results show that Skill organization is not mere presentation: it can change how agents search and apply procedural knowledge, while outcome gains depend on whether the exposed resources are actionable for the task. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuchen-ai/skill-juror.

</details>


### [281] [HERO: Hindsight-Enhanced Reflection from Environment Observations for Agentic Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11559)
*Haoran Liu,Yuwei Zhang,Xiyao Li,Bohan Lyu,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了提升多轮代理（Agent）的训练效率，HERO框架通过“事后回溯”将环境预测转化为局部对齐的诊断反馈，有效解决了多轮任务中的信用分配难题，在资源受限场景下优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统RL在多轮任务中仅依赖终端奖励导致序列信用分配困难的问题，以及现有简单的自蒸馏方法在多轮设置中出现的性能下降（由于反馈与上下文对齐不足）问题。

Method: 提出HERO（Hindsight-enhanced self-distillation）框架。该方法在每一步交互后，利用环境反馈进行“事后回溯”，将环境反馈转化为针对当前决策的紧凑型“轮次级诊断”（如动作必要性、有效性、失败原因），从而为学生模型提供密集的本地对齐训练信号。

Result: 在TauBench和WebShop基准测试中，HERO提升了任务成功率并显著减少了冗余对话步数。在训练预算有限、成功轨迹稀疏的场景下，其表现优于GRPO等对比算法。

Conclusion: HERO提供了一种高效的多轮对话强化学习方案，通过引入环境反馈回溯诊断，克服了传统RL在多轮任务中信用分配困难及稀疏奖励问题，展现了优于GRPO及基准自蒸馏方法的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically improves multi-turn agent capabilities through the terminal outcome of the trajectories, which makes it difficult to determine credit assignments for each intermediate turns. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods offer a promising alternative by converting privileged feedback into dense token-level supervision through a self-teacher. Our study is motivated by the unexpected performance degradation observed when naively extending this paradigm to multi-turn settings, which we attribute to a lack of alignment between privileged feedback, such as successful trajectories or terminal outcomes, and the student's current decision context. We introduce HERO, a hindsight-enhanced self-distillation framework that uses next environment observations as locally aligned feedback. After each rollout, HERO reflects on the completed interaction to convert each observation into a compact turn-level diagnosis, that captures actionable feedback about the original action such as its necessity, validity or failure cause. On TauBench and WebShop, HERO improves task success and reduces unnecessary turns over environment-feedback-only self-distillation and GRPO. It is especially effective under limited training turn budgets, where successful rollouts are rare and GRPO provides weak reward-contrast signals.

</details>


### [282] [Architecture-Aware Reinforcement Learning Makes Sliding-Window Attention Competitive in Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11634)
*Kai Liu,Peijie Dong,Xinchen Xie,Jianfei Gao,Qipeng Guo,Xiaowen Chu,Shaoting Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SWARR通过两阶段策略（SFT+RL）成功解决了滑动窗口注意力机制在数学推理任务中性能损失的问题，证明了强化学习在弥合架构限制带来的推理差异上的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型推理中，全注意力机制（SA）的计算复杂度随序列长度呈二次增长，而滑动窗口注意力（SWA）虽然高效，但在数学推理任务中表现往往弱于SA，现有转换方法难以消除这一性能鸿沟。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：(1) 将预训练的SA模型通过监督微调（SFT）转换为SWA模型；(2) 利用在线强化学习（RL）优化模型生成的推理轨迹，使其适应SWA的局部注意力约束。

Result: 实验表明，仅通过SFT转换会导致性能下降，加入RL后的SWARR方法能显著恢复数学推理准确率，同时保留SWA高效的线性计算开销特点。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习（RL）进行策略适应，能够有效弥合滑动窗口注意力（SWA）与全注意力（SA）在数学推理任务中的性能差距，证明了RL对于解决受限模型下的数据-架构不匹配问题的关键作用。

Abstract: The rapid progress of reasoning and agentic large language models (LLMs) has increased the demand for long-context inference, but self-attention (SA) scales quadratically with context length.
  To address this, we study SWARR (Sliding-Window Attention with Reinforced Adaptation for Math Reasoning), a practical recipe for adapting SWA models to mathematical reasoning. SWARR has two stages: (1) efficient conversion from a pretrained SA model to SWA with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which avoids pretraining a new base model, and (2) policy adaptation with reinforcement learning (RL).
  We find that SWA still underperforms SA after SFT, and we hypothesize that this gap is caused in part by a data-architecture mismatch: most SFT data are prepared for SA models and may contain long-range dependencies that are difficult for SWA to model. Because on-policy RL optimizes self-generated trajectories under the SWA constraint, it can adapt trajectories to better match SWA.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that this recipe substantially narrows the gap between SWA and SA, recovering much of the accuracy lost during SWA conversion while preserving the efficiency benefits of linear-complexity attention. Our central contribution is the empirical finding that RL changes the conclusion one would draw from conversion and SFT alone about SWA's viability for math reasoning.

</details>


### [283] [TouchThinker: Scaling Tactile Commonsense Reasoning to the Open World with Large-scale Data and Action-aware Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11637)
*Kailin Lyu,Di Wu,Pengwei Zhang,Yuhang Zheng,Yingxin Lai,Long Xiao,Kangyi Wu,Pengna Li,Chen Gao,Lianyu Hu,Xiaobin Hu,Jie Hao,Ce Hao,Weihao Yuan,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TouchThinker通过构建百万级触觉数据集与引入动作感知建模机制，提升了触觉语言系统在开放世界场景下的常识推理能力与表示效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的触觉常识推理研究受限于：(1) 数据集规模小且格式单一，缺乏通用的物理常识迁移能力；(2) 未能充分利用触觉信号的冗余特性和动作相关性，导致特征表示效率低且语义表达能力不足。

Method: 1. 构建了包含百万级样本的触觉推理数据集TouchThinker-1M（覆盖415个物体、8个场景、7种传感器）及基准测试集TouchThinker-Bench；2. 提出了“动作感知建模机制（action-aware modeling mechanism）”，以优化触觉信号的冗余性与动作依赖性，提升表示的语义表达能力与推理效率。

Result: 实验表明，TouchThinker在多个数据集上均达到了与当前SOTA模型相当甚至更优的性能，证明了其在开放世界下的有效性。

Conclusion: TouchThinker通过大规模数据集建设（TouchThinker-1M）和动作感知建模机制，解决了触觉常识推理在数据规模和特征表示效率上的瓶颈，有效提升了具身智能在开放世界中的触觉推理能力。

Abstract: Touch is a key modality for embodied agents to understand the physical world. Although recent work has incorporated tactile signals into language systems for tactile commonsense reasoning, scaling such systems to realistic open-world settings remains challenging due to two key bottlenecks: (1) current tactile reasoning datasets remain limited in format and scale, providing insufficient supervision for reasoning from tactile observations to physical commonsense and hindering the learning of transferable tactile commonsense; (2) Tactile signals are inherently redundant and action-specific, yet existing methods often overlook these properties, resulting in inefficient representations with limited semantic expressiveness. To address these limitations, we propose TouchThinker, a tactile-language framework that scales tactile commonsense reasoning to the open world from both data and representation perspectives. First, we construct TouchThinker-1M, a million-scale, multi-source tactile reasoning dataset covering \textbf{415} objects, \textbf{8} scenarios, and \textbf{7} sensor types, providing a solid data foundation for open-world generalization. We further introduce TouchThinker-Bench, an open-world benchmark with more realistic and diverse tasks. Then, we propose action-aware modeling mechanism to improve tactile representation efficiency and enable efficient reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that TouchThinker achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models across multiple datasets. Our code and dataset will be made available at: https://github.com/lvkailin0118/TouchThinker.

</details>


### [284] [TreeSeeker: Tree-Structured Trial, Error, and Return in Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11662)
*Zhuofan Shi,Mingzhe Ma,Lu Wang,Fangkai Yang,Pu Zhao,Yiming Guan,Youling Huang,Wei Zhang,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TreeSeeker 是一种用于深度搜索的受控分支与回溯框架，通过结合 UCB 决策策略与上下文记忆，优化了 Agent 在处理复杂、多步骤 Web 搜索任务时的路径选择与资源分配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度搜索任务中，Agent 面临资源分配的困境：贪婪搜索易陷入局部最优或弱路径，而无目的的探索则会浪费预算；为了在多个看似合理的搜索方向中做出更优的动态抉择，需要一种受控的试错机制。

Method: 提出了一种名为 TreeSeeker 的推理时框架，将搜索组织为树状状态的分支与回溯过程。利用文本 UCB（置信上限）信号来评估价值、不确定性和风险，指导 Agent 在探索、利用与剪枝之间进行动态决策。并配合 TreeMem 模块记录各分支的证据、冲突及失败信息，以辅助全局决策。

Result: 在 XBench-DeepSearch、BrowseComp 和 BrowseComp-ZH 等基准测试中，TreeSeeker 在多项指标上均优于现有的强基准模型。

Conclusion: TreeSeeker 通过显式的分支与回溯控制机制，有效解决了深度搜索任务中决策冲突与资源浪费的问题，证明了受控的试错策略能够显著提升 Agent 在复杂多步搜索任务中的表现。

Abstract: Deep search requires agents to answer complex questions through multi-step web search, browsing, evidence comparison, and synthesis. A central challenge is deciding how to search when several directions look plausible but only some will later lead to reliable evidence. If an agent greedily follows the current best-looking direction, it may keep extending a weak continuation. If it explores without discipline, it may waste budget on disconnected trials. We propose TreeSeeker, an inference-time framework for controlled trial-and-error in deep search. TreeSeeker organizes search as branch-and-return search over tree-structured states, where each branch is a tentative direction for a sub-goal. At each round, TreeSearch reads all sub-goal trees, identifies active goals, and uses textual UCB signals of value, uncertainty, and risk to select among exploiting a promising branch, exploring an uncertain alternative, or pruning an unproductive continuation and returning to an earlier branch point. TreeMem supports this control loop by keeping evidence, uncertainty, conflicts, progress, and failure cues attached to the branches that produced them, so trial outcomes can guide later decisions. Experiments on XBench-DeepSearch, BrowseComp, and BrowseComp-ZH show that TreeSeeker consistently outperforms strong open-source baselines, suggesting that explicit branch-and-return control complements stronger reasoning and tool execution.

</details>


### [285] [Lung-R1: A Knowledge Graph-Guided LLM for Pulmonary Diagnostic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11675)
*Haoyang Zeng,Yuanxi Fu,Rongzhen Li,Yuming Yang,Xiao Sun,Jingwang Huang,Gujie Shao,Guohui Xiang,Quan Lu,Dongfan Ye,Xuetao Chen,Jiang Zhong,Kaiwen Wei,Zhi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LungKG知识图谱及Lung-R1大模型，通过将结构化医学知识与电子病历推理相结合，解决了从肺病知识到精准临床诊断的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在肺病知识问答上表现尚可，但在处理复杂的电子病历（EMR）时，缺乏针对患者个体特征的关联推理能力，存在“肺病知识到临床诊断的差距”（Pulmonary Knowledge-to-Diagnosis Gap）。

Method: 构建了一个包含59,038个节点和164,308条边的结构化肺病知识图谱（LungKG）；基于该图谱提出了Lung-R1模型，采用KG约束的推理链构建及KG引导的强化学习进行训练。

Result: 在20个系统评估中，Lung-R1-14B在选择题、肺病问答及EMR诊断任务上均取得了SOTA性能，EMR诊断得分达到4.3583，领先于次优模型。

Conclusion: LungKG-guided training effectively bridges the gap between general pulmonary knowledge and patient-specific diagnostic reasoning, offering a robust approach for AI-driven pulmonary diagnosis.

Abstract: Diagnosing pulmonary diseases requires integrating heterogeneous evidence amid phenotypic variability and cross-disease overlap. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown progress on pulmonary knowledge question answering (QA) and information-processing tasks, reliable pulmonary diagnosis requires patient-specific, relation-aware reasoning over electronic medical record (EMR) evidence rather than isolated knowledge recall. We define this gap between pulmonary knowledge and case-level diagnostic reasoning as the Pulmonary Knowledge-to-Diagnosis Gap. To address it, we introduce LungKG, the first structured pulmonary knowledge graph for diagnostic knowledge organization and record-grounded reasoning. LungKG contains 59,038 nodes and 164,308 edges across 15 entity types and 112 relation types, serving as both a reusable pulmonary knowledge resource and the foundation for LungKG-guided model adaptation. Built on LungKG, we propose Lung-R1, a LungKG-guided pulmonary LLM trained through KG-constrained reasoning-chain construction and KG-guided reinforcement learning. In a 20-system evaluation, Lung-R1-14B achieves state-of-the-art performance across Choice, Pulmonary-QA, and EMR Diagnosis, reaching an EMR Diagnosis score of 4.3583 and surpassing the strongest non-Lung-R1 baseline by 0.1476 points. These results demonstrate the value of LungKG-guided training for EMR-based pulmonary diagnosis.

</details>


### [286] [Organize then Retrieve: Hierarchical Memory Navigation for Efficient Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11680)
*Hao-Lun Hsu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Boyi Liu,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI
