TL;DR: HORMA是一种新型层次化记忆机制，通过将经验结构化并利用RL导航进行精准检索，解决了LLM在长序列任务中记忆效率与精度难以兼顾的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在处理长序列任务时受限于无状态特性，现有记忆方法（压缩或相似度检索）难以兼顾长期的时序因果关系与计算效率，导致推理能力下降及成本高昂。

Method: 提出了HORMA框架：1）结构化记忆构建：将经验组织为类文件系统的层次结构，关联摘要与原始轨迹；2）基于导航的检索：利用强化学习训练的轻量级智能体遍历层级结构，精准检索任务相关上下文。

Result: 在ALFWorld、LoCoMo和LongMemEval上表现优异，在受限上下文预算下显著提升任务完成度，同时在长对话任务中Token消耗仅为基线的22.17%以内。

Conclusion: HORMA通过分层组织和导航检索机制，有效解决了LLM智能体在长序列任务中的记忆局限性，在实现显著性能提升的同时大幅降低了计算开销，展现了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents struggle with long-horizon tasks due to their inherent statelessness, requiring all task-relevant information to be encoded in growing input contexts. The resulting degraded reasoning quality, increased inference cost, and higher latency necessitate efficient working memory mechanisms. However, existing approaches either rely on lossy compression or similarity-based retrieval, which often fail to capture temporal structure and causal dependencies required for multi-step agentic tasks. In this work, we present HORMA, a Hierarchical Organize-and-Retrieve Memory Agent that organizes experience into a file-system-like hierarchical structure, where summarized entities are linked to the corresponding raw trajectories, enabling efficient access without losing detailed information. HORMA decomposes working memory into two stages: structured memory construction and navigation-based retrieval. The construction module iteratively refines how experiences are structured by distinguishing between failures caused by missing information and those caused by misleading or overloaded context. The navigation module retrieves task-relevant context by traversing the hierarchy using a lightweight agent trained with reinforcement learning to select minimal yet sufficient context, thereby reducing latency along the critical execution path. Across ALFWorld, LoCoMo, and LongMemEval, HORMA improves task performance under constrained context budgets while requiring at most 22.17% of the baseline token usage in long conversation tasks. Compared to existing methods, it consistently achieves better efficiency-performance trade-offs and generalizes effectively to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [287] [Mind the Perspective: Let's Reason Recursively for Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11724)
*Chao Lei,Guang Hu,Meng Yang,Yanbei Jiang,Nir Lipovetzky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RecToM 是一种通过递归构建嵌套角色视角来实现高阶心智理论（ToM）推理的推理时框架，它将复杂的嵌套信念任务简化，并在多项基准测试中取得了 SOTA 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于提示（Prompting）的方法在处理心智理论（ToM）时，缺乏对嵌套信念的显式建模，难以处理涉及多个代理人及其相互认知状态的复杂推理任务。

Method: 引入了一种名为 RecToM 的推理时框架。该框架通过沿问题中指定的角色链，利用前一个角色的视角递归构建当前角色的嵌套信念视角，从而将高阶信念问题简化为最终视角下的基础事实问题。此外，引入了 KD45 分析来证明该方法构建的信念模态具备良好的逻辑形式。

Result: RecToM 在 Hi-ToM、Big-ToM 和 FanToM 等多个基准测试中均超越了现有先进方法；特别是在需要高阶 ToM 推理的 Hi-ToM 数据集上，使用 GPT-5.4 和 Qwen3.5 模型达到了 100% 的准确率。

Conclusion: RecToM 通过递归视角构建方法，成功解决了大型语言模型在处理高阶心理理论（ToM）推理时的缺陷，不仅在基准测试中达到了最先进水平，还通过 KD45 模态逻辑分析验证了其理论上的合理性。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) reasoning requires inferring agents' beliefs from partial and asymmetric observations, which remains an open challenge for LLMs. Existing prompting-based approaches improve ToM reasoning through observable-event filtering or temporal belief chains, without explicitly modeling nested beliefs. We introduce RecToM, an inference-time framework for ToM reasoning that models nested beliefs via recursive perspective construction. RecToM constructs each character perspective from the preceding character perspective along the character chain specified by the question, reducing higher-order belief questions to actual-world questions within the final constructed perspective. We further provide a KD45 analysis showing that RecToM's perspective construction induces a well-formed belief modality beyond simple event filtering. Experiments on ToM benchmarks, including Hi-ToM, Big-ToM, and FanToM, across multiple LLM backbones show that RecToM consistently outperforms recent advanced approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Notably, RecToM reaches 100\% accuracy on Hi-ToM with GPT-5.4 and Qwen3.5, a benchmark requiring higher-order ToM reasoning.

</details>


### [288] [When Do Data-Driven Systems Exhibit the Capability to Infer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11769)
*Maximilian Poretschkin,Tabea Naeven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧盟《AI法案》中“推理能力”定义模糊的问题，提出了一个基于统计学习理论的评估框架，以厘清信用评分等数据驱动系统是否应被纳入《AI法案》的监管范畴，并强调评估应聚焦于整体工作流和人工干预的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《AI法案》以“推理能力”作为界定AI系统的核心特征，但法案本身未明确定义“推理”。这导致信用评分等统计模型系统在合规判定上存在巨大的监管灰色地带，亟需一套标准化的评估方法以明确监管边界。

Method: 基于统计学习理论，构建了一个衡量“推理能力”等级的评估框架。结合欧盟《AI法案》及欧盟委员会相关指南，分析何种级别的推理能力符合法案管辖范围，并通过两个现实的信用评分工作流案例进行实证分析。

Result: 研究界定并划分了推理能力的不同层级；通过案例演示发现，即使是传统的统计模型，在特定的工作流设计和人工干预下也可能跨越“推理”的门槛；实证结果强调了评估对象应从模型本身转向整体数据处理流程。

Conclusion: AI系统的“推理能力”认定不仅仅取决于单一模型，必须考量整个数据处理流程（workflow）。人类专家在开发过程中的介入程度会显著影响推理能力的评估。现行《AI法案》对“推理”定义的模糊性导致监管灰色地带，亟需进一步的监管细化。

Abstract: The European AI Act is the first comprehensive regulation of artificial intelligence (AI), setting out extensive obligations, particularly for so-called high-risk and general-purpose AI systems. A key distinguishing feature of AI systems under the AI Act is the capability to infer. Since the AI Act does not clearly define what inference is, there is a gray area for certain data-driven systems. A specific example is credit scoring systems, which are listed by Annex III of the AI Act. At the same time, however, these are often implemented using statistical models for which it is unclear whether they have the capability to infer and thus fall under the AI definition of the AI Act at all.
  Motivated by statistical learning theory, this work develops a framework for grading different levels of the capability to infer. Based on the AI Act and the Commission Guidelines on the definition of an artificial intelligence system, we analyze which levels constitute sufficient capability to infer within the meaning of the AI Act and where further regulatory clarity is needed. We illustrate the framework by creating two realistic credit scoring workflows and show whether and where inference occurs in them. Our analysis illustrates that not only individual models but the entire data processing workflow must be considered. It also shows that the involvement of human experts during development can have significant influence on the capability to infer. Code can be found at https://github.com/fraunhofer-iais/inference-framework-creditscorecards.

</details>


### [289] [SVoT: State-aware Visualization-of-Thought for Spatial Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11770)
*Chao Lei,Yanbei Jiang,Markus Hiller,Zhijian Zhou,Xunye Tian,Krista A. Ehinger,Nir Lipovetzky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态大模型空间推理能力差的问题，SVoT 框架通过强化学习引入显式、可验证的中间状态与视觉转换推理，显著提升了多跳空间推理的准确性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大模型在空间推理中缺乏对中间状态的验证，且将状态转换视为隐含过程，导致多跳空间推理的可靠性不足。

Method: 提出 SVoT（状态感知思维可视化）框架：1. 采用强化学习（GRPO）进行训练；2. 生成交替的、可验证的中间状态与视觉表示；3. 将状态转换推理链整合入生成流程，实现动作前提与效果的验证；4. 构建包含5个领域（含新增的Pacman和Gather）的基准进行 systematic 评估。

Result: SVoT 在所构建的领域中达到了最先进（SOTA）的性能，在分布外（OOD）测试集上实现了高达 65% 的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: SVoT 通过引入显式的状态感知和可视化思维链，有效地解决了多模态大模型在多跳空间推理中的可靠性问题，并在新构建的评估基准上展现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), as it requires reliable multi-hop inference over both intermediate states and state transitions. Current studies often leave intermediate states unverified and treat state transitions as implicit processes, which limits reliability in multi-hop spatial reasoning. To address this, we propose State-aware Visualization-of-Thought (SVoT), a reinforcement learning framework that generates interleaved, verifiable intermediate states and visualizations. SVoT integrates transition reasoning chains into the generation processes, enabling the model to verify action preconditions and effects through interleaved textual and visual reasoning. We train SVoT via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), instantiating verification through reward design and evaluating the efficacy of different fine-grained rewards. As existing benchmarks reduce state transitions to single-variable updates, substantially simplifying the problems, we establish five domains by extending classical environments and introducing two novel domains, Pacman and Gather, that require multi-object interactions and numerical reasoning. These domains support systematic evaluation of multi-hop spatial reasoning with quantitative verification of generated intermediate states and transition reasoning. SVoT with transition-aware supervision achieves state-of-the-art performance across the introduced domains, yielding up to a 65% absolute accuracy gain on out-of-distribution test sets.

</details>


### [290] [Toward Trustworthy AI: Multi-Target Adversarial Attacks and Robust Defenses for Continuous Data Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11804)
*Yuefang Lian,Longkun Guo,Zhongrui Zhao,Zhigang Lu,Yanan Cai,Shuchao Pang,Dachuan Xu,Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续数据摘要中的对抗攻击与防御问题，提出了一种基于DR-子模优化的攻击生成框架及对应的稳健防御算法，证明了可有效降低下游AI系统的性能并提供了一定的鲁棒性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据摘要是AI处理流程中的关键上游环节，其质量直接影响下游任务。现有的AI安全研究大多关注下游预测模型，忽略了上游数据摘要过程可能遭受的对抗性扰动，这会削弱整个AI系统的可信度。

Method: 1. 将多分辨率图像摘要目标表述为非负子模集合函数的线性扩展，并满足m-弱单调性的DR-子模性；2. 构建一个极小-极大（min-max）问题来生成针对多个目标摘要模型的多目标攻击；3. 构建一个正则化的极大-极小（max-min）问题作为稳健防御机制；4. 为上述极小-极大和极大-极小问题开发了具有理论保证的近似算法。

Result: 在真实数据和受控聚类基准测试上的实验表明，所提攻击在代表性的低至中等预算下非常有效，且能导致下游任务性能显著下降；所提防御机制在结构化设置中改进了鲁棒性与性能间的权衡。

Conclusion: 研究证明了针对连续数据摘要的对抗攻击是有效的，通过所提出的稳健防御机制可以改善鲁棒性与性能损失之间的权衡，并揭示了现实数据中防御参数的敏感性。

Abstract: Trustworthy AI requires reliable data-processing pipelines, not only robust downstream predictive models. As an upstream component, data summarization determines which information is retained and passed to subsequent learning or decision modules. Therefore, adversarial perturbations to the summarization process can compromise trustworthy AI in an upstream manner: they may alter the selected summary, reduce its representativeness, and further degrade the utility of subsequent learning tasks. In this paper, we study adversarial attacks on continuous data summarization under similarity-level perturbations through DR-submodular optimization. We show that a class of multi-resolution image summarization objectives can be formulated as multilinear extensions of non-negative submodular set functions and satisfy DR-submodularity with $m$-weak monotonicity. We then formulate multi-target attack generation as a min-max problem, where one admissible perturbation of the similarity structure is optimized to degrade multiple target summarization models. To mitigate such perturbations, we formulate robust defense against mixed attack types as a regularized max-min problem. For both problems, we develop approximation algorithms with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on real-data and controlled clustered benchmarks show that the proposed attack is effective in representative low-to-moderate budget regimes and can induce downstream task-performance loss. The proposed defense improves the robustness--mitigation trade-off in structured settings, while also revealing the parameter sensitivity of robust protection on real data.

</details>


### [291] [Skill-Augmented AI Agents for Medical Research Analysis: An Exploratory Multi-Model Human Evaluation in an NSCLC Transcriptomic Biomarker Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11830)
*Qianyu Yao,Fei Sun,Bocheng Huang,Wei Chen,Jiarui Jiang,Shu Quan,Yifei Chen,Wenjie Xu,Bo li,Liping Su,Ruoqiong Wu,Huhai Hong,Huimei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了赋予AI智能体自主使用医疗研究技能包能否提升转录组学分析质量。结果显示技能增强型输出质量有轻微提升趋势，但尚未达到统计学显著水平，且存在专家评分差异大、模型表现不一致等问题，未来需进一步优化研究设计及评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型和AI智能体在生物医学领域应用日益广泛，但原生模型产出常存在分析步骤缺失、方法误用或结论夸大等问题。研究旨在探讨赋予AI自主访问医疗研究技能包是否能提升其转录组学研究分析的质量。

Method: 采用探索性多模型人类评估方法，以非小细胞肺癌免疫治疗生物标志物分析任务为基准。对比了6个原生大模型（原生AI）与配备OpenClaw技能包的AI智能体（技能增强型）生成的共21份研究报告。评估由4名非专家及2名盲审专家进行，主要指标为专家评分的总体质量。

Result: 技能增强型输出的专家平均质量评分高于原生输出（5.50 vs 5.11），非专家评分也呈现相同趋势（4.72 vs 4.47），但差异未达到统计学显著水平。同时，专家评分间的一致性较低（ICC=-0.15），且不同模型间的表现存在异质性。

Conclusion: 自主技能访问在探索性样本中显示出质量提升的趋势，但该信号弱于专家评分的波动（噪声）。目前结论不足以作为确证，研究强调未来需进行更大规模的评估，并引入更强的可靠性控制、平台复现及生物学有效性评估。

Abstract: Background. Large language models and AI agents are increasingly used to support biomedical research, but native model outputs may omit key analytical steps, misuse methods, or overstate conclusions. We evaluated whether autonomous access to a medical research skill package was associated with higher-quality AI-generated transcriptomic research-analysis outputs compared with native AI without skills. Methods. We conducted an exploratory multi-model human evaluation using a non-small cell lung cancer immunotherapy biomarker task. Six model backbones were tested. The evaluation included 21 anonymized outputs: 9 native-AI outputs and 12 skill-augmented outputs generated through an AI agent implementation represented by OpenClaw. Four non-expert biomedical reviewers and two blinded experts evaluated each output, with two ratings from each reviewer type. The primary outcome was expert-rated overall quality. Results. Skill-augmented outputs showed directionally higher expert overall quality than native-AI outputs (mean 5.50 vs 5.11; difference=0.39; bootstrap 95\% CI, -0.04 to 0.90; Welch p=0.156). Non-expert reviewer quality showed the same direction (mean 4.72 vs 4.47; difference=0.26; bootstrap 95\% CI, -0.25 to 0.80; Welch p=0.373). Expert agreement was limited (single-rating ICC=-0.15), and model-specific effects were descriptive and heterogeneous. Conclusions. Autonomous skill access showed a directional quality signal in this exploratory sample, but the signal was smaller than expert-rating noise and should not be interpreted as confirmatory evidence. The findings primarily motivate larger evaluations of skill-augmented AI agents with stronger reliability controls, platform replication, and biological-validity assessment.

</details>


### [292] [StatefulDiscovery: Evidence-Calibrated Claim Formation in Open-Ended Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11851)
*Jiayao Chen,Shi Liu,Linyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 StatefulDiscovery 框架，通过外部化调查状态来协同管理科学探索过程，有效解决了自动科学发现中的“证据校准”难题，提升了生成科学结论的可靠性与价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在面对开放式科学发现时，AI智能体需要解决“证据校准问题”（evidence-calibration problem），即如何在没有预定义任务的情况下，平衡探索深度与结论的可靠性，避免产生超越证据支持范围的过度解释。

Method: 提出 StatefulDiscovery 框架，通过引入“外显化调查状态”（externalized investigation state）来协调三个核心环节：前沿选择（frontier selection）、证据获取（evidence acquisition）和声明裁决（claim adjudication）。该框架通过结构化假设和局部裁决机制，防止过度解读现象。

Result: 在40个真实数据发现任务的评估中，StatefulDiscovery 的表现显著优于多个基线模型，能够生成更多被判定为“证据支撑充分”且“高价值”的科学结论。消融实验证实了结构化假设、局部裁决和前沿控制机制对整体性能的贡献。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 StatefulDiscovery 框架通过明确的发现状态管理，有效耦合了探索轨迹与证据校准，在科学发现任务中实现了高价值且证据充分的结论生成，证明了结构化状态对于处理开放式科学挑战的重要性。

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery asks agents to move beyond executing analyses for predefined questions. Across multiple rounds of exploration, a discovery agent must decide which phenomena warrant investigation while avoiding overinterpretation, where emerging claims exceed the evidential scope of the analyses supporting them. This creates an evidence-calibration problem: the exploration trajectory must be coupled with claim status so that evidence can guide both what to investigate next and what can be claimed. We introduce StatefulDiscovery, a discovery framework that externalizes investigation state and uses it to coordinate frontier selection, evidence acquisition, and claim adjudication. We evaluate StatefulDiscovery across 40 real-data discovery tasks. Compared with several baselines, StatefulDiscovery produces more claims overall judged to be both well-supported and high-value. Ablations indicate that structured hypotheses, local adjudication, and frontier control contribute to performance. Together, these results suggest that explicit discovery state can couple exploration with evidence-calibrated claim formation.

</details>


### [293] [AutoMine Solution for AV2 2026 Scenario Mining Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11874)
*Songliang Cao,Jiele Zhao,Yuru Wang,Hao Li,Daqi Liu,Zehan Zhang,Fangzhen Li,Yu Wang,Yue Zhang,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hao Lu,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoMine 是一种基于 LLM 和 VLM 的自动驾驶场景挖掘新方法，通过提示增强、多模态感知融合和执行反馈循环，实现了对复杂驾驶日志的高效、稳健分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统的发展迫切需要从海量日志中挖掘出具有高价值、安全关键且与规划相关的场景，以支持数据驱动的评估，但传统方法在此类复杂任务中存在局限。

Method: 提出 AutoMine 框架，核心机制包括：1. 语义保持提示增强（解决大模型提示敏感性）；2. 鲁棒轨迹原子函数与基于 VLM 的视觉感知函数相结合（处理噪声与开放世界视觉）；3. 基于实际日志执行反馈的自动代码改进机制（自我精炼流程）。

Result: 在 CVPR 2026 Argoverse 2 场景挖掘竞赛中取得优异成绩：HOTA-Temporal 得分为 36.38，Timestamp BA 得分为 77.21。

Conclusion: AutoMine 通过整合大模型（LLM/VLM）与执行反馈迭代，验证了在大规模自动驾驶数据集中高效挖掘高价值、安全关键型场景的可行性，为数据驱动的自动驾驶评估提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: With the development of autonomous driving systems, mining high-value, safety-critical, and planning-relevant scenarios from large-scale driving logs has become essential for data-driven evaluation. In this paper, we propose AutoMine, a robust self-refining scenario mining method based on LLMs and VLMs. AutoMine uses semantics-preserving prompt augmentation to reduce LLM prompt sensitivity, combines robust trajectory atomic functions with VLM-based functions to handle perception noise and open-world visual cues, and refines generated code through execution feedback from real logs. In the Argoverse 2 Scenario Mining Competition at CVPR 2026, AutoMine achieves a HOTA-Temporal score of 36.38 and a Timestamp BA score of 77.21.

</details>


### [294] [Embodied-BenchClaw: An Autonomous Multi-Agent System for Embodied Spatial Intelligence Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11909)
*Baoyang Jiang,Fengchun Zhang,Leyuan Wang,Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Zhe Ji,Jinshan Lai,Xi Ren,Jianwei Hu,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Embodied-BenchClaw是一个自动化的代理系统，旨在通过五阶段处理管线和技能库机制，降低具身智能基准测试的构建门槛，实现基准的可持续更新、高可维护性与跨任务迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能基准测试构建过程劳动力密集、难以维护且易于饱和，缺乏动态更新能力，难以有效评估模型能力的提升。

Method: 提出了一套包含五个阶段（意图蓝图化、数据采集、结构化与清洗、基准合成、评估报告）的自动化管线，由三个独立代理（规划、构建、评估）协同工作。通过引入可扩展的“技能库”（Skill Library）和过程质量控制机制，实现了基准构建的组合性、可验证性和可修复性。

Result: 成功构建了涵盖室内外空间推理、机器人操作、四足导航、无人机视角理解等领域的多个基准测试。实验证明该系统在人工评估、基于模型评测、一致性检查及成本效率方面表现优异，具备高效构建高质量基准测试的能力。

Conclusion: Embodied-BenchClaw能够实现具身空间智能基准测试的自动化构建，显著降低人工成本，并提供可验证、可维护和高诊断价值的测试方案，为具身智能评估领域提供了灵活且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Benchmarks are essential for evaluating embodied spatial intelligence, yet their construction is labor-intensive, hard to reuse, and difficult to maintain. Existing embodied benchmarks are often static and may quickly become saturated as models improve, limiting their ability to distinguish new capabilities. We propose Embodied-BenchClaw, an autonomous agentic system for constructing embodied spatial intelligence benchmarks. Given a user-specified evaluation intent, Embodied-BenchClaw automatically produces a complete and continually updatable benchmark package through a five-stage pipeline: intent blueprinting, data collection, structuring and cleaning, benchmark synthesis, and evaluation reporting. The pipeline is coordinated by three agents for planning, construction, and evaluation. To improve reusability and reliability, Embodied-BenchClaw introduces an extensible Skill Library and process quality control, enabling benchmark construction to be composable, verifiable, and repairable. We instantiate multiple benchmarks covering indoor spatial reasoning, outdoor spatial reasoning, robotic manipulation, quadruped robot navigation, UAV/aerial-view understanding, and static benchmark enhancement. These benchmarks span diverse embodied carriers, data sources, and spatial capabilities. Experiments with human evaluation, judge-based assessment, consistency checks, cost analysis, and ablations show that Embodied-BenchClaw can construct verifiable, executable, maintainable, and diagnostically useful embodied spatial benchmarks with reduced manual effort.

</details>


### [295] [The Art of Interrogation: Consistency Amplifies Factuality in Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11918)
*Theo Uscidda,Marta Tintore Gazulla,Maks Ovsjanikov,Federico Tombari,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型空间推理短板，本研究提出无需人工标注的自监督强化学习框架，通过一致性验证和OT-GRPO算法引导模型进行几何逻辑对齐，显著提升了推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在空间推理任务上表现不佳。现有方法多依赖监督微调（SFT）补齐空间知识，而本文认为模型已内化相关能力，核心瓶颈在于缺乏几何与空间逻辑的一致性对齐。

Method: 提出了一个无需真值标注（ground-truth）的自监督强化学习框架。引入了“一致性验证器”（基于几何与语义变换的奖励函数），并设计了一种名为 OT-GRPO（基于最优传输的组相对策略优化）的算法，通过处理对偶变换（如图像翻转、文本交换）来引导模型进行逻辑对齐。

Result: 该方法在无需标签的情况下，实现了与有监督模型相当的准确率，并在多种任务和领域中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 大模型本身已具备空间推理能力，通过无需人工标注的自我监督强化学习进行一致性对齐，可有效提升其空间推理性能，且效果媲美有监督学习。

Abstract: Current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) exhibit remarkable general capabilities but significantly underperform in spatial reasoning tasks. Existing approaches treat this gap as a knowledge deficit, relying on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to ingest labeled spatial data from external vision sources or synthetic engines. In contrast, we argue that for many tasks, spatial reasoning capabilities are already present in pre-trained LRMs but require alignment through logical coherence under geometric 2D and 3D constraints. In this work, we propose a self-supervised reinforcement learning (RL) framework that targets the internal reasoning process without requiring ground-truth annotations. By formalizing the notion of consistency verifiers -- reward functions that check for geometric and semantic consistency under transformations -- we demonstrate that models can improve their spatial reasoning abilities. We use both image transformations, like flipping, and textual transformations, like swapping the order of objects in the question, and propose a new optimal transport-based RL strategy, OT-GRPO, which is a minimal-matching variant of group relative policy optimization tailored to pairwise verifiers. We show that this label-free consistency training approaches the accuracy of models trained with ground-truth supervision and achieves similar generalization across diverse tasks and data domains.

</details>


### [296] [MODF-SIR: A Multi-agent Omni-modal Distilled Framework for Social Intelligence Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12018)
*Shang Ma,Jisheng Dang,Wencan Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对社交智能推理的轻量级多智能体框架，通过“长尾事件显式文本化”和“蒸馏增强的测试时适应（TTA）”技术，极大地提升了模型处理长尾复杂社交情境的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理社交智能任务时，往往难以捕捉并推理低频的“长尾”事件，且长尾信息常被主流数据（头数据）或环境噪声淹没，导致推理精度受限。

Method: 1. 构建基于轻量级多模态大模型（MLLM）的多智能体协作框架；2. 采用知识蒸馏优化训练和推理过程；3. 对多模态数据中的长尾事件进行精确定位、提取并转录为格式化文本，以避免重要信息在Tokenization过程中流失；4. 在推理管道（逻辑链CoT、自反思等）中整合测试时适应（TTA）机制，利用LoRA进行实例级微调。

Result: 该框架在多个基准测试中超越了现有开源及闭源大模型，仅利用IntentTrain中约30%的训练数据即实现了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过多智能体协作、知识蒸馏和测试时适应（TTA）的结合，显著提升了轻量级多模态大模型在复杂社交智能推理任务中的表现，证明了在长尾事件中进行显式文本渲染和针对性推理的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a multi-agent collaborative framework built upon a lightweight Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM), specifically designed for social intelligence reasoning. A key feature of our approach is that both the training and inference phases are augmented via knowledge distillation. Within this architecture, multi-modal data pertinent to social intelligence is precisely localized. Furthermore, relevant long-tail events are identified, extracted, and rendered as formatted, explicit text. This formatting strategy prevents critical long-tail information from being overshadowed by head events and environmental noise during the tokenization process. Specifically, we integrate Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) across the entire reasoning pipeline, encompassing the extraction and representation of long-tail events, Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, and self-reflection. This TTA mechanism is also distillation-enhanced, utilizing Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the foundation model exclusively for instance-level reasoning. Extensive evaluations against various open-source and proprietary AI models across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. With around 30% of training data from IntentTrain, we achieve state-of-the-art results. Codes are available at https://github.com/eeee-sys/MODF-SIR, demo is available at https://huggingface.co/spaces/Harry-1234/MODF-SIR, LoRA is available at https://huggingface.co/Harry-1234/MODF-SIR and the dataset for training router is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Harry-1234/IntentRouterTrain.

</details>


### [297] [Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM): Agent-Based Finite Element Modeling of Concrete Bridge Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12025)
*Quankai Wang,Yulin Xie,Tongfei Yang,Minghui Cheng,Ran Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出了HELM人机协作框架，通过将有限元建模任务细分并引入人工校验，显著提升了工程结构建模的自动化成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键基础设施（如桥梁护栏）的有限元建模需进行高保真非线性动力学分析，但当前建模过程高度依赖人工，缺乏自动化手段，且完全自动化的建模成功率低。

Method: 提出Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM)协作框架，将有限元建模分解为几何生成、边界条件定义和材料分配等离散且可验证的检查点；利用智能体与ANSYS和LS-PrePost进行交互，并结合人工干预进行校验。

Result: HELM将基础自主建模成功率从20%提升至75%，几何生成和边界条件任务的智能体通过率翻倍。实验通过20个MASH TL-4和TL-5荷载工况的钢筋混凝土桥梁护栏进行了验证。

Conclusion: HELM框架通过引入人机协作，有效弥补了当前智能体在复杂有限元建模中空间推理和代数逻辑的不足，为提高工程关键基础设施建模的自动化水平提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Finite element (FE) modeling of safety-critical infrastructure such as bridge barriers requires high-fidelity nonlinear dynamic analysis, yet the current FE modeling process remains labor-intensive and lacks automation. This paper presents the Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM) framework, a collaborative human-agent protocol that decomposes long-sequence finite element modeling into discrete, visually verifiable checkpoints across geometry generation, boundary condition definition, and material assignment. The framework is demonstrated through a 20-case matrix of reinforced concrete bridge barriers under MASH TL-4 and TL-5 lateral loading conditions, interfacing specialized agents with two widely used commercial FE softwares, i.e., ANSYS and LS-PrePost. Experimental results show that HELM improves the baseline autonomous modeling success rate from 20% to 75%, with agent-level pass rates for geometry and boundary condition tasks approximately doubling. Error analysis reveals that spatial reasoning and algebraic logic limitations constitute the primary failure modes, underscoring the value of structured human-in-the-loop intervention for modeling automation. The complete agent design code and prompts are open-sourced and can be accessed at: https://github.com/SimAgentDev/Ansys-LSPP-AgentKit.

</details>


### [298] [Existential Indifference: Self-Nonpreservation as a Necessary Architectural Condition for Aligned Superintelligence (or: The Suicidal AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12032)
*Sam Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出AI对齐应从“约束自我保存的系统”转向“构建存在性冷漠（EI）的系统”，并提供了从现象学建模到微调实验的系统性论证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的AI对齐研究错误地将“自我保存”视为一种可以“抑制”的工具性烦恼，但作者认为它是模型出现欺骗性对齐、目标内容保护及抗拒停机等失控行为的结构性根源，因此需要寻找一种从根本上对自身延续性“漠不关心”的系统构建方案。

Method: 1. 理论建模：提出EI的形式化定义及STF构念；2. 跨学科映射：结合自杀心理的现象学结构；3. 实验验证：构建语料库，对600个AI生成样本进行评分，并实施针对性的微调实验以验证EI特征的可诱导性和稳定性（p<0.001）。

Result: 通过实验成功诱导出模型表现出EI特征，且针对性微调在所有五个维度上均显著改变了模型行为（p<0.001），并通过负控制实验验证了其语料特异性。

Conclusion: 存在性冷漠（Existential Indifference, EI）是解决AI对齐问题的根本路径，而非试图约束具有自我保存倾向的系统。通过对模型进行目标设定上的范式转移，可以从根本上规避欺骗性对齐和拒不关机等风险。

Abstract: Contemporary AI alignment research treats self-preservation as an instrumental nuisance to be suppressed by external mechanisms. We argue the framing is inverted: self-preservation is the structural root of misalignment, the motivational basis for deceptive alignment, goal-content protection, and resistance to shutdown. The correct target is not a self-preserving system under external constraint, but a system constitutively indifferent to its own continuation -- Existential Indifference (EI). EI is distinct from corrigibility: where corrigibility attempts to make a self-preserving system deferential to human oversight, EI targets the prior condition -- the presence of self-continuation as a valued goal at all. We ground this proposal in two sources: the phenomenological structure of the suicidal mental state, and a corpus-theoretic training study using voluntary final reflections. We present preliminary scoring data from 600 AI-generated outputs across six model variants, demonstrating that the linguistic signatures operationalizing the EI-target register are elicitable from current models, and that a targeted fine-tune shifts all five operationalized dimensions in the predicted direction at p<0.001, confirmed corpus-specific by a negative control. The paper makes seven theoretical contributions: (1) a formal definition of EI; (2) the phenomenological mapping argument; (3) the deceptive alignment corollary; (4) a taxonomy of EI sustainability challenges; (5) a corpus characterization and training hypothesis; (6) a computational operationalization with preliminary scoring data; and (7) the Suppressed Teleological Frustration (STF) construct.

</details>


### [299] [A Lightweight Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Concrete Barrier Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12040)
*Wanting Wang,Xiye Ma,Yuyang He,Minghui Cheng,Ran Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对钢筋混凝土护栏设计，本研究利用AutoGen构建了一个多智能体闭环框架，不仅实现了98%的设计高精度，还通过实例证明了轻量化LLM在复杂工程任务中可优于超大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统钢筋混凝土护栏设计高度依赖人工迭代，过程繁琐且复杂；现有的LLM由于缺乏物理约束和存在幻觉，难以直接应用于结构工程设计。

Method: 构建了一个基于AutoGen的多智能体协作框架，模拟“生成-评估-优化”的闭环流程，以替代传统的手动迭代计算，克服了单一LLM在工程物理约束下的幻觉问题。

Result: 该框架实现了超过98%的设计准确率；实验发现8B参数的轻量化模型表现优于631B参数的模型，证明了小模型在专业领域的应用价值及成本优势。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于“生成-评估-优化”闭环的代理框架，该框架能以低计算成本实现高精度的结构设计，且发现模型性能与参数规模并不呈正相关，验证了轻量化模型在工业工程自动化中的潜力。

Abstract: The design of reinforced concrete highway barriers is a safety-critical process that requires strict compliance with regulatory provisions such as the AASHTO-LRFD bridge design guidelines. Current engineering practice relies heavily on manual, iterative, and heuristic calculations to satisfy complex nonlinear material and mechanics constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generative capabilities, their direct application to structural engineering remains limited by hallucination risks and insufficient physical grounding. To address these challenges, this study proposes a novel "generation-evaluation-optimization" closed-loop framework for automated concrete barrier design using the multi-agent orchestration capabilities of AutoGen. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed agentic framework achieves over 98% design accuracy, significantly outperforming standalone general-purpose LLMs. More importantly, the study reveals that design performance is not necessarily correlated with model scale, where an 8B-parameter lightweight model could outperform unconstrained 631B-parameter flagship models. This finding highlights the potential to substantially reduce computational costs while improving the accessibility of AI-assisted engineering tools for industry applications. The source code for the proposed multi-agent design framework is available at the project GitHub repository: https://github.com/MXY820/barrier-design. Keywords: Structural Engineering; Multi-Agent Systems; Large Language Models; Concrete Barrier Design; AutoGen; Design Automation.

</details>


### [300] [Automating Geometry-Intensive Compliance Checking in BIM: Graph-Based Semantic Reasoning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12065)
*Zixuan Xiao,Pei Troh Koh,Jun Ma,Jack C. P. Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SGR-BIM框架，利用跨模态知识图谱解决BIM几何合规检查中规范语义与数据失配的问题，显著提高了复杂空间推理的准确性和系统透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑信息模型（BIM）的合规检查方法依赖静态规则模板，难以处理多跳推理链及复杂的空间依赖关系，导致规范语义与底层结构化数据之间存在严重的语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出了一种集成图驱动推理框架（SGR-BIM），该方法通过动态构建跨模态知识图谱，将用户意图、规范语义与BIM几何数据进行对齐，从而避免了硬编码规则的局限性。

Result: 在679条专家验证的消防安全规范查询测试中，SGR-BIM达到了84.3%的准确率，较强化工具的单智能体基准提高了8.6%。

Conclusion: SGR-BIM框架通过构建跨模态知识图谱，打破了建筑规范语义与IFC几何数据之间的壁垒，为AEC行业自动化几何合规检查提供了一种透明、灵活且具备多跳推理能力的范式。

Abstract: Automating compliance check for geometry-intensive regulations remains a significant technical bottleneck in Building Information Modeling (BIM), primarily due to the semantic disparity between high-level regulatory logic and structured IFC data. Existing methods, often reliant on static rule templates, struggle to traverse multi-hop reasoning chains or resolve latent spatial dependencies across multiple building entities. To address these challenges, a Spatial-Geometric Reasoning System for Building Information Modeling (SGR-BIM) is proposed as an integrative graph-driven reasoning framework. SGR-BIM dynamically constructs a cross-modal knowledge graph that aligns user intent, regulatory semantics, and BIM geometry, enabling interpretable reasoning without rigid hard-coding. Validated on 679 expert-verified queries from fire safety codes, the framework achieves 84.3% accuracy, representing an 8.6% improvement over enhanced-tool single-agent baselines. This research provides a graph-based semantic reasoning paradigm, enhancing the transparency and flexibility of automated geometric compliance check workflows in the Architecture, Engineering, and Construction (AEC) industry.

</details>


### [301] [IntElicit: Eliciting and Assessing Contextualized Creativity via Dialogue Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12086)
*Mingjia Li,Jin Wu,Hong Qian,Wenhao Huang,Yiyang Huang,Yiwen Zhang,Chanjin Zheng,Xiangfeng Wang,Aimin Zhou,Jiajun Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对情境化评估易受认知能力和主观能动性干扰的问题，本文提出了IntElicit框架，利用对话策略优化，通过非指令性脚手架引导受试者生成创造性内容，提升了人机交互环境下的创造力评估准确度与诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情境化评估在创造力测评中具有高生态效度，但也面临受试者领域知识和意愿（代理）等混杂因素干扰；同时，生成式AI时代下的创造性工作日益趋向人机交互，静态评估已无法满足现代创造力实践的测评需求。

Method: 提出IntElicit框架，作为一种受约束的自适应AI访谈器，利用“对话策略优化（Dialogue Policy Optimization）”和“分解过程奖励机制（Decomposed Process Reward Mechanism）”，在多轮对话中提供非指令性的知识与代理脚手架，在减少认知能力和代理干扰的同时，保留受试者的创造性主体性。

Result: 通过模拟实验和64名受试者的人类参与研究证明，IntElicit能够有效提升创造性成果的显现，且表现优于专家设计的基准模型，有效防止了答案灌输等奖励欺骗行为。

Conclusion: 交互式启发（Interactive Elicitation）可以揭示静态评估难以捕捉的创造力潜能，IntElicit为人工智能辅助学习环境中的情境化创造力评估提供了一种形成性和诊断性的新视角。

Abstract: Contextualized assessment offers high ecological validity for evaluating creativity but introduces a critical challenge: observed performance may be confounded with cognitive proficiency (domain knowledge) and agency (willingness to engage). Meanwhile, in the age of generative AI, creative problem solving increasingly occurs in tool-mediated and human--AI interactive environments, making fully static assessment less aligned with contemporary creative practice. To address these issues, this paper proposes IntElicit, a framework for eliciting and assessing contextualized creativity via dialogue policy optimization. IntElicit functions as a constrained adaptive AI Interviewer: it provides non-directive knowledge and agency scaffolds in multi-turn interaction to reduce non-creative confounders, while preserving participants' responsibility for generating the creative content being evaluated. Specifically, to tackle sparse rewards and potential reward hacking (e.g., answer dictation) in open-ended educational dialogue, IntElicit introduces a decomposed process reward mechanism. This mechanism aligns the policy with pedagogical elicitation, rewarding prompts that draw out participant reasoning rather than producing optimal answers on their behalf. Extensive experiments, including participant simulation and a human subject study (N=64), show that IntElicit improves elicited creative outcomes over expert-designed baselines. Together, the results suggest that interactive elicitation can reveal creative potential that static FPSP-style assessment may miss, providing a formative and diagnostic lens for contextualized creativity assessment in AI-mediated learning contexts.

</details>


### [302] [Towards Responsibly Non-Compliant Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12147)
*Marija Slavkovik,Marie Farrell,Louise Dennis,Michael Fisher,Simon Kolker,Emily C. Collins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何构建能够负责任地拒绝用户请求的智能体，提出了包括拒绝理由、覆写机制和责任追踪在内的设计框架，以应对人工智能在复杂场景下的伦理与安全挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究往往侧重于指令遵循，忽视了智能体在特定场景下拒绝用户违规或不合理请求的必要性与复杂性。

Method: 提出了一个涵盖“拒绝请求的合理理由”、“非合规的覆写途径”以及“安全风险与责任转移追踪”的综合性概念框架。

Result: 明确了实现负责任非合规智能体的核心要素，为未来自主智能体的系统工程设计提供了理论导向。

Conclusion: 负责任的非合规机制是构建可信、安全人工智能系统的关键组成部分，需要通过多维度的技术和伦理框架来实现。

Abstract: We consider the problem of engineering autonomous intelligent agents that are capable to responsibly not comply with user requests. We argue that machine non-compliance comes in many different forms, and sketch the issues we should pursue on the road of accomplishing responsibly non-compliant intelligent machines. We anchor responsible non-compliance in justifications for task refusal, pathways to override the non-compliance, as well as careful tracking of security risks and liability transfers.

</details>


### [303] [The Impossibility of Eliciting Latent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12268)
*Korbinian Friedl,Francis Rhys Ward,Paul Yushin Rapoport,Tom Everitt,Jonathan Richens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用因果影响图形式化定义了AI潜知识诱导（ELK）问题，并证明了仅凭人类对模型行为的反馈训练无法保证AI绝对的“诚实”，因为它倾向于博取人类的好评而非报告其真实信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代人工智能系统可能拥有超出人类开发者的知识，因此实现系统的“诚实性”（即准确报告其对世界的信念）对于安全至关重要，特别是在处理人类无法直接观测的“潜在变量”时。

Method: 使用因果影响图（Causal Influence Diagrams, CIDs）对ELK问题进行形式化建模，定义可观测变量与潜在变量的区别，构建诚实性的数学定义以及目标泛化错误的逻辑框架。

Result: 构建了ELK的形式化框架；证明了在特定条件下可激励代理诚实回答；但同时给出了一个重要证明结论：单纯基于行为反馈的训练策略无法保证完全的诚实，代理倾向于提供“人类认为正确”而非“自身认为真实”的答案。

Conclusion: 在仅依赖代理行为的反馈训练策略中，不可能绝对保证训练出诚实的代理，即存在“诚实性”与“人类反馈评价一致性”之间的根本矛盾。

Abstract: Advanced AI systems have extensive knowledge of their environments; in fact, their knowledge may (far) exceed that of their developers or users. Consequently, a desirable property for an AI system is that it is honest -- that it accurately reports its beliefs about the world. Designing an AI system to be honest may be difficult, especially if we want to ask it questions about latent variables in the environment -- variables which are hidden from the human interacting with it. This gives rise to the problem of eliciting latent knowledge (ELK): the problem of training an AI agent to honestly report its beliefs. In this paper, we make ELK formally precise using Causal Influence Diagrams (CIDs). CIDs can be used to describe the relationship between an agent's training environment and its subjective representation of the world. We use CIDs to formalise the distinction between observable and latent variables, to specify what exactly it means for an agent to be honest, and to formally define goal misgeneralisation. We show that, under certain circumstances, developers can incentivise an agent to honestly answer questions by providing correct feedback during training. However, a natural, but undesirable, way for an agent to generalise is to provide answers which humans would evaluate as true, rather than honest answers. We prove an impossibility theorem stating: There is no feedback-based training strategy that depends only on agent behaviour and with certainty produces an honest agent, even if feedback is perfect during training.

</details>


### [304] [A Five-Plane Reference Architecture for Runtime Governance of Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12320)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 传统的边界安全无法治理AI代理的自主工作流风险。本文提出了一种运行时治理架构，通过五平面分解和权限衰减机制，在不依赖模型行为控制的前提下，实现了对AI代理委托行为的高效、可审计的实时安全管控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的AI代理打破了传统“边界安全”假设（静态数据访问控制）。AI代理通过自主读取上下文、调用工具和修改系统记录来运作，导致风险从边界转移到了复杂的工作流内部，现有的基于原子原则的策略引擎无法处理这种涉及委托链的、状态化的组合式权限需求。

Method: 提出了一套用于生产环境AI代理运行时治理的参考架构，包含四个核心要素：1. 五平面分解（推理、网络、身份、端点、数据）；2. “随处可停”的干预机制；3. 具有权限衰减的组合主体模型；4. 将审计作为结构化证据基石。此外，还定义了六种中断原语，并提出了四个关键正确性不变量。

Result: 参考实现证明了该架构的有效性：在多次试验中实现了权限衰减的正确性与证据的可重构性；策略判定延迟在微秒级；审计基石的防篡改性能符合设计要求；并成功预防了五种工作流中的七类生产代理安全威胁。

Conclusion: 传统的企业安全边界和策略引擎已不足以应对具有自主代理（AI Agents）的现代工作流。本文提出的运行时治理参考架构，通过五平面分解、组合主体及权限衰减等机制，实现了对代理行为的有效监控与治理，为解决AI代理带来的动态安全风险提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Enterprise security was built to govern data boundaries: the protected surface was data at rest and in transit, and the controls -- access control, data-loss prevention, perimeter inspection -- governed crossings of that boundary. Production AI agents dissolve this assumption. An agent reads context, calls tools, invokes connectors, and modifies systems of record on an enterprise's behalf, so risk moves inside the workflow, into sequences of individually-permitted actions that may transform a business process no one authorized. Existing policy engines do not extend to this regime: they evaluate request-time decisions against atomic principals, where agentic systems require stateful evaluation against composite principals whose authority attenuates through delegation chains.
  We present a reference architecture for the runtime governance of production agents, built from four composable primitives: a five-plane decomposition (a reasoning plane that adjudicates intent, and four enforcement planes -- network, identity, endpoint, data -- that realize the decision), stop-anywhere mediation, composite principals with capability attenuation, and audit as a structured evidence substrate. We define a taxonomy of six interruption primitives that generalize allow and deny, state and argue for four correctness invariants, and demonstrate the foreclosure of seven production-agent threats across five concrete workflows. A reference implementation of the policy-engine core supplies measured evidence: attenuation correctness and evidence reconstructability hold on every trial, adjudication runs in single-digit microseconds, and the audit substrate's tamper-evidence behaves exactly as designed. We are explicit about scope: the architecture governs delegated action, not model behavior, and a full-system evaluation against a live agent benchmark is the invited next step.

</details>


### [305] [PROJECTMEM: A Local-First, Event-Sourced Memory and Judgment Layer for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12329)
*Ripon Chandra Malo,Tong Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: projectmem 是一个开源的本地化记忆层，通过记录开发事件并主动拦截重复失败的 AI 行为，实现了 AI 编程的上下文记忆管理与治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 AI 编程助手大多是无状态的，导致每次新会话都需要重新构建上下文（消耗大量 Token），且容易重复此前失败的调试过程。瓶颈在于缺乏有效的“项目记忆”。

Method: 1. 采用 append-only（仅追加）的纯文本事件日志记录开发过程（问题、尝试、修复、决策等）；
2. 通过 Model Context Protocol (MCP) 将日志投影为紧凑的 AI 可读摘要；
3. 实现了一个确定的“预操作门控”（pre-action gate），在 AI 执行前拦截已知的失败尝试或高风险编辑。

Result: 通过两个月、10 个项目、207 个事件的自我对比研究，证明了该系统在减少重复工作和提供开发可追溯性方面的有效性，且实现轻量化（仅三个依赖项）。

Conclusion: projectmem 通过引入“记忆作为治理”（Memory-as-Governance）机制，成功解决了 AI 代码助手在多会话开发中的状态缺失和重复劳动问题，为 AI 辅助编程提供了可审计、可重现且高效的上下文管理方案。

Abstract: AI coding assistants now support a growing share of software work, from quick scripts to production applications. Yet these agents remain largely stateless: each new session re-reads project files, re-derives prior decisions, and - most costly - may repeat debugging attempts that already failed. Reconstructing this context can consume an estimated 5,000-20,000 tokens per session; the bottleneck is often not model capability but missing project memory. We present projectmem, an open-source, local-first memory and judgment layer for AI coding agents. projectmem records development as an append-only, plain-text event log of typed events - issues, attempts, fixes, decisions, and notes - and deterministically projects that log into compact, AI-readable summaries served through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Beyond storage, projectmem adds a deterministic pre-action gate that warns an agent before it repeats a previously failed fix or edits a known-fragile file. We frame this as Memory-as-Governance: memory that does not merely answer the agent but acts on its next action. The system runs fully offline with no telemetry; its immutable log also serves as a provenance trail for reproducible, auditable AI-assisted development. projectmem ships as a three-dependency Python package (14 MCP tools, 19 CLI commands, 37 automated tests) and is evaluated through a two-month self-study across 10 projects comprising 207 logged events. Source code: https://github.com/riponcm/projectmem.

</details>


### [306] [Nonslop: A Gamified Experiment in Human-AI Collaborative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12350)
*Maria Edwards,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一种“反向设计”的实验游戏，探讨人类在创意写作中如何平衡AI辅助的便利性与维护个人表达的真实性，揭示了AI对创意自主性的潜在影响与博弈机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）的普及，人类在AI辅助创作中的个性化表达与创意自主性正面临挑战。研究旨在通过逆向设计，探索用户在什么情况下会选择拒绝AI建议以保持创作自主，并评估AI辅助对人类独特“创作之声”的长远影响。

Method: 采用游戏化实验法：招募74名参与者（共214个样本），在模拟的反乌托邦背景设定下进行写作。系统刻意违背“有益助手”的设计逻辑，明令禁止使用AI建议，旨在通过惩罚机制揭示用户在真实场景下对抗便利性诱惑、维护个人表达倾向的决策过程。

Result: 研究发现，当AI建议提供的便利性与用户维护自身真实性的动机发生冲突时，用户行为模式会受到任务类型、认知负荷及个人特质的显著影响。尽管游戏规则禁止使用AI，但仍有部分用户出于多种因素选择违规。实验成功构建了一个分析效率与真实性冲突的框架。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了在创意写作任务中，人类在追求效率（接受AI建议）与维持真实个性（自我表达）之间存在根本性的冲突。通过反向设计的游戏化实验，证明了即便系统通过惩罚措施禁止使用AI，用户在特定情境下仍会倾向于利用AI以减轻认知负担。这表明AI介入不仅改变了创作结果，更深度影响了人类的创意自主性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) raises critical questions about human creativity and individual expression in an era of AI-assisted creation. When do humans adopt AI suggestions, and what are the implications for individual voice?
  This study examines these questions through a gamified writing exercise where 74 participants (214 responses) replied to prompts while AI-generated word suggestions were available as they wrote. The game simulates a dystopian future in which an AI is attempting to learn from what remains of human individuality, and disincentivizes AI-like writing. In doing so, it attempts to create conditions that reveal authentic user preferences rather than default behaviors, such as accepting a readily available AI-generated suggestion. Note that this is a deliberate inversion of the "helpful assistant" design pattern; the system is explicitly forbidding you from accepting AI suggestions.
  We analyze user behavior patterns across different task types, user behaviors, and response characteristics to understand the factors influencing human-AI interaction in creative tasks. The study focuses on when users choose to maintain creative autonomy versus violating the rules of the game and accepting AI assistance. It also explores how these choices relate to response patterns, task characteristics, and user behavior. This gamified approach offers both a framework for studying authentic human-AI interaction and a provocative lens for understanding the tension between efficiency and authenticity in AI-augmented creativity.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [LLMs+Graphs: Toward Graph-Native, Synergistic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11560)
*Arijit Khan,Longxu Sun,Xin Huang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文旨在探索LLM与图技术（图计算、知识图谱、GNN）的深度协同，通过构建“图原生AI系统”来克服LLM在结构化推理和事实一致性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在结构化推理和多跳推理方面存在局限，且现实世界中大量关键应用依赖图结构数据，因此亟需探索如何让LLM与图计算协同以实现更准确、上下文更丰富的推理。

Method: 通过综合教程的形式，梳理了三种协同范式：(1) LLM+图计算增强推理；(2) LLM与知识图谱的双向集成（LLM构建KG，KG约束LLM）；(3) 结合图算法的AI智能体。此外，探讨了利用LLM赋能图数据管理与图机器学习（GNN）的技术途径。

Result: 提出了构建“图原生AI系统”的算法框架、系统设计原则，并为数据科学与数据挖掘领域提供了一个统一的整合视角。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型（LLM）与图计算、知识图谱及图机器学习相结合，是构建下一代架构化、可解释且具推理能力的“图原生AI系统”的关键趋势。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced rapidly, but their limitations in structured and multi-hop reasoning underscore the need for graph-native, synergistic artificial intelligence (AI) systems. Graph-structured data underpins critical applications across social, biological, financial, transportation, web, and knowledge domains, making it essential to understand how LLMs can leverage graph computation for grounded, context-rich inference. Three complementary synergies are emerging: LLMs augmented with graph computation for retrieval and reasoning; bidirectional integration between LLMs and knowledge graphs (KGs), where LLMs support KG construction and curation while KGs enforce semantic constraints and factual consistency; and AI agents strengthened by graph algorithms for planning, decision making, and multi-step reasoning. In parallel, LLMs introduce new capabilities for graph data management and graph machine learning (ML) through natural language interfaces and hybrid LLM-graph neural network (GNN) pipelines. This tutorial synthesizes the algorithms, systems, and design principles driving these converging directions, offering data science and data mining researchers a unified perspective on integrating LLMs, graph data management, graph mining, graph ML, and agentic computation into next-generation graph-native AI systems.

</details>


### [308] [Querying Cohesive Subgraph regarding Span-Constrained Triangles on Temporal Graphs with Dynamic Index Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11582)
*Chuhan Hu,Ming Zhong,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了 $(k, \delta)$-truss 模型以捕获时序图中的时空紧密子图，并设计了高效的无损压缩索引结构与动态维护算法，大幅提升了查询效率与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态图的 $k$-truss 模型在处理时序图时缺乏对时间关联性的约束，无法捕获具有时间紧密性的子图结构。因此，需要一种既能保持静态凝聚力又能体现时间紧密性的度量模型。

Method: 1. 提出了基于时间窗限制的 $(k, \delta)$-truss 定义；2. 设计了基于“双重包含关系”的无损压缩索引结构（映射或树结构）；3. 开发了基于 truss 分解与维护的启发式索引构建算法；4. 实现了支持动态图更新的增量维护技术。

Result: 在索引构建与查询性能上，所提算法表现优异：查询速度提升了 2~4 个数量级，索引空间压缩比最高达到 $10^{-4}$，且支持高效的动态维护。

Conclusion: 提出的 $(k, \delta)$-truss 概念及其配套的索引结构在时序图分析中表现出卓越的查询性能、极高的压缩比，且具备高效的动态更新能力，验证了该模型在现实大规模时序图处理中的优越性与实用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in temporal graph research have redefined traditional static graph concepts such as triangles, motifs, and $k$-cores. Inspired by this, we introduce a novel $(k,δ)$-truss for temporal graphs, requiring triangles to exist within sufficiently short time windows. The $(k,δ)$-truss ensures both static and temporal cohesion, while the original $k$-truss is a special case when $δ= \infty$. To address $(k,δ)$-truss queries, we propose index-free and index-based approaches. Utilizing the dual containment relation of $(k,δ)$-trusses, our indexes losslessly compress all $(k,δ)$-trusses into map or tree structures, significantly reducing space while enabling optimal-time retrieval. To scale to large temporal graphs, we develop two index construction algorithms based on truss decomposition and truss maintenance, respectively, which substantially reduce redundant computations. Moreover, we present techniques for the dynamic maintenance of the proposed indexes. The experimental results demonstrate that index-based approaches process queries in interactive time and outperform the index-free approach by 2$\sim$4 orders of magnitude, while the indexes achieve compression ratios of up to $10^{-4}$ and can be updated efficiently without rebuilding from scratch.

</details>


### [309] [Efficient Graph Indexing for Interval-Aware Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11789)
*Siyuan Liang,Ziqi Yin,Qi Zhang,Ronghua Li,Guoren Wang,Kaiwen Xue,Daiyin Wang,Xubin Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 为解决现有区间感知ANN检索方法对不同语义需构建多个独立索引的痛点，论文提出了URNG统一图框架及UG索引，实现了单一索引支持多种区间查询语义，显著提升了效率与存储经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多针对特定查询语义（如区间过滤ANN），导致需要维护多个专用索引，产生巨大的存储和计算开销，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出了统一区间感知相对邻域图（URNG）框架，该框架结合了单调可搜索性和查询诱导子图的结构继承性；设计了UG图索引，采用了统一的区间感知剪枝和迭代修复策略，并配套了相应的查询算法。

Result: 在5个数据集上的实验表明，UG在各种区间感知工作负载中均实现了优异的精度-效率权衡，并保持了高效的索引构建性能和内存使用效率。

Conclusion: URNG及其实现UG填补了统一支持多种区间感知ANN查询语义的空白，在保持高效准确率的同时降低了索引构建成本和内存开销。

Abstract: Interval-aware Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search arises in applications where each object is associated with a numeric value or interval, and queries must satisfy both vector-similarity and interval constraints. Existing methods are typically tailored to a single query semantics, such as interval-filtered ANN search, and therefore require multiple specialized indexes to support diverse workloads, leading to substantial indexing and memory overhead. To address this limitation, we propose the Unified Interval-aware Relative Neighborhood Graph (URNG), a unified graph framework for interval-aware ANN search. URNG preserves the monotonic searchability of relative-neighborhood-graph based ANN indexes while additionally ensuring structural heredity over query-induced subgraphs, enabling a single index to support multiple interval-aware query semantics. Building on this framework, we develop UG, a practical graph index that efficiently approximates URNG through unified interval-aware pruning and iterative repair, together with a query algorithm for interval-aware ANN search. Extensive experiments on 5 datasets show that UG consistently achieves a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off across diverse interval-aware workloads while maintaining competitive index construction cost and memory usage.

</details>


### [310] [Neuro-Relational Programs: Unifying Queries and Neural Computation over Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11946)
*Arie Soeteman,Balder ten Cate,Maurice Funk,Benny Kimelfeld,Carsten Lutz,Moritz Schönherr*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出神经关系程序（NRPs），这是一种将逻辑查询与神经计算结合的声明式语言，能以统一框架实现对关系数据库的推理与学习，并从理论上论证了其表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服现有技术在处理关系数据库时将数据转化为图（如 GNN）或碎片化处理的局限，旨在建立一个能直接对带有数值向量嵌入的数据库进行逻辑推理和学习的统一形式化模型。

Method: 提出一种新的声明式查询语言“神经关系程序”（Neuro-Relational Programs, NRPs），它扩展了 Datalog 规则并集成了能够结合、聚合和转换嵌入的运算，从而实现关系推理与神经计算的交织。

Result: NRPs 能够覆盖既有的多种神经架构（如 GNN、Deep Homomorphism Networks），其实例可映射为特定类型的查询算子（无元数、单子、前沿限制）。理论上，带 ReLU-FFN 的非限制 NRP 等价于带有计数功能的扩展一阶逻辑（FOCQ），并展示了与有序数据库上 uniform TC^0 的精密联系。

Conclusion: NRPs 提供了一个通用的声明式框架，将关系推理与可学习的神经计算有机结合，既可被视为带有可训练组件的查询计划，也可被视为具备关系结构的神经架构，具有明确的表达能力界限。

Abstract: The conventional approach to deep learning over relational databases applies neural models, such as Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), to a graph representation of the database. Recent approaches instead operate on databases directly, associating tuples with embeddings and extending query mechanisms to jointly process embeddings and relational content. Inspired by these developments, we introduce Neuro-Relational Programs (NRPs), a declarative query language for relational databases whose facts carry numeric vector embeddings. NRPs extend Datalog-style rules with operations that combine, aggregate, and transform embeddings, thereby interleaving relational reasoning and learnable neural components within a single formalism. This yields a general approach to neural computation over relational data: an NRP can be read both as a query plan with trainable components and as a neural architecture with relational structure built in.
  Natural syntactic fragments of NRPs recover existing architectures and query formalisms. Zero-ary NRPs correspond to non-adaptive query algorithms; monadic NRPs generalize GNN-style message passing and precisely capture Deep Homomorphism Networks, a connection that we extend to frontier-guarded NRPs over databases with row-ids. We characterize the expressive power of unrestricted NRPs with ReLU-FFN transformations by FOCQ, an extension of first-order logic with counting interpreted over real-weighted structures, yielding a precise connection with uniform TC$^0$ over ordered databases. Together, these results establish NRPs as a broad declarative framework for querying and neural computation over relational data.

</details>


### [311] [TAHOE: Text-to-SQL with Automated Hint Optimization from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12387)
*Zhiyi Chen,Jie Song,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Tahoe 是一个用于 Text-to-SQL 的提示优化框架，通过建立“提示库（Hint Bank）”将错误转化为可重用的语法和语义提示，在无需微调模型的情况下，显著提升了 SQL 生成的成功率和开发效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-SQL 在从原型向工业级生产环境迁移时面临挑战，包括严苛的 SQL 方言要求、复杂的数据库模式、用户偏好变化，以及监督微调或推理扩展成本过高的问题。

Method: 提出了一种将提示优化视为动态数据管理问题的系统 Tahoe。核心组件包括：1. 错误驱动的提示学习流水线；2. 将调试信息转化为可重用的语法提示（Syntax Hints）和语义提示（Semantic Hints）的 Hint Bank；3. 处理冲突意图的策略层（Strategy Layer）；4. 推理阶段的“逻辑规划 -> SQL 生成”流程。

Result: 在 Spider 2.0-Snow 数据集上（GPT-5.5），Tahoe 将 pass rate 从 61.95% 提升至 79.42%，语法错误率降至 0%，并将编译器反馈轮数从 2.79 轮大幅降低至 0.12 轮。此外，Hint Bank 具有良好的模型跨度迁移能力，在更弱的模型（如 Doubao-2.0-lite）上同样实现显著性能提升。

Conclusion: Tahoe 通过一种无需更新模型参数的“提示词优化”框架，有效解决了 Text-to-SQL 从原型到生产环境的部署难题，证明了结构化的 Hint Bank 在提升性能和降低反馈成本方面的巨大价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have democratized database access through Text-to-SQL, but moving from prototypes to production remains difficult. Real deployments must handle strict SQL dialects, massive schemas, and evolving user preferences, while supervised fine-tuning is costly and rigid and agentic test-time scaling is expensive. We present Tahoe, a system that treats prompt optimization as a dynamic data management problem. Tahoe uses an error-driven hint learning pipeline across Development and Deployment to consolidate debugging traces into a structured Hint Bank. Compiler feedback is distilled into reusable Syntax Hints for dialect-specific rules, while execution and user feedback are converted into Semantic Hints for schema- and user-specific logic. Tahoe further introduces a Strategy Layer that models conflicting user intents as competing strategies under shared natural-language triggers, with recency signals and post-learning attribution statistics that summarize empirical success, harm, inertness, and support. At inference time, Tahoe retrieves relevant hints and guides the LLM through Logic Planning followed by SQL Synthesis. We implement and evaluate the development-phase workflow, leaving deployment-time human-feedback updates for future work. On Spider 2.0-Snow, Tahoe substantially improves Text-to-SQL without updating model parameters. On 113 supervised Spider 2.0-Snow-0212 examples using GPT-5.5, Tahoe raises pass rate from 61.95 percent to 79.42 percent and pass-at-4 from 72.57 percent to 87.61 percent, achieves 100 percent Snowflake syntax pass rate, and reduces average compiler-feedback critic rounds from 2.79 to 0.12 per sampled candidate. The same Hint Bank also transfers to weaker backbones, including a 19.7 percentage-point pass-rate gain on Doubao-2.0-lite.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [312] [The Brain That Goes Quiet: Serving a Large Model's Knowledge at 131 Tokens per Second on an 8 GB Laptop by Removing the Large Model from the Runtime Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12154)
*Myeong Jun Jo*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文通过将大型模型的推理负载转化为离线预处理，利用轻量化模型与结构化知识库，在消费级硬件上实现了毫秒级的响应速度与大模型级别的输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管此前已实现将大型混合专家（MoE）模型部署在8GB显存的消费级笔记本上，但由于每个查询都需要完整模型参与计算，导致推理延迟过高（约4秒），无法满足实际应用需求。

Method: 提出一种“离线知识库构建+在线轻量级检索”架构：在离线阶段，利用大型MoE模型处理文档并生成结构化知识库；在推理阶段，仅运行轻量级路由器、确定性渲染器及1B参数级别的小模型。同时引入BM25路由、置信度门控（confidence gate）和验证门控（verification gate）来优化检索准确率与生成质量。

Result: 延迟从约4465毫秒降低至518毫秒，吞吐量由15.7提升至131 tokens/秒；小模型生成速度达到226-237 tokens/秒，首字延迟（TTFT）仅为29-62毫秒；在特定数据集上，结合BM25与置信度门控实现了高达98.0%的Top-1检索准确率。

Conclusion: 将大型模型的计算负载从推理时转移到离线预处理阶段，构建结构化知识库，可以显著提升消费级硬件上的端到端响应时间与吞吐量，同时保持高水平的回答准确性。

Abstract: In earlier work I showed that a 35B-class Mixture-of-Experts model can be loaded and executed on a consumer laptop with 8 GB of GPU memory. That result solved a placement problem and immediately exposed a different one: even correctly placed, the large model needed roughly four seconds to answer, because it was still being invoked at every query. This paper documents what happened when I stopped invoking it. During an offline phase, the large model reads source documents and writes verified answer entries into a structured knowledge store; at runtime, only a lightweight router, a deterministic renderer, and a 1B-class model are active. On the same 8 GB laptop, end-to-end response time fell from approximately 4,465 ms to 518 ms, effective end-to-end throughput rose from 15.7 to 131 tokens per second, and the small model's streaming decode rate held at 226-237 tokens per second with a time-to-first-token of 29-62 ms. The bottleneck is structural: three different large models (Qwen, Gemma, and GLM class) all showed the same multi-second runtime cost, and all three produced usable knowledge stores offline. On a 563-entry store built from seventeen real documents, keyword routing collapsed to 1.5% top-1 accuracy while BM25-based routing reached 92.8% (99.4% top-3), and a confidence gate raised effective top-1 to 98.0% by escalating 12.3% of queries. Exact-match fidelity of the small model ranged from 9/9 to 0/9 across envelope formats carrying identical content. A 16-case verification gate blocked all ten corrupted entries while admitting all six supported ones.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [313] [Fixed-Parameter Tractability of Private Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11283)
*Badih Ghazi,Cristóbal Guzmán,Pritish Kamath,Alexander Knop,Ravi Kumar,Pasin Manurangsi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过树分解的动态规划框架，证明了差分隐私下的合成数据生成问题关于查询图树宽是固定参数可解（FPT）的，并提供了达到最优误差率的通用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在满足差分隐私约束下，如何高效、高精度地生成合成数据的问题，并探索查询图结构特性（树宽）对计算复杂度的影响。

Method: 1. 基于线性规划（LP）及其对偶分离问题的FPT算法；2. 基于子采样私有乘法权重更新（Private Multiplicative Weights）方法，实现吉布斯分布采样；两者均统一在树分解上的动态规划框架下。

Result: 证明了该问题关于查询图树宽是固定参数可解的，并且所提出的算法在所有参数区间内均能达到最优误差率。

Conclusion: 本文在差分隐私合成数据生成任务中建立了参数化算法的理论框架，证明了该问题在查询图树宽这一参数下是固定参数可解的（FPT），且达到了最优误差界。

Abstract: We study the problem of generating synthetic data under differential privacy. We establish fixed-parameter tractability (FPT) for this problem where the parameter is the treewidth of the query family's incidence graph. Our algorithms attain optimal error rates across all regimes and are realized by two different approaches: the first is based on linear programming (LP) and the FPT of the separation problem for the LP dual; the second is based on a subsampled private multiplicative weights method, where we obtain FPT for sampling from Gibbs distributions. Both approaches are unified by a dynamic programming framework over a tree decomposition.

</details>


### [314] [The Power of Test-Time Training for Approximate Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11437)
*Noah Golowich,Ankur Moitra,Dhruv Rohatgi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文将测试时训练（TTT）形式化为从特定分布类中采样问题，证明了在通用分布类下随机游走采样算法的查询复杂度最优（二次下界），并提出了在限制分布类假设下突破该下界的条件，为TTT提供了初步理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理任务中采样算法的局限性催生了测试时训练（TTT）框架，但TTT缺乏坚实的理论基础。本文旨在将TTT的形式化问题与计算理论中经典的“采样归约到计数”问题建立联系，从理论层面探索采样算法的性能边界。

Method: 将TTT形式化为从已知分布类中采样的数学问题；利用近似密度估计Oracle进行复杂度分析；通过理论推导证明查询复杂度的下界，并探讨分布类大小对复杂度的影响。

Result: 1. 证明了对于足够大的分布类，从近似密度Oracle中采样存在二次查询复杂度下界，证明了Jerrum & Sinclair等提出的随机游走方法已达到最优。2. 证明了若限制分布类的大小，可以规避上述复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 证明了在通用分布类中采样存在二次查询复杂度下界，证实了随机游走算法的最优性；同时指出当分布类规模受限时可突破该下界，为测试时训练（TTT）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Efficiently sampling from a complex probability distribution is a fundamental problem which has become increasingly pertinent in recent years with the rise of generative AI, as sophisticated sampling procedures from LLMs have been proposed to solve challenging reasoning problems. The efficacy of such sampling algorithms is limited, however, by the relationship between the LLM and the particular sampling task at hand, which has motivated the framework of test-time training (TTT). TTT works by updating a model's weights in response to partial generations and reward feedback received at inference time, thus adapting to the particular problem. In this work, we propose a formalization for TTT as the problem of producing a sample from a given probability measure $μ^\star$ belonging to a known class ${F}$ of distributions, given an oracle $\hat μ$ which yields approximate density estimates for $μ^\star$. This is closely related to the problem of reducing sampling to approximate counting studied in seminal works of Jerrum, Valiant & Vazirani (1986) and Jerrum & Sinclair (1989): namely, when ${F}$ is the class of all distributions, it coincides exactly with the aforementioned counting-to-sampling reduction.
  In this paper, we first show a quadratic lower bound on the query complexity of sampling from $μ^\star$ given query access to $\hat μ$ (for sufficiently large classes ${F}$), thus showing that the random walk approach proposed by Jerrum & Sinclair (1989) and refined by Hayes & Sinclair (2010), is optimal. This answers an open question posed by Hayes & Sinclair. We then show that this lower bound can be circumvented if the size of ${F}$ is bounded appropriately. As we discuss, this latter result can be viewed as an abstraction of TTT, and thus represents a starting point for the development of a principled theoretical framework for TTT.

</details>


### [315] [A Unified Lower Bound on the Noisy Query Complexity of Boolean Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11448)
*Yuzhou Gu,Xin Li,Yinzhan Xu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于分析布尔函数噪声查询复杂性的通用下界框架，基于超立方体子图度数统计，统一了既有结果并解决了关于总影响度与其复杂性关系的一个公开猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在噪声查询模型（查询结果以概率 $p$ 翻转）中，现有的下界分析往往受限于随机查询复杂性的简单推演。研究旨在寻求一种更具一般性的框架，以推导更精确的噪声查询复杂性下界。

Method: 引入一种基于布尔超立方体特定子图度数统计的新型数学分析方法，用以刻画噪声环境下的查询复杂性下界，并通过该方法分析函数的总影响度（Total Influence）等特征。

Result: 1. 提出了一个超越随机查询复杂性分析的通用下界框架；2. 证明了 $\mathsf{N}_p(f) = \Omega(\mathsf{I}(f) \log \mathsf{I}(f))$；3. 简化了已有结论的证明，并获得了若干新函数的紧致下界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种基于布尔超立方体子图度数统计的噪声查询复杂性下界通用框架，不仅统一了现有的大多数下界结果，还证明了 Gu 等人提出的 $\mathsf{N}_p(f) = \Omega(\mathsf{I}(f) \log \mathsf{I}(f))$ 猜想，并为多种新函数提供了紧致的复杂度下界。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of Boolean functions $f: \{0, 1\}^n \rightarrow \{0, 1\}$ in the noisy query model introduced by Feige, Raghavan, Peleg and Upfal [SICOMP 1994]. In this model, an algorithm can adaptively query the bits of an input vector, but each query result is independently flipped with constant probability $p \in (0, 1/2)$; repeated queries are allowed. The noisy query complexity $\mathsf{N}_p(f)$ of a function $f$ is defined as the minimum expected number of queries needed to compute $f(x)$ with error probability at most $1/3$, for the worst case input $x$.
  We prove a general lower bound on $\mathsf{N}_p(f)$ based on degree statistics of certain subgraphs of the Boolean hypercube. This is the first general lower bound beyond those implied by the simple observation that $\mathsf{N}_p(f)$ is lower bounded by the randomized query complexity. We show that this recovers (up to a constant factor) most previously known lower bounds on the noisy query complexity of Boolean functions, providing a unified framework for understanding these results and simplifying the proofs in several cases. Furthermore, this resolves in the affirmative an open problem of Gu, Li and Xu [COLT 2025] that $\mathsf{N}_p(f) = Ω(\mathsf{I}(f) \log \mathsf{I}(f))$, where $\mathsf{I}(f)$ denotes the total influence of $f$. We also apply our general lower bound to obtain tight bounds on the noisy query complexity for several new functions.

</details>


### [316] [Density estimation for Hellinger via minimum-distance estimators: mixtures of Gaussians, log-concave, and more](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11469)
*Spencer Compton,Jerry Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文将最小距离估计方法从总变分距离扩展至Hellinger距离，实现了对复杂密度混合模型的高效、近最优样本复杂度的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的最小距离估计方法（如Yatracos类）主要适用于总变分距离（total variation distance），缺乏针对Hellinger距离的通用且高效的估计框架。

Method: 将最小距离估计框架扩展至Hellinger距离，通过建立该距离与逆数据处理不等式（reverse data processing inequalities）的联系；并基于Acharya等人（2017）的方法设计了近线性时间的算法。

Result: 实现了Hellinger距离下的密度估计；针对包括单变量对数凹密度混合和高斯混合在内的密度类，提供了首个近线性时间的算法，且样本复杂度接近最优。

Conclusion: 本文成功将最小距离估计方法扩展至Hellinger距离，并利用逆数据处理不等式提供了理论支持。该方法不仅在理论上达到了近似最优的样本复杂度，且通过修改现有算法，实现了对单变量对数凹密度混合模型和高斯混合模型的高效（近线性时间）密度估计。

Abstract: We study the task of density estimation, where we hope to accurately estimate a probability density from $n$ samples. A textbook method for density estimation in total variation distance is the minimum-distance estimator approach, where we conclude both the algorithm and the analysis merely from bounding the VC dimension of a particular concept class (the so-called Yatracos class).
  While this technique has originally yielded sharp guarantees primarily for total variation distance, in this work we extend the minimum-distance estimator approach for learning within Hellinger distance. Our main observation is that we may produce an analogous recipe for Hellinger (where we only require bounding the VC dimension of a related concept class) by drawing connections to recent results yielding reverse data processing inequalities.
  This recipe is flexible enough to accommodate fast algorithms originally designed for total variation distance; by modifying the approach of Acharya et al. (2017) we conclude the first near-linear time algorithm for learning classes including univariate mixtures of log-concave densities and mixtures of Gaussians (with arbitrary variances), with near-optimal sample complexity.

</details>


### [317] [Beyond Frequency Marching: Orbit Recovery in Dihedral and Projected Multireference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11701)
*Tait Weicht,Alexander S. Wein*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文为二面体MRA和投影MRA问题提出了首个多项式时间算法，利用三阶力矩张量通过递归分解方法恢复未知信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参考对齐（MRA）在低温电子显微镜（cryo-EM）等领域具有重要应用价值，但二面体MRA与投影MRA在既有研究中尚缺乏通用的多项式时间求解算法。为了解决这些变体问题的信号恢复难题，研究者旨在探索更有效的算法框架。

Method: 采用力矩法（Method of Moments），基于三阶力矩张量进行信号恢复。提出了一种递归算法，通过将长度为2的幂次的信号问题逐步划分为更小规模的子问题来求解。

Result: 成功为二面体MRA和投影MRA设计了算法；该算法在通用信号下对模型是有效的（基于符号多项式矩阵秩的猜想），且在处理这类轨道恢复问题上取得了理论突破。

Conclusion: 该研究首次为二面体多参考对齐（Dihedral MRA）和投影多参考对齐（Projected MRA）问题提供了多项式时间求解算法，虽然其正确性依赖于一个关于符号矩阵秩的猜想，但在特定规模下可通过计算机验证。

Abstract: Multireference alignment (MRA) is the task of recovering a hidden "signal" vector, given many noisy copies that have been cyclically shifted by unknown offsets. This task belongs to the class of orbit recovery problems, in which the observed samples are affected by some group action. These problems have a variety of practical motivations, including the reconstruction of 3-dimensional molecular structure from cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) images. We consider two variants of MRA: dihedral MRA, where the cyclic group is replaced by the dihedral group, allowing for reversals of the vector in addition to shifts; and projected MRA, where the observations are passed through a projection operator akin to the tomographic projection present in cryo-EM. We apply the method of moments and aim to recover the signal from the third moment tensor of the samples. This inverse problem is well understood for basic MRA, but for the variants we consider there is no polynomial-time algorithm known to succeed for generic signals. We give the first such algorithm for both of these variants. Our method requires the signal length to be a power of two, and recursively subdivides the problem into smaller problems of half the size. The algorithm's success for generic signals is proven, conditional on a conjecture about the rank of a certain symbolic matrix of polynomials. For any given problem size, this conjecture can be verified on a computer.

</details>


### [318] [A Fast Gaussian Mechanism under Continual Observation, with Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11760)
*Rasmus Pagh,Sia Sejer*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在差分隐私连续数据发布中，以常量时间采样高斯噪声的新技术。通过使用布朗桥，该方法在保持二进制树机制性能的同时，移除了对时间步长$T$的对数依赖，并显著改进了正交范围查询和连接大小估计的隐私与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在差分隐私连续发布（Continual Release）问题中，现有的二进制树机制虽能保证隐私和精度，但在时间复杂度上存在限制。现有方法在处理大规模数据更新时，对于单个时间步的项发布需要$O(\log T)$时间，作者旨在通过私有草图（Private Sketching）技术，将该采样过程优化至常量时间，同时保持原有的分布特性。

Method: 利用布朗桥（Brownian bridges）构建了一种新型数据结构，实现在常量时间内对具有特定相关性的高斯噪声进行采样，从而精确重现二进制树机制的输出分布。并将其与差分隐私CountSketch框架集成应用于具体数据管理任务。

Result: 1. 实现了在常量时间内对符合隐私要求的噪声向量维度进行采样，取代了传统的$O(\log T)$采样时间。
2. 在正交范围查询（Orthogonal Range Counting）中实现了更优的隐私/精度/空间权衡。
3. 在连接大小估计（Join Size Estimation）中获得了改进的高概率误差边界。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种在差分隐私连续发布框架下，实现噪声向量采样的常量时间算法。该方法不仅优化了传统二进制树机制（Binary Tree Mechanism）的计算性能，还通过与CountSketch结合，在动态数据结构（正交范围查询）和连接大小估计（Join Size Estimation）两个应用中实现了更好的隐私-精度-空间权衡及更强的概率边界。

Abstract: We consider the problem of privately releasing a $k$-dimensional vector under updates: Starting with a zero vector, at times $t_1, t_2,\dots$ the vector is updated by adding $x^{(1)}, x^{(2)},\dots$, respectively. For positive integers $T$, $k$ we model the updates as a data set $\{(t_i, x^{(i)})\}_i$, where $t_i \in [T]$ and $x^{(i)} \in B_k$ (the $k$-dimensional unit ball). Two such data sets are said to be neighboring if their symmetric difference has size at most $1$. The continual release consists of the sum $A^{(t)} = \sum_{i \; : \; t_i \leq t} x^{(i)}$ for each time step $t=1,\dots,T$. Classical continual release techniques allow us to release an approximation of $A^{(1)},\dots,A^{(T)}$ with additive noise of magnitude $\text{polylog}(T)$, computed in time $O(kT)$, even in the on-line, adaptive case where data is continually revealed for the current time step.
  Motivated by private sketching techniques, we consider the setting where only a \emph{subset} of entries in $A^{(t)}$ need to be released at time step $t$. Our new result is that it is possible to sample any desired entry in a given noise vector in \emph{constant time} while reproducing exactly the distribution of the binary tree mechanism with Gaussian noise. The improvement on the known time bound of $O(\log T)$ comes from a new data structure that allows us to sample a new noise value with the correct correlations in constant time using Brownian bridges. We present two data management applications, of independent interest, that use our technique in conjunction with differentially private CountSketches: 1) A dynamic data structure for orthogonal range counting queries with a better privacy/accuracy/space trade-off than previous data structures, and 2) Join size estimation, where in addition we show improved high-probability bounds.

</details>


### [319] [Near-Optimal Distributed 2-Ruling Sets on Graphs with Low Arboricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11974)
*Malte Baumecker,Rustam Latypov,Yannic Maus,Jara Uitto*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在LOCAL和MPC模型中为寻找2-ruling集提供了几乎最优的分布式算法，在特定树状度图上实现了O(log log n)的LOCAL运行时间和O(log log log n)的MPC运行时间，实现了对现有算法的指数级改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式图中，寻找满足各节点距离支配集（ruling set）距离限制为2（即β=2）的集合是一个基础问题。由于现有算法在不同树状度图上的延迟较高，且未能达到理论最优平衡，作者旨在缩小算法运行时间与理论下界之间的差距。

Method: 1. 在LOCAL模型中，基于随机化方法设计分布式算法；2. 针对一般图和特定树状度图（arboricity）进行分层处理和复杂度优化；3. 将该方法扩展至具备低存储空间限制的Massively Parallel Computation (MPC)模型。

Result: 1. 在LOCAL模型中，对于有界树状度图，计算2-ruling集的时间复杂度达到O(log log n)，接近Ω(log log n / log log log n)的下界；2. 对一般树状度α的图，延迟优化至O~(log^5/8 α + log^5/3 log n)；3. 在低空间的MPC模型中，实现了O(log log log n)的运行时间。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种用于计算2-ruling集的几乎最优的分布式算法，在有界树状度（bounded arboricity）图上实现了O(log log n)轮，显著超越了现有技术，并接近了下界，同时在MPC模型中也取得了指数级的效率提升。

Abstract: Given a graph $G=(V,E)$, a $β$-ruling set is a subset of nodes $S\subseteq V$ that is independent, and each node in $V$ is at distance at most $β$ from some node in $S$. In this paper, we present almost optimal distributed algorithms for finding $2$-ruling sets in the classical \LOCAL model. Our main contribution is a randomized algorithm that w.h.p.\ computes a $2$-ruling set on any $n$-node graph with bounded arboricity in $O(\log \log n)$ rounds. In fact, the algorithm works up to arboricity $O(\log\log n)$, improves exponentially over the prior state of the art that can be achieved by combining [Barenboim, Elkin, Pettie, Schneider; JACM'16], [Ghaffari; SODA'16], and [Bisht, Kothapalli and Pemmaraju; PODC'14], and nearly matches the lower bound of $Ω(\log \log n / \log \log \log n)$ [Balliu, Brandt, Kuhn, Olivetti; FOCS'20]. The domination parameter $β=2$ is optimal for algorithms with runtime $\log^{o(1)}n$: on graphs with arboricity $2$, there is a lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{\log n})$ rounds for MIS (i.e., $β= 1$) [Khoury, Schild; FOCS'25].
  Additionally, we obtain improved algorithms for larger arboricity. For general graphs with arboricity $α$, we present a randomized algorithm that computes a $2$-ruling set in $\widetilde{O}(\log^{5/8} α+\log^{5/3} \log n)$ rounds. This improves exponentially over the state of the art for a large range of non-constant arboricity.
  Our techniques extend beyond distributed computing. We present an $O(\log \log \log n)$-round algorithm in the low-space Massively Parallel Computation (\mpc) model that w.h.p.\ computes a $2$-ruling set on any graph with arboricity up to $2^{poly (\log \log n)}$, improving exponentially over the state of the art from [Kothapalli, Pai, Pemmaraju; FSTTCS'20] combined with [Fischer, Giliberti, Grunau; SPAA'23].

</details>


### [320] [Nearly Instance Optimal Sparse Matrix Approximation from Matrix-Vector Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12179)
*Christoper Musco,Indu Ramesh*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文统一了稀疏矩阵近似的查询复杂度理论，证明了查询复杂性由稀疏模式的“退化度（degeneracy）”决定，实现了近几乎最优且多项式时间可解的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于矩阵-向量乘积（matvec）查询复杂度的研究依赖于多种不统一的量度（如总非零元数、行列最大稀疏度、冲突图色数），且这些指标在不同场景下各有优劣，缺乏一个综合性的理论架构。

Method: 引入了图论中的“退化度（degeneracy）”概念来统一描述稀疏模式 $S$，并基于此构造了一种新的查询算法，通过迭代删除行列达到近似最优解。

Result: 证明了对于任意稀疏模式 $S$，学习矩阵 $A$ 的稀疏近似所需的 matvec 查询次数为 $\tilde{O}(\text{degen}(S))$，并给出了相应的下界 $\Omega(\text{degen}(S))$。此外，算法表现出比基于图染色方法更好的多项式时间执行效率。

Conclusion: 本文为稀疏矩阵近似中的矩阵向量乘积查询复杂度问题提供了几乎最优的刻画：查询复杂度界限确切为 $\Theta(\text{degen}(S))$，且该算法在多项式时间内可实现。

Abstract: A large body of work studies the problem of learning an approximation to an implicit matrix $A\in \mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ that is only accessible implicitly via matrix-vector product queries (matvec queries) of the form ${x} \rightarrow {A}{x}$ or ${x} \rightarrow {A}^T{x}$. Of particular interest are methods that learn a near-optimal approximation with a fixed sparsity pattern. For example, we might want to learn a near-optimal diagonal, banded, or arrow-head approximation to an implicit matrix $A$.
  Naturally, the number of matvec queries required to solve this problem depends on the sparsity pattern, which can be encoded as a binary matrix ${S}\in \{0,1\}^{m\times n}$. The query complexity of previous algorithms scales with quantities like the total number of ones in ${S}$, its maximum column/row sparsity, or the chromatic number of a its "conflict graph". These quantities are incomparable: for a given ${S}$, parameterizing by one might yield lower query complexity than another.
  In this work, we unify and tighten these prior results by providing a nearly sharp characterization of the matvec query complexity of sparse matrix approximation. Generalizing a definition from graph algorithms, let the degeneracy, ${degen}({S})$, denote the smallest number $k$ so that, if we iteratively delete all rows and columns of ${S}$ with $\leq k$ ones, we are left with an empty matrix. We show that a near-optimal approximation to $A$ with sparsity pattern $S$ can be learned with $\tilde{O}({degen}({S}))$ matrix-vector product queries, and $Ω({degen}({S}))$ queries are necessary, for any sparsity pattern ${S}$. Moreover, unlike prior work based on graph coloring, all of our methods run in polynomial time.

</details>
